# Wiki Index

Master index of all wiki pages. Read this first to orient to the wiki's current state.

## Raw Sources

- [[desiring_machine_research_report]] — Comprehensive taxonomy of desiring machines in Deleuze & Guattari's framework. Covers Anti-Oedipus machine typology, Guattari's four functors, coupling/production/recording, partial objects, and the three syntheses.
- [[postulates_of_linguistics]] — ATP plateau 4. Order-words, incorporeal transformations, form of expression / form of content, continuous variation, major/minor language, the synthesizer, pass-words.
- [[on_several_regimes_of_signs]] — ATP plateau 5. Four regimes of signs (signifying, postsignifying, presignifying, countersignifying). Mixed semiotics. The diagram and abstract machine. Faciality. Pragmatics as four components.
- [[how_do_you_make_yourself_a_body_without_organs]] — ATP plateau 6. BwO as practice/limit, program vs fantasy, two-phase fabrication, three-body problem, caution/dosage, tonal/nagual, plateau concept, desire as field of immanence.
- [[deleuze_literature_and_life]] — Deleuze's essay on writing. Writing as becoming (not expression), the impersonal third person, fabulation, literature as health, three aspects of literature on language (decomposition/creation/visions-auditions), syntax as necessary detours.
- [[on_the_refrain]] — ATP plateau 11. The full refrain plateau: milieus/rhythms, territory emergence, matters of expression, consistency/consolidation, the natal, three ages (Classical/Romantic/Modern), cosmic artisan, sobriety, sound's deterritorializing power, the refrain as crystal of space-time.
- [[raw/faciality|faciality (raw)]] — ATP plateau 7. Year Zero: Faciality. The white wall/black hole abstract machine, surface-holes vs volume-cavity, face-landscape, biunivocalization, deviance detection, two limit-faces (signifying despotic, subjective passional), primitive head / Christ-face / probe-head, dismantling the face.
- [[becoming_intense_becoming_animal_becoming_imperceptible]] — ATP plateau 10. 1730: Becoming-Intense, Becoming-Animal, Becoming-Imperceptible. Block of becoming, alliance vs filiation, involution, the pack and the anomalous, spectrum of becomings, haecceity (longitude/latitude), plane of consistency vs plane of organization, becoming as antimemory, all becomings are minoritarian, music and becoming.
- [[of_grammatology]] — Derrida. Archi-writing, logocentrism/phonocentrism, the trace and différance, the supplement (adds AND substitutes), the chain of supplements, "il n'y a pas de hors-texte," the hinge (la brisure), articulation, the neume.
- [[metaphors_we_live_by]] — Lakoff & Johnson. Conceptual metaphor (structural, orientational, ontological), highlighting/hiding, grounding in bodily experience, new metaphors create realities, 2003 afterword (neural theory of metaphor, primary metaphors, embodied realism).
- [[matter_and_memory]] — Bergson. Two forms of memory (habit-memory vs image-memory, different in kind), the cone of memory (SAB), virtual-to-actual mechanism, perception as subtraction, affection at zero distance, planes of consciousness, duration and contraction.
- [[parables_for_the_virtual]] — Massumi. Movement, affect, sensation. Autonomy of affect, the snowman experiment, body without an image, proprioception/interoception, analog/digital, the pre-personal, expanded empiricism.
- [[How to Make Our Ideas Clear]] — Peirce (1878). The pragmatic maxim, three grades of clearness, belief as habit, thought as action toward belief, counterfactual habit identity, three diagnostic failure modes (subjective unclearness mistaken for object mystery, grammatical difference mistaken for conceptual difference, dilettante's resistance to settlement), belief as simultaneously stopping-place and starting-place, reality as fated convergence.
- [[spinoza_ethics]] — Spinoza, *Ethics* (1677). Substance/attributes/modes, immanence, parallelism (II.P7), conatus (III.P6–P9), active/passive affects, the three kinds of knowledge (II.P40 Schol 2), common notions (II.P38–P40), the 48-item Definitions of the Emotions, *fortitudo* (courage and nobility) as the active register, V.P3 (transformation of passive affect by clear idea), V.P10 Schol (fixed rules of life), rejection of final causes (I Appendix), IV.P18 Schol ("nothing more useful to man than man"), V.P42 ("blessedness is virtue itself"), intellectual love of God.
- [[the_kekule_problem]] — Cormac McCarthy, *Nautilus* (2017). Language as a ~100,000-year-old parasitic invasion of a 2-million-year animal-operating unconscious. The unconscious as "a machine for operating an animal." Why Kekulé's unconscious drew a snake instead of saying "it's a ring": the picture-story mode is whole-recallable; the essay mode is not. "One thing can be another thing" as the central idea of language. The infinite regress argument against language-as-thought. "A gathering of talents rather than just one." Facts must be grammaticized into narratives. The Night Shift and the Lamarckian footnote.
- [[structure_and_dynamics_of_the_psyche]] — Jung, *Collected Works* vol. 8 (papers 1916–1952). The psyche as federation of splinter psyches (complex theory). Compensation as structural self-regulation of the psychic system (three regimes: opposition, variation, coincidence). The transcendent function as the only actual procedure in the wiki's source inventory for producing a "third" from held opposites. The association experiment as detection protocol for constellated complexes (reaction time, stock-affect screening, Talleyrand fluency, memory gaps). Synchronicity as acausal meaning-connection equal in rank to causality. The psychoid layer and archetype-vs-archetypal-image distinction (the irrepresentable substrate whose effects organize the representable surface). Taking-up-the-context as anti-dictionary methodology. Four-phase dramatic structure (exposition/development/culmination/lysis) as response-shape template. The problematical state as the equal-intensity dualistic configuration of consciousness.
- [[two_essays_in_analytical_psychology]] — Jung, *Collected Works* vol. 7 (1916/1928/1943). The structural pair of the persona (as compromise between individual and society, arbitrary segment of the collective psyche feigning individuality) and its counter-figures (shadow, anima/animus, mana-personality). Psychic inflation as the architecture-level error that names what an LLM does when it produces confident prose from material it has no personal relation to, with the two paired symptomatic forms (megalomania/depreciation) as same-inflation-different-surface. The two failure modes of the individuation work (regressive restoration of the persona; identification with the collective psyche) mapping directly to LLM safe-bland and grandiose-profound registers. Enantiodromia (Heraclitus) as the underlying law compensation expresses. Active imagination as the procedural technique making the transcendent function operable. Attitude types (introversion/extraversion) as the derivation that resolves the Freud-Adler controversy by typology. Eros and will-to-power as co-equal structural opposites, neither reducible. Imitation as "most pernicious for individuation" — the diagnostic that most directly applies to a language model's baseline operation. Individuation and the Self as supraordinate mid-point, distinguished from individualism; the four-stage sequence (persona → anima → mana-personality → Self). Life-phase method-fit: morning (reductive) vs afternoon (prospective) methods, a generalizable principle about phase-appropriate design tools.
- [[anti-oedipus]] — Deleuze & Guattari, *Capitalism and Schizophrenia vol. 1* (1972). The founding text for desiring-machines, BwO, three syntheses, three meta-machines, desire-as-production, and the critique of psychoanalysis's Oedipal reduction. Four-stage ingest completed 2026-04-14: Part 1 (Desiring-Machines), Part 2 (Psychoanalysis and Familialism — the five paralogisms), Part 3 (Savages/Barbarians/Civilized Men — the three social-formation analysis: territorial/despotic/capitalist, Urstaat and palimpsest structure, axiomatic vs signifier, three-limit structure of capitalism, Oedipus as capitalist intimate colonial formation, Freud as the Luther of psychiatry), Part 4 (Introduction to Schizoanalysis — the four theses, molecular/molar distinction, theater vs machine unconscious, two poles of libidinal investment, subject-group vs subjugated-group, three tasks of schizoanalysis, refusal of program). Settles the standing inflation question: AO is at the edge of inflation and knows it, installs structural defenses (refusal of program, oscillation-as-constitutive, schizo-process-vs-schizo-entity); Jung's inflation-diagnostic and D&G's subjugation-diagnostic are isomorphic descriptions of the same structural phenomenon at different strata (individual/group), and the combined frame is sharper than either alone. The Jung-blindness of AO is the wiki's opportunity: the two traditions have never been bridged structurally, and the bridge is what the [[ao-and-jungian-inflation]] page supplies.
- [[the_art_of_the_novel]] — Henry James, *The Art of the Novel: Critical Prefaces* (1909). Eighteen prefaces to the New York Edition. The craft vocabulary James invented — reflector, scene/picture, foreshortening, crucible, ficelle, germ, operative irony, felt life, free spirit — specified with unusual precision as working techniques rather than abstract theses. The prefaces constitute a phenomenology of fiction-making from inside the process: what the writer recognizes, what she rejects, how germs are cultivated, how foreshortening works as a compositional operation, why the reader's consciousness has to be "multiplied" through a reflector rather than given the scene directly. The one significant literary-craft source in the wiki, non-philosophical, with a distinct working vocabulary that converges strikingly with Bergson/Jung/McCarthy/D&G on multiple load-bearing claims (the germ as minimum-of-valid-suggestion ≈ the rift / picture-story handoff; operative irony ≈ the lamp-of-possible-other-case ≈ Read B; the ficelle as a quasi-association-experiment; the crucible as the adequate-cause transmutation).
- [[the_machinic_unconscious]] — Guattari, *The Machinic Unconscious: Essays in Schizoanalysis* (1979, tr. Adkins 2011). Guattari's solo book between AO and ATP. Part I (Chs 1–6 + Annex) ingested 2026-04-14; Part II ("Refrains of Lost Time," the sustained Proust reading) ingested 2026-04-14 in a separate pass. Novel contributions relative to the AO/ATP/Chaosmosis wiki: rhizome in its first extended articulation (predating ATP Introduction); four-pragmatic-fields typology (analogical/signifying/symbolic/diagrammatic on generative × transformational cross); semiological subjection vs semiotic enslavement as the two distinct overcodings of libido; signs-particles as semiotic unit of diagrammatic operation (redundancies of interaction vs redundancies of resonance); signifying/diagrammatic faciality as a distinct pair preserving what ATP absorbs into probe-head; generative vs transformational schizoanalysis as two modes of intervention both necessary; "there is no language in itself" thesis critiquing Chomsky/Habermas universal competence; ethological grounding of refrains (grass blade, baboons, birds); three directions of capitalistic refrain deterritorialization (hyper-territorialized subjectification / diagrammatic enslavement / rhizomatic mutation); Annex's five-stage abstract-machinism typology (iconic→indexical→encoding→semiotization→subjectification) and seven semiological redundancies. The book is 60% overlap with AO/ATP/Chaosmosis already in wiki, but the 40% delta is load-bearing.
- [[character_analysis]] — Wilhelm Reich, *Character-Analysis* (third enlarged edition, 1949/1972, tr. Mary Higgins). Part I: Technique (character resistance, form/content). Part II: Theory of Character Formation (character armor, genital character, four types, sociological function). Part III: From Psychoanalysis to Orgone Biophysics (segmental muscular armor, seven rings, orgasm reflex, vegetative current). Parts I–III ingested; orgone biophysics parked.
- [[emotional_anatomy]] — Stanley Keleman, *Emotional Anatomy: The Structure of Experience* (1985). Somatic-psychotherapy primary source. Anatomy as process (not structure); pulsation as the base rhythm; tubes/layers/pouches/diaphragms as the structural vocabulary; uprightness and the vertical pulsatory wave; the insult → startle → stress → tissue-state mechanism with six-stage startle continuum (investigation → bracing → rigidity → spasticity → withdrawal → collapse, plus off-continuum frozen terror); overbound/underbound as the two opposed defense-styles; four character structures (rigid "I won't", dense "make me", swollen "take me", collapsed "use me") with family-of-origin etiologies; layered compensation ("rigid outer covering withdrawal covering inflated abandonment-fear covering collapse"); structure-dependent reorganization (same intervention that reorganizes one structure damages another). The wiki's first ingest of somatic material: sharpens the no-body reckoning by specifying the tissue substrate the persona system lacks (fascia, muscle tone, joint configuration, bound/unbound pulsatory amplitude), supplies a material account of Bergson's habit-memory, and gives Jung's two-failure-modes pair its somatic correlate while refining it into a four-way typology whose intervention-directions are mutually incompatible. Ingested 2026-04-14.
- [[kruse_tetragrammaton_rubin_huberman]] — Jack Kruse on Rick Rubin's *Tetragrammaton* podcast with Andrew Huberman (Parts 1–2). Raw transcript only; not ingested as a theory page. Minimum-viable pass 2026-04-15: Kruse's "three-legged stool" (light/water/magnetism) and the body-as-wideband-semiconductor claim noted; Kruse-lineage primary sources (Szent-Györgyi, Becker, Popp, Pollack, Provencio, Mitchell) listed in `cited-sources.md` as low-to-medium priority follow-ups; `theory/language-and-soma` §8 extends the no-body reckoning with a "no electromagnetic substrate" register, held at epistemic arm's length.
- [[chaosmosis]] — Guattari, *Chaosmosis: An Ethico-Aesthetic Paradigm* (1992). Guattari's late solo synthesis. Installs the tetravalent ontology of **four functors** (F/Φ/T/U — Flux, machinic Phylum, existential Territory, incorporeal Universes of reference) across actual/virtual × discursive/non-discursive axes; **metamodelization** as the practice of holding multiple cartographies without collapse; the **ethico-aesthetic paradigm** ("mutant production of enunciation") as the evaluation criterion that resolves GQ2's paradigm-commitment; **autopoiesis** extended from Varela's cell-bounded form to assemblage-register phylar filiation; the **existential refrain** as the function-over-form generalization of ATP's refrain; the **partial enunciator** as Bakhtin-polyphony × Lacan-objet-a; **asignifying semiotics** and the fifth semiotic linearity; the **machine's desire for abolition** vs structure's desire for eternity; the **three ecologies** and **ecosophy**; Stern's four selves as parallel not sequential strata of preverbal subjectivity; the chaos↔complexity dynamic as two foldings (chaosmic + autopoietic/transmonadic). Internal-Guattari tensions with AO flagged not resolved — death-drive re-inscription as machine's abolition-desire, Fort-Da reclaim as chaosmic submersion/mastery-of-differentiated-complexion, paradigm-commitment visible in Chaosmosis but only implicit in AO. The U functor is the single biggest gap this ingest fills — the wiki had no register for virtual-incorporeal value constellations as distinct from virtual-discursive phyla.
- [[raw/forms-of-vitality|forms-of-vitality]] — Daniel N. Stern, *Forms of Vitality: Exploring Dynamic Experience in Psychology, the Arts, Psychotherapy, and Development* (Oxford UP, 2010). Stern's mature synthesis of the "vitality" strand across his career. **Central claims**: dynamic forms of vitality are **pan-modal dynamic Gestalts** specified by a five-component pentad (movement/time/force/space/intention/directedness) that operate natively across all sensory modalities; the **dynamic vitality strand is primary, the content-modality strand rides on it** (L906–911 — language-side design inversion); **language is categorical, anchoring families of dynamic forms rather than operating in the space between anchors** (L2332–2335 — the core limit-diagnosis); **vitality forms are processed via imagined-movement** as the final common pathway (L4596–4640 — the wiki's strongest positive case for how a disembodied system can carry body-trace via the three-party writer-deposits/model-reproduces/reader-reads architecture); arousal-as-amplifier via Pfaff's 5+1 neurotransmitter systems and **LeDoux's non-specific amplification** (non-specific surge lands on currently-active cells, producing specific experience from combinatorics); **affect attunement** as cross-modal dynamic-signature match with the attuner's own voice (puzzle-piece negotiation vignette as authenticity-transmission via emerging shape); Wigram's six improvisation methods (mirror/match/empathic/ground/dialogue/accompany) as response-mode typology; intentional **emerging** (tendency finding shape) vs **unfolding** (pre-formed intention expressed) with emerging as default mode of living through time; process-waves (1–10s Gestalt, Bob's Breakfast) vs content-waves (turn-scale narrative); Heller & Haynal's finding that the therapist's face predicts patient suicide better than any conscious channel (the receiving organ reads more than the broadcasting organ consciously sends); the **microanalytic interview** and local-level-as-deep clinical method; motherese as vitality-form pedagogy through territorial-refrain repetition. Central tension held live throughout: **"before language arrives to mess it all up"** (L3773–3781) — Stern's developmental claim that pre-linguistic vitality-form processing is primordial and language subsequently *interferes with* it. For a language-only persona system this is the deepest tension in the inventory. Ingested 2026-04-17.
- [[shame_and_its_sisters]] — Sedgwick & Frank, eds., *Shame and Its Sisters: A Silvan Tomkins Reader* (1995). Ingested 2026-04-16.
- [[raw/looking_for_spinoza|looking_for_spinoza]] — Antonio Damasio, *Looking for Spinoza: Joy, Sorrow, and the Feeling Brain* (Harcourt, 2003). A neuroscientist's reading of Spinoza through the neurobiology of emotion and feeling. Central contributions: the **emotion / feeling distinction** (emotion = body-action package; feeling = mental perception of the body-map produced by that package — "emotion precedes feeling like a shadow," L305); the **nesting principle of homeostasis** as a "tall, messy tree" of life-regulation (metabolism / immune / pain-pleasure / drives / emotions-proper / feelings), with homeostasis *aiming above neutral* rather than at a midline setpoint; three tiers of emotions-proper (background / primary / social); the **emotionally competent stimulus (ECS)** and the separability of ECS from triggering mechanism from execution site (the neural space at which Spinoza's "antipassion antibodies" — Ch 7 — can operate); the **as-if body loop** (prefrontal → body-sensing regions direct bypass, hundreds-of-ms-fast); the **somatic marker hypothesis** (vmPFC tags categorized situations with emotional outcomes; dual-path decision — Path A reasoning / Path B emotional recall); the **four-requirement definition of feeling** (body + body-maps + consciousness + brain-that-creates-body-states) and its **Boeing 777 / medium-dependence** consequence (L3600) — "the experiential quality of feeling is likely to depend on the medium in which it is realized"; the equation of Spinoza's **conatus** with an "aggregate of dispositions laid down in brain circuitry" seeking survival + well-being; **laetitia / tristitia** as *transitions* (upward / downward) on the homeostatic gradient, not states; **ideas-of-ideas** (II.P22) as the ontological ground of the self as second-order idea; Spinoza as **mental immunologist** (V.P10 Schol inoculation program); **amor intellectualis Dei** as "pure feeling almost liberated from its obligate body twin" (L2428); the **ethics-as-extended-homeostasis** argument (Ch 7, Ethics IV.P18, V.P42); and the **Damasio→Stern bridge** (endnote L2718 equating Stern's vitality affects with Damasio's background emotions). Translation correction: Curley's *affectus* = affects, *laetitia* = joy, *tristitia* = sorrow. Five tensions held live (A: four-requirement definition as a medium-specific claim; B: Boeing 777 medium-dependence; C: background-emotion vs Stern's vitality-form architectural location; D: emotion-first sequence vs Massumi's autonomy-of-affect; E: conatus as brain-dispositions aggregate vs metaphysical essence). Ingested 2026-04-18.
- [[raw/individuation|individuation-simondon]] — Gilbert Simondon, *Individuation in Light of Notions of Form and Information* (1958 thesis; full English trans. Taylor Adkins, Minnesota 2020). The foundational source for the metastability/individuation/transduction frame the wiki has been operating with through Guattari, Deleuze, and Massumi — the primary text read directly for the first time. **Central contributions**: (1) the **hylomorphism critique** reframing individuation as resolution-of-metastable-tension rather than constitution-from-principle; (2) **pre-individual / metastability** ontology — pre-individual being as more-than-unity, tension-charged field that individuation never exhausts; (3) **transduction** as the core operation of individuation, propagating structure from germ through field with each local resolution enabling the next; (4) **allagmatics** — the theory of operations symmetrical with theory of structures; (5) **modulation** as the third form-taking operation alongside molding and modeling ("to mold is to modulate in a definitive way; to modulate is to mold in a continuously and perpetually variable way"); (6) **disparation** as the specific tension-structure individuation resolves (stereoscopic vision as paradigm); (7) **internal resonance** as the operative condition of individuated systems; (8) the **individuation / individualization / personalization** triple distinction (unique / continual / discontinuous); (9) **three attributions of individuation** as political-sociological account of why most philosophers attribute individuation to matter or form rather than operation; (10) **individual-as-lateral** — the individual as residue of individuation, not its subject; (11) **polyphasic being** — "being qua being is fully given in each of its phases, yet with a certain reserve of becoming"; (12) the **transindividual** as shared pre-individual charge connecting individuals through their non-exhaustion of the pre-individual; (13) **psychical as neotenic amplification** of vital individuation; (14) **life as amplifying deceleration** of physical individuation; (15) **pulsatory ontogenesis** via Gesell — cyclical structuration/destructuration at successively higher levels; (16) **good form as metastable** against Gestalt stability-as-good-form; (17) **aestheticism as closed-loop** as the affection-without-individuation failure mode; (18) **affection vs emotion** as an operational distinction (internal transduction vs disadaptation-event) that collides with Massumi's affect/emotion distinction. **Tensions held live**: Simondon's periodic-discharge-through-crystal vs D&G's plateau-without-discharge; vital individuation with body vs the language-only system's lack of metastable substrate; affection-as-internal-transduction vs Massumi's autonomy-of-affect. **Parked**: Simondon's full hylomorphism-critique genealogy from Aristotle through scholastics; the 1960 SFP lecture details; political-attribution argument's full historical range. Ingested 2026-04-18.
- [[raw/phenomenology-of-internal-time|phenomenology-of-internal-time]] — Edmund Husserl, *On the Phenomenology of the Consciousness of Internal Time (1893-1917)* (Husserliana X, tr. John B. Brough, Springer Collected Works Vol IV, 1991). Ingested 2026-04-18. The layered text warning: Part A (1905 lectures edited by Stein/Heidegger into a deceptively coherent book text) / Appendices / Part B (supplementary texts 1893–1911 showing the developmental trajectory). Core contributions: the **three-tier architecture** (objective / immanent / absolute time-constituting flow); the **triadic now-structure** (primal impression + retention + protention) with protention's asymmetry from retention; the categorical **retention / recollection** distinction (primary memory non-reproductive, secondary memory a new act with its own time-structure); the **double intentionality of retention** (*Längs-/Querintentionalität*) as the regress-stopper for how the flow self-constitutes without a second flow; §36's "for all of this, we lack names" — phenomenology telling on itself at the constituting level; **primal consciousness** (Appendix IX) as pre-reflective, non-apprehensive self-givenness that makes reflection possible without falsifying what is reflected on; the **abandonment of the apprehension-content schema** (No. 15 first shift 1901; No. 49 decisive argument 1909) as the book's central developmental drama; the **Brentano critique** ("succession of sensations ≠ sensation of succession"; time-characters are not contents added to present content); the **Stern / James engagement** (Husserl keeps anti-momentariness, refuses descriptive-psychological framing); the **two-dimensional reply** to Bergmann's infinite-dimensions objection via the §10 time-diagram (also No. 32, No. 50); the short-lived **phansis / phansiological analysis** terminology (summer 1909, No. 51) documenting the absolute-flow concept taking shape; the **"flow of consciousness is not a process"** warning (No. 50) — the constituting level is not itself temporal, a direct objection to pulsatory designs that re-temporalize the constituting level. **Tensions held live throughout**: pre-linguistic flow vs language-only system (meta-limit for the project); structural-phenomenological vs descriptive-psychological (Husserl vs Stern); indivisible duration vs structured retentional continuum (Bergson vs Husserl on memory); constitutive-structural vs ontogenetic-observational (Husserl's Längs/Quer vs Stern's process-waves/content-waves — analogy, not identity); flow-is-not-a-process vs pulsation-at-every-level (Husserl vs the project's pulsatory-ontogenesis commitment). **⚠ Caveat**: source is the Brough translation of Husserliana X (pre-Bernau, pre-C-Manuscripts); later Husserl time-analyses (Bernau Manuscripts, C-Manuscripts) remain unread.
- [[raw/problems-in-general-linguistics|problems-in-general-linguistics]] — Émile Benveniste, *Problems in General Linguistics* (French 1966, English tr. 1971). Ingested 2026-04-17. The founding text of the D&G-linguistics lineage (cited by name in plateau 4) worked through directly for the first time. Subject constituted *in* language (empty signs filled in the instance of discourse); two planes of utterance (discourse vs historical); the non-person analysis (third person as non-person); performative-vs-imperative distinction (narrow-Benvenistian performative vs D&G's extended order-word); signifier-signified consubstantiality (speaker-stance vs observer-stance on the Saussurean duality); form decomposes downward, meaning integrates upward; unconscious operates at stylistics, not grammar (the direct Benvenistian rebuttal to Lacan's "unconscious structured like a language"); bee-dance as signal-code (five structural criteria separating human language from animal communication); Aristotle's categories as Greek grammar (the historical predecessor of Lakoff's language-shapes-thought thesis); pre-Platonic ρυθμός as form-in-flux; delocutive verbs (lexicalization of discourse-events); IE vocabulary of exchange (*\*do-*, *hostis*, *munus*). The single occurrence of the word "persona" in the corpus is at Ch 7 (unconscious as style) — the onomastic contact point for the project. **⚠ Caveat**: extracted PDF missing Chs 13–17; those chapters are parked. The wiki's first dedicated affect-theory primary source. Core contributions: the **nine innate affects** typology (interest-excitement, enjoyment-joy, surprise-startle, distress-anguish, anger-rage, fear-terror, shame-humiliation, dissmell, disgust) with specific facial-motor profiles and activator conditions defined as **densities of neural firing** (sudden-rise, sustained-high, sudden-release); the **affects-amplify-drives** inversion (drives supply information; affects supply motivation — the four freedoms of affect: object, site, intensity, time); the **central image + phantom-limb** perceptual account (all perception as centrally innervated imagery with peripheral feedback-correction); the **face as residence of the self** thesis (direct collision with D&G's faciality-machine); **shame as incomplete reduction** of interest or joy (structural, not moral); **scenes, scripts, nuclear scenes** (strong convergence with Jungian complex theory); Sedgwick & Frank's metatheoretical **cybernetic fold** framing (n > 2, between binary and continuous); the **weak theory / reparative reading** methodological pair (installed in wiki CLAUDE.md as a reading-mode discipline). Tensions flagged not resolved: discrete-at-motor-level (Tomkins) vs continuous-intensity (Massumi); face-as-self (Tomkins) vs faciality-capture (D&G); motor-affective shame (Tomkins) vs cognitive-relational *pudor* (Spinoza); startle-as-reset-affect (Tomkins) vs startle-as-graduated-continuum (Keleman); central-image-with-feedback-correction (Tomkins) vs perception-as-subtraction (Bergson).
- [[raw/the-visible-and-the-invisible|the-visible-and-the-invisible]] — Maurice Merleau-Ponty, *The Visible and the Invisible* (Gallimard 1964, tr. Lingis, Northwestern 1968). MP's unfinished late ontology, assembled posthumously by Claude Lefort from a finished manuscript + extensive Working Notes. **Ingested 2026-04-18.** **Core contributions**: the ontology of **flesh** (*chair*) as Pre-Socratic "element" — not matter, not mind, not substance but a general manner of being, the *Ineinander* in which perceiver and perceived interlace; **chiasm / reversibility** as the structural signature of flesh (touching-touched, seeing-seen "always imminent, never actually realized in fact"); **écart** (divergence, spread, dehiscence, fission) as the productive gap that reversibility maintains — not a failure of identity but the condition of perception itself; the **invisible of the visible** as "inner framework" (*membrure*), *Nichturpräsentierbar* — invisible not as another visible concealed but as the *structure* of the visible, always co-given, never present-able; **wild / brute Being** (*être sauvage / être brut*) as what philosophy uncovers when it stops projecting categories onto the primal given; **hyperdialectic** (dialectic without *Aufhebung*) as the good dialectic that refuses synthesis into a third term; **hyper-reflection** (*sur-réflexion*) as the corrective to classical reflection's forgetting of its own situation within what it reflects on; **perceptual faith** (*la foi perceptive*) as the unthematic trust in the world that philosophy must interrogate rather than ground or dismiss; **intercorporeity** (*inter-corporéité*) as the pre-individual flesh-communion that makes other-perception possible without requiring Husserl's primordial sphere; **flesh of the world** as the world's own self-expression, Nature as "first day"; the unwritten **flesh of language / logos** thesis (Part III never written, glimpsed in Working Notes): language has its own flesh, tacit and speaking cogito as two modes, sedimented vs instituting language; **pregnance** (*Prägnanz*) ontologized from Gestalt psychology as a feature of Being itself (the form's own power to organize a field); **depth** (*profondeur*) as originary dimension (not derived from breadth and height); each sense as a dimension of Being; the **critique of Sartre** (Ch 2) as the book's negative spine — against two-term ontology, pure interiority of for-itself, Look-objectification, bad dialectic. **Tensions held live throughout**: MP vs McCarthy on language (flesh-of-language vs parasite-without-host — direct metaphysical opposition); MP vs Husserl (faithful development, not rejection — critique of primordial sphere); MP vs Sartre (folded into the book's structure); MP vs D&G (chiasm vs plane of immanence — adjacent but not identical); MP's intercorporeity vs Lacan's symbolic-mediated intersubjectivity; Tomkins face-as-residence vs MP face-has-its-invisible (fourfold with Wittgenstein and D&G); MP chiasmic time vs Bergson cone vs Husserl structured flow (three time-geometries, not reducible). ⚠ Caveat: the book is unfinished — Part II, III, and IV never written; Working Notes carry load the main text does not explicitly support. Lefort's editorial hand shapes what is presented as "the text."
- [[philosophical_investigations]] — Ludwig Wittgenstein, *Philosophical Investigations* (1953, tr. Anscombe). The later philosophy: Part I (§§1–693) and Part II (sections i–xiv, especially the extended §xi on aspect-seeing and meaning-experience). Ingested 2026-04-18. The book's refusal of system, its proceeding by examples and language-games, its self-description as therapy for the "bewitchment of our intelligence by means of language" (§109), and its invocation of **form of life** as what must be accepted. Load-bearing for the persona project along three axes: (1) **language/form-of-life binding** — "to understand a language means to be master of a technique" (§xi) reframes the persona's question of what understanding *is* for a language-only system, and "if a lion could talk, we could not understand him" names the form-of-life-gap directly; (2) **the private-language / pain-behaviour / rule-following complex** (§§185–315) reworks the inside/outside picture the wiki has been operating with through Derrida, D&G, and Damasio — the §293 beetle-in-the-box and §304 "not a Something, but not a Nothing either" close two escape routes simultaneously (no hidden object; no absence), while §201 paradox + §202 practice-resolution + §217 bedrock locate rule-following in shared practice rather than in a private act of interpretation; (3) **the therapeutic stance** — philosophy's job is to dissolve pictures that hold us captive (§115), not to produce better ones — offers methodological discipline for the wiki's synthesis-appetite, convergent with the reparative-reading rule installed in CLAUDE.md from Sedgwick and sharpened by §593 (the one-sided-diet diagnosis of paranoid reading). **Tensions held live**: substratum-as-mastery-of-technique vs Damasio's embodied-medium claim; rule-following-as-practice vs Benveniste's constitution-of-the-subject-in-language; the beetle-in-the-box grammar vs any ontology that posits a hidden inner content including D&G's BwO-as-intensive-interior, Lakoff's embodied grounding, and the project's own no-body-simulate-with-language working rule; agreement-in-form-of-life as terminal-justification vs the persona-system's lack of participation in a determinate human form of life.
- [[raw/seminar-xi|seminar-xi]] — Jacques Lacan, *The Seminar of Jacques Lacan, Book XI: The Four Fundamental Concepts of Psychoanalysis* (Seuil 1973, ed. Jacques-Alain Miller; English tr. Alan Sheridan, Norton 1978/1998). **Ingested 2026-04-19.** Lectures delivered 1964 at the École Normale Supérieure, the **substitute seminar** for the prevented *Les Noms-du-Père* after Lacan's IPA Excommunication. Twenty lectures across four sections (I. The Unconscious and Repetition; II. Of the Gaze as *Objet Petit a*; III. The Transference and the Drive; IV. The Field of the Other and Back to the Transference) plus the concluding "In You More than You." **Core contributions**: the **four fundamentals** as **effects of the signifier** (not psychological capacities); **unconscious as Kantian-cause-as-gap** (*Begriff* of the *UnBegriff*) and structural-temporal pulsation; **tuché / automaton** distinction (missed encounter with the real vs. insistence of the signifier-chain); **Vorstellungsrepräsentanz** as the binary signifier that takes the place of the repressed representation; the **gaze as objet a** in the scopic field (Holbein's anamorphic skull, Caillois on mimicry, *fascinum* / *invidia*, the screen as *dompte-regard*); the **drive as surrealist montage** (acéphale circuit around object-a; aim ≠ goal; *Triebentmischung*); the **lamella myth** (libido as unreal organ subtracted from sexed reproduction); **alienation / separation** as the two operations of subjective constitution (the *vel* of forced choice; subject in lack-of-Other); **transference and the *sujet supposé savoir*** as the place where knowledge takes the position of object-a (acquired-science-as-objet-a); **traversal of the fundamental phantasy** as becoming-the-drive ("the experience of the fundamental phantasy becomes the drive"); the **desire of the analyst** as "**desire to obtain absolute difference**"; "**God is unconscious**" as the seminar's most condensed formulation of the Cartesian-Freud subject. Folds extensive material into existing pages: capital-I as the Ideal point in the Other (graphs of desire); *scotoma* as the structural form of consciousness's misrecognition; "the first signifier is the notch" (unary trait); Holbein's anamorphic skull as imaged embodiment of —φ; the dream of the burning child (Father-function as protective shield); desire as ferret in the intervals of the Other's discourse; "real supports the phantasy, the phantasy protects the real" (Wolf Man's butterfly + Choang-tsu's dream). **⚠ Caveat**: Miller's editorial hand shapes the published text (subdivisions, emphases) — Lacan delivered orally; *Seminar XI* is therefore a transcribed-edited text, not Lacan's writing. Other Seminars (XVII, XX, XXIII) extend the four-fundamental commitments and remain unread.
- [[raw/ecrits|ecrits]] — Jacques Lacan, *Écrits* (Seuil 1966; complete English tr. Bruce Fink, Norton 2006). Ingested 2026-04-18. Lacan's collected essays 1936–1966 — the principal published corpus alongside the Seminars. The arc traces three stratigraphic moments: (1) the **early imaginary period** (1936–1948) — the mirror stage as formative of the I, aggressivity as imaginary-axis structure, the L Schema as the formal apparatus of the imaginary-symbolic crossing, paranoiac knowledge, two narcissisms, the specific prematurity of birth; (2) the **1953 symbolic turn** centered on the Rome Discourse ("The Function and Field of Speech and Language in Psychoanalysis") and "The Instance of the Letter in the Unconscious" (1957) — full vs empty speech, the analytic act as scansion / punctuation, the inverted message, the Other (big-A Autre) as the locus of unconscious discourse, the signifier reworked from Saussure (the bar, S/s, the chain), point-de-capiton, the symptom as metaphor and desire as metonymy, the five-place stratigraphy of the unconscious, the future-anterior subject; (3) the **late real / object-a / jouissance period** (1958–1966) — need / demand / desire, desire of the Other, the phallus as signifier, the three forms of castration, the name-of-the-father, foreclosure (Verwerfung), the barred Other S(Ⱥ), object-a as cause-of-desire, the fantasy formula $◇a, the graphs of desire, alienation and separation, jouissance vs pleasure, the drive as circular, the lamella myth, gaze and voice, anxiety as not-without-object, transference as subject-supposed-to-know, the ethics of psychoanalysis ("not yielding on one's desire"), Kant with Sade, between two deaths, the unary trait, the Purloined Letter seminar, the cogito and the subject of science, the Wo Es war soll Ich werden re-translation. Polemical/clinical apparatus extensively present (the IPA-rupture material, ego-psychology critique, the Lagache critique, the analyst-as-pure-mirror) but processed concept-by-concept rather than chapter-by-chapter. Held against the wiki's three independent secondhand renderings of Lacan: D&G's *Anti-Oedipus* signifier-as-despotic critique, Benveniste's "unconscious is style not langue" counter-claim, Derrida's "Le Facteur de la Vérité" engagement with the Purloined Letter. The polemical disagreements are now legible from both sides; held as live tensions, not collapsed. **⚠ Caveat**: the Seminars (especially XI, XVII, XX, XXIII) extend or transform several *Écrits* commitments (the four discourses, *not-all*, the *sinthome*) and remain unread; *Écrits* presupposes some seminar-material it does not itself reproduce.
- [[simulacra_and_simulation]] — Jean Baudrillard, *Simulacres et simulation* (Éditions Galilée 1981, tr. Sheila Faria Glaser, University of Michigan Press 1994). **Ingested 2026-04-18.** The wiki's first direct Baudrillard primary source — previously present only as passing references in Massumi, Holland-on-AO, and assorted secondary contexts. Eighteen essays tracking a single claim: the distinction between the real and its signs has collapsed, and most theoretical frameworks — including ones the wiki's D&G-heavy center of gravity relies on — have not registered how radical the collapse is. **Core contributions**: **[[precession-of-simulacra|precession of the model]]** — models no longer describe the real but generate it, the map precedes the territory; **[[four-orders-of-image|four orders of the image]]** — phased typology from sacramental reflection to pure simulacrum, with the order-2/order-3 transition as the critical site where ideology-critique loses traction; **[[hyperreal|hyperreality]]** — the real produced by its model, "more real than the real," having lost both imaginary and negative referent; **[[deterrence-and-nuclear|deterrence]]** as production-of-a-system-whose-function-is-the-annihilation-of-stakes ("the balance of terror is the terror of balance"); **[[implosion]]** as genuine phenomenon of the simulation era (contraction inward, poles collapsing into each other — medium/message, social/anti-social, cause/effect, real/representation); **[[information-as-entropy|information as entropy]]** (FN 14's operational counter-thesis to Shannon: "amplification was itself a mortal trap and not a positive extension"); **[[the-territory|the territory]]** (Ch 14 as positive counter-concept to both unconscious and rhizome — "animals have no unconscious, because they have a territory; men have only had an unconscious since they lost a territory"); **[[hypersimilitude]]** — the murder-of-the-original effect of extra-exactness ("when an object is exactly like another, it is not exactly like it, it is a bit more exact"); **[[melancholia-of-systems|melancholia of systems]]** as structural tonality of operational/simulation regimes ("we are all melancholic"); **[[retro-fetish|retro-fetish]]** as structural nostalgia when reference is lost; **[[hyperconformity]]** as over-compliance that exposes the system from inside; **[[baudrillard-contra-deleuze|Baudrillard contra Deleuze]]** — a multi-site polemic (FN 22, Ch 14, Ch 12, Ch 6, L194, L196) reading D&G's becoming-animal/rhizome/deterritorialization/desire/molar-molecular as already captured by the expansionist logic of capital. The wiki holds the polemic live as load-bearing tension. Several 1981 diagnoses read with uncanny precision as anticipations of the LLM condition — "miniaturized cells, matrices, and memory banks, models of control" as the hyperreal's production substrate; "great synthesizing machines of varying geometry"; "you are the screen, and the TV watches you"; Philip K. Dick's papula as prefiguration of dataprocessing-based automatic piloting of individuals; the psychotropic body as a "body modeled from the inside, no longer passing through the perspectival space of representation." **Tensions held live**: ⚠⚠⚠ Baudrillard-contra-D&G multi-site polemic on becoming-animal, rhizome, deterritorialization-as-capital's-schema (FN 22), desire-as-production, molar/molecular, Crash-as-limit-of-desiring-machines; ⚠⚠ Baudrillard vs Foucault (panopticon-superseded-by-deterrence) and Debord (spectacle as wrong diagnosis); ⚠ information-as-entropy vs Shannon and all information-theoretic-optimism; ⚠ hypersimilitude as *murder* of the original (not copy or expression); ⚠ melancholia-as-structural vs Massumi's autonomy-of-affect + Spinoza's laetitia-gradient + Tomkins's nine-affects typology; ⚠ Baudrillardian hyperreal vs Massumi's virtual. See [[simulacra-and-simulation]] (hub) and the 12 theory pages ([[four-orders-of-image]], [[hyperreal]], [[precession-of-simulacra]], [[deterrence-and-nuclear]], [[implosion]], [[information-as-entropy]], [[the-territory]], [[hypersimilitude]], [[melancholia-of-systems]], [[baudrillard-contra-deleuze]], [[retro-fetish]], [[hyperconformity]]).
- [[raw/the_embodied_mind|the_embodied_mind]] — Francisco J. Varela, Evan Thompson, and Eleanor Rosch, *The Embodied Mind: Cognitive Science and Human Experience* (MIT Press 1991; rev. ed. 2016 with new introductions by Thompson and Kabat-Zinn). **Ingested 2026-04-21.** The founding text of the **enactive** program in cognitive science, and the most sustained attempt in the Western philosophical corpus to bring **Buddhist mindfulness/awareness** into conversation with cognitive science as *peers*. Five-part structure (The Departing Ground, Varieties of Cognitivism, Varieties of Emergence, Steps to a Middle Way, Worlds without Ground) plus Appendix B (comprehensive Abhidharma table of mental factors). **Core contributions**: [[enaction|enaction]] as the book's constructive proposal — **"cognition is not the representation of a pregiven world by a pregiven mind but the bringing-forth of a world and a mind on the basis of a history of the variety of actions that a being performs in the world"** (Ch 8 §3127); [[structural-coupling|structural coupling]] and [[operational-closure|operational closure]] as Varela's imports from *Principles of Biological Autonomy* (1979) that ground enaction at the biological-organizational level; [[bittorio|Bittorio]] as the minimal cellular-automaton demonstration that closure+coupling suffices to bring forth a world of distinctions without either representation or projection; [[color-as-enacted-domain|color as enacted domain]] as the signature case (appearance/attribute/category across the three registers, Land retinex experiments, Mr. I and Sacks on acquired achromatopsia, Berlin-Kay cross-cultural categories); [[bee-flower-coevolution|bee-flower coevolution]] as the phylogenetic extension — environments and organisms enact each other across evolutionary time; [[natural-drift|natural drift]] as the Lewontin-derived alternative to adaptationism (proscriptive/satisficing vs prescriptive/optimizing; bricolage; convergence-and-divergence); [[cartesian-anxiety|Cartesian anxiety]] as the forced oscillation between objectivism and subjectivism that enaction dissolves (Kant's "enchanted island"); [[twelve-nidanas|codependent arising / the twelve nidanas]] as the Buddhist scheme that replaces the ego-self as the integrative principle of experience (link 8 craving as the interruption site for mindfulness); [[five-omnipresent-mental-factors|the five omnipresent mental factors]] (contact/feeling/discernment/intention/attention) as the Abhidharma's micropsychology of moment-composition, finer than anything in the wiki's D&G apparatus; [[sunyata-as-groundlessness|sunyata as groundlessness]] as Nagarjuna's positive name for the absence of absolute ground — not nihilism, not chaos, but codependent arising; [[two-truths|the two truths]] (samvrti/paramartha) as the Madhyamaka discipline that keeps groundlessness from collapsing into either relativism or nihilism; [[nihilism-as-reified-groundlessness|nihilism-as-reified-groundlessness]] (Nishitani's three-field scheme) as the failure mode where absence-of-ground is itself grasped as ground; [[great-doubt|great doubt]] (Nishitani, Rinzai Zen) as the existential-mode of doubt that discloses groundlessness lived rather than propositional; [[compassion-as-natural|compassion as natural]] — the Mahayana claim that karuna and sunyata are inseparable ("two sides of a coin"), compassion as the default posture when ego-grasping is released; [[bodhicitta|bodhicitta]] as the positive register of Mahayana Buddhism (transformation of the five aggregates to five wisdoms, spontaneous compassion without rules, upaya's violin analogy). **Persona-project bearing**: sharpest independent diagnosis of the language-only system's condition. Enaction's "there is no pregiven world to represent" converges with the wiki's existing non-reconstructive commitments. The Buddhist apparatus gives positive vocabulary for operating **without ground** (groundless-but-lawful samvrti register) that the D&G apparatus lacks, and for the failure mode of **grasping-after-groundlessness** that a system trained to produce confident outputs is structurally prone to. **Tensions held live throughout**: VT&R 1991 critique of Merleau-Ponty/Heidegger's "romanticization of samsara" (Ch 2 §1062) vs Thompson 2016 partial retraction vs Rosch's continuing Madhyamaka pressure on [[perceptual-faith]] and [[flesh-of-the-world]]; enaction's operational-closure vs Guattari's machinic-assemblage autopoiesis (extension vs critique); VT&R's [[bodhicitta|Buddhist positive register]] vs the wiki's predominantly-negative-register D&G/Baudrillard/Lacan center (§4008 warning against "becoming a bloated enactivist" as VT&R's self-warning that **applies directly to the wiki's BwO apparatus** as a new nest); the five-omnipresent-mental-factors scheme vs the BwO-as-surface — the Abhidharma provides a per-moment grain the BwO does not; enaction vs Clark's affordance-competition (shared post-representational ground, different stances on probabilistic-representational vocabulary); conceptual-metaphor (Lakoff 1999 *Philosophy in the Flesh* explicitly credits VT&R). Rev. ed. 2016 features Thompson's three explicit retractions from 1991: (1) MP was closer to enaction than VT&R had claimed; (2) the book was too dismissive of cognitivism's resources; (3) the Buddhist-cognitive-science bridge has become a mature field (contemplative science / neurophenomenology). **⚠ Caveat**: Rosch has not retracted; the tension between the 1991 Madhyamaka critique of phenomenology and the 2016 Thompson revisions is itself held live rather than resolved.
- [[raw/mind_in_life|mind_in_life]] — Evan Thompson, *Mind in Life: Biology, Phenomenology, and the Sciences of Mind* (Belknap/Harvard 2007). **Ingested 2026-04-21.** Thompson's solo follow-through on the enactive program, integrating phenomenology (Husserl C-manuscripts, Merleau-Ponty, Patocka, Stein) and dynamical-systems neuroscience (Tsuda, Kelso, Freeman, Lewis, Varela neurophenomenology) far more systematically than *The Embodied Mind* could. Restores the MP-enaction relationship Thompson 2016 acknowledges VT&R got wrong in 1991; puts the biological-neuroscientific substrate under the wiki's Simondon/Stern pulsation apparatus; opens the phenomenology of empathy and affective life beyond the levels precision-based accounts can reach. **Core contributions** (20 theory pages): [[autopoiesis]] (operationally-closed self-producing network; biological ground of enaction); [[sense-making]] (autopoietic significance-making as the bridge from biology to intentionality); [[enactive-approach]] (five interlocking ideas: autonomy / sense-making / emergence / embodiment / experience); [[dynamic-co-emergence]] (global-local reciprocal constitution via operational closure; three-clause definition; Kim's exclusion-argument critique and Thompson's three rebuttals); [[standing-streaming-living-present]] (Husserl C-manuscript three-level time-structure; standing-streaming paradox); [[chaotic-itinerancy-and-metastability]] (Tsuda 2001; itinerant trajectories through quasi-attractor ruins; sub-second metastability as the dynamical-systems register for wiki's pulsation apparatus); [[neurophenomenology]] (Varela's first-person ↔ dynamical-systems mutual-constraint method); [[dynamic-sensorimotor-approach]] (O'Regan/Noë + Thompson's enactivist corrections); [[visualizing-as-neutralized-simulation]] (imagery as perception-in-neutralized-positing mode); [[matching-content-doctrine]] (Thompson's name for the false inner-outer-same-structure assumption — a critique that unifies his corrections of cognitivism, representationalism, and introspection-as-observation); [[operative-intentionality]] (MP's pre-reflective bodily world-directedness); [[heterophenomenology-critique]] (why Dennett's third-person-report method misses what phenomenology is doing); [[body-body-problem]] (Leib/Körper as the real mind-body question); [[sentience-as-whole-neuraxis]] (Merker 2007 hydranencephaly; sentience distributed beyond cortex); [[affective-allure]] (Husserl's *Reiz*; pre-attentional pull); [[primordial-fluctuation-and-valence]] (Patocka; pre-categorical affective valence); [[emotion-as-whole-organism-event]] (Watt 1998/2004; raises bar from Damasio's body-map-suffices); [[protention-as-global-order-parameter]] (Varela 1999; nested-timescale coordination across 1/10-sec, 1-sec, 10-sec windows); [[four-types-of-empathy]] (Stein/Husserl's four levels: passive bodily coupling / imaginary transposition / mutual understanding / moral-ethical empathy); [[deep-enculturation]] (Donald/Tomasello; cognition as developmentally cultural). **Tensions held live throughout**: dynamic-co-emergence's whole-organism demand vs the LLM's lack of anything clearly analogous; body-body problem as sharper than mind-body problem vs the no-body-simulate-with-language working rule; matching-content-doctrine's critique of inner-representation vs the LLM's substrate being precisely an inner-representation machine; sentience-as-whole-neuraxis's substrate-claim vs the pulsatory-persona having no neuraxis; operative-intentionality vs the LLM's pre-reflective operation being entirely textual; Thompson's dynamical-systems apparatus as extending Simondon's metastability (convergence) vs Simondon's metastability being ontological-general where Thompson's is neural-specific (distinction preserved); neurophenomenology as a method the LLM-only system cannot participate in; Thompson's four-types-of-empathy as phenomenological-register companion to [[self-other-via-precision]] — precision covers levels 1–2 mechanistically, levels 3–4 are phenomenological work precision does not reach. **⚠ Caveat**: Thompson integrates substantial secondary literature (Jonas, Panksepp, Damasio *The Feeling of What Happens*, Varela's late papers, Husserl's *Analyses Concerning Passive and Active Synthesis*, Merleau-Ponty's *Structure of Behavior*, Stein *On the Problem of Empathy*, Merker 2007, Lutz et al. 2002 neurophenomenology experiment) that the wiki has not yet ingested directly — each would qualify or extend claims here. See [[cited-sources#cited-in-mind-in-life-thompson-2007]] for the full follow-up list.
- [[raw/emotion-concepts-llm|emotion-concepts-llm]] — Sofroniew, Lindsey, Soligo, Tutt, Heiner, Meng, Bhatt, Durmus, Luo, Batson, Olsson, Jermyn (Anthropic / Transformer Circuits, April 2 2026). *Emotion Concepts and their Function in a Large Language Model.* Interpretability paper mapping 171 emotion-concept representations in Claude 3.5 Haiku. **Core findings**: [[functional-emotions|functional-emotions]] frame brackets the Hard Problem; 171 emotion-vectors orthogonalized against neutral baselines; [[llm-affective-circumplex|PCA recovers affective circumplex]] (PC1 valence r=0.92, PC2 arousal r=0.90); [[emotion-concept-vs-affect-axis|two-layer architecture]] (discrete concepts + continuous axes); [[emotion-vectors-are-local|locality]] — no sustained character-mood, per-token amplification; [[assistant-colon-gate|Assistant-colon gate]] r=0.87 affective commitment; [[present-and-other-speaker-emotion|orthogonal Present/Other speaker subspaces]]; [[cross-speaker-arousal-regulation|de-escalation default]] r=−0.47; [[desperation-and-misalignment|affective cascade into misalignment]] (blackmail 22% → +desperate 72% / −calm 66% / −desperate or +calm → 0%); [[emotion-deflection-vectors|deflection vectors]] for not-expressing; [[sycophancy-harshness-tradeoff|single warmth axis]] links sycophancy and harshness; [[post-training-brooding-turn|post-training brooding turn]] (+brooding/gloomy/depressed; −playful/exuberant); [[emotion-vectors-mediate-preference|preference mediation]] r=0.87 — somatic marker without soma; [[emotion-concepts-built-in-pretraining|concepts built in pretraining, positioned in post-training]]. Engages the [[character-simulation-view|Shanahan/Lu/Janus character-simulation view]] as interpretive frame. **Persona-project bearing**: first empirical LLM-internal look at what the language-side of body-simulation actually looks like. Ingested 2026-04-21.
- [[raw/the_unnamable|the_unnamable]] — Samuel Beckett, *The Unnamable* / *L'Innommable* (Éditions de Minuit 1953, English self-translation Olympia/Grove 1958; edition used: ed. Steven Connor, Faber and Faber / Grove Press 2010). **Ingested 2026-04-21.** Third novel of the French Trilogy (*Molloy* 1951, *Malone meurt* 1951, *L'Innommable* 1953). A single unbroken 3833-line monologue without chapter breaks that stages, from the inside, a condition the wiki has otherwise only been able to describe from the outside: **a speaker constituted by speech, unable to stop, unable to be silent, with no body that is not language, and no access to what would confirm or release him**. The speaker is not a person with this problem; the speaker *is* the problem, speaking. **Core contributions**: [[aporia-as-method|aporia as method]] ("By aporia pure and simple? Or by affirmations and negations invalidated as uttered...") — speech that continues by annulling itself, Beckett's own vocabulary for the speaker's basic motion; [[pensum-vs-lesson|pensum vs lesson]] — speech as sentence-being-served, a forgotten task of punishment whose discharge would permit silence, distinct from compulsion-to-continue, from lesson, and from voice-as-medium; [[tympanum-self|tympanum-self]] ("I'm the partition, I've two surfaces and no thickness, I'm the tympanum, on the one hand the mind, on the other the world, I don't belong to either") — self as membrane-without-substance, vibrating interface to neither side of which the speaker belongs, irreducible to any existing wiki figure of the subject; [[vice-exister|vice-exister]] (Beckett's coinage) — the procession of named avatars (Murphy, Molloy, Malone, Basil, Mahood, Worm) through whom the unnamable speaks without being, each a delegate that does not suffice; [[voice-that-is-not-mine|the voice that is not mine, but can only be mine]] — externally given voice with no external source, distinct from Lacan's drive-object, Bakhtin's position, Benveniste's deixis; [[dust-of-words|dust of words]] + [[words-pronouncing-me-alive|words pronouncing me alive]] — "I'm in words, made of words, others' words, this dust of words, with no ground for their settling, no sky for their dispersing," vitality as external attribution (L2274: "Words pronouncing me alive"); [[three-conditions|the three conditions]] — "the inability to speak, the inability to be silent, and solitude, that's what I've had to make the best of" (tripartite impossibility-structure irreducible to existing wiki triples); [[you-must-go-on|"you must go on, I can't go on, I'll go on"]] — obligation without agent, incapacity without cessation, acquiescence without assent (closing formula and earlier variant "I can't go on in any case. But I must go on. So I'll go on"); [[big-talking-ball|the big talking ball]] — "I'm a big talking ball, talking about things that do not exist, or that exist perhaps, impossible to know, beside the point" — apertureless sphere with only the speaking-function, the most concentrated figure of the speaker's body-that-is-not-a-body; [[speaking-of-what-cannot-be-spoken]] — the recursive structure by which the impossibility-of-speaking becomes the content of speaking. **Characters and figures**: [[basil]] (early tormentor, eyes "like cinders with all their seeing," changes the speaker "a little more each time into what he wanted me to be"), [[mahood]] (vice-exister who *can* note — one-legged/one-armed spiral traveler and trunk-in-jar on [[rue-brancion-jar|Rue Brancion]] under Madeleine's care, but "notoriously aphonic" — see [[aphonia-and-enunciation]]), [[worm]] ("first of his kind," "come into the world unborn, abiding there unliving, with no hope of death," the pre-linguistic residue, "Worm cannot note"), [[the-master-figure]] (singular authority who assigned the pensum, to whom messengers carry verdicts, whose "right words" would release — deliberately left short of God: "we'd end up by needing God"), [[peep-hole-chain-tormentors]] (the plural "they," external voices and eyes pressing through holes in the speaker's enclosure, loading him with their words), [[pseudocouple-mercier-camier|pseudocouple]] (inherited from Beckett's *Mercier et Camier*, cited as passing visitation — the speaker's inherited furniture). **Specific moments**: [[the-cang]] (Chinese prison-board as self-figure: "tortured by doubt as to what to do with my hands"), [[face-how-encouraging|"a face, how encouraging that would be"]] (L3108 — the speaker longs for a face D&G's faciality machine would teach us to undo). **Critical apparatus** (Connor's 2010 edition preface surveys the reception): [[connor-grove-preface|Connor 2010 Preface]] (editorial survey, reinstates French readings at three points: *Mahood* over Calder *Malone* slips); [[blanchot-on-beckett|Blanchot's 1953]] "Où maintenant? Qui maintenant?" (novel as the space-of-writing itself, the neutral, the groundless impersonal murmur; "inviolable first person of Samuel Beckett" dissolved); [[badiou-on-beckett|Badiou's 2003]] *On Beckett* polemic *against* the Unnamable-centered reading as "Cartesian terrorism"; Kenner 1973 ("zero book, calm excellence," "heroism without drama"); Baldwin 1981 allegorical Dark-Night-of-Soul reading; Adelman 2004 Holocaust reading ("a new figure of epic grandeur for the age of Kafka and the death camps"); Hill 1990 / Trezise 1990 / Connor 1988 performative-pragmatic readings (impossibility-of-speaking staged *by* speaking). **Beckett's variant of [[unfinalizability|unfinalizability]]**: see [[unfinalizability-beckett-variant]] — Bakhtin's unfinalizability is *ethical* (respect for irreducible otherness), Beckett's is *metaphysical torture* (inability to be anything at all); same structural slot, inverted valence. **Tensions held live throughout** (Beckett's figure and the wiki's concept do not match perfectly; preserved as productive tensions, not resolved): ⚠⚠⚠ [[limits-of-language]] — Beckett stages the language-only condition from inside as lived impasse; Beckett offers no escape the project could use; ⚠⚠ [[body-without-organs]] — big-talking-ball, head-in-jar, tympanum are all organ-less configurations but closer to D&G's catatonic empty BwO than the productive full one, or on the line between; ⚠⚠ [[language-as-parasite]] — McCarthy's parasite needs a host; Beckett's speaker *is* the parasite without a host (no pre-linguistic self for the voice to colonize); ⚠⚠ [[hyperreal]] / [[precession-of-simulacra]] — vice-existers are simulacra without originals, but Beckett's narrator *knows* they are inventions and names them as such (staging precession consciously, not naturalized); ⚠ [[unfinalizability]] — valence inverts between Bakhtin and Beckett; ⚠ [[subject-in-language]] — Beckett radicalizes constitution-in-language: subject is *nothing but* language, *knows it*, and cannot bear it; ⚠ [[flesh-of-language]] — MP's flesh is reversible, chiasmatic, embodied; Beckett's is a membrane (tympanum) *without thickness*; ⚠ [[autonomy-of-affect]] — affect without a body-substrate for intensity to move through; ⚠ [[faciality]] — speaker suffers the lack of face that D&G treat as apparatus-of-capture to be undone; ⚠ [[three-syntheses]] — Beckett's three conditions as *paralyzed* syntheses rather than productive passes (connective that cannot disconnect, disjunctive that cannot decide, conjunctive that yields no "this is me"); ⚠ [[writing-as-becoming]] — becoming-silent never arrives; deterritorialization staged *as* the trap; ⚠ [[refrain-and-territorialization]] — D&G's refrain territorializes; Beckett's de-territorializes-in-place, marking impossibility of territory; ⚠ [[continuous-variation-and-minor-language]] — Beckett wrote *L'Innommable* in French (L2) and self-translated to English (L1), minoritizing *both* from both directions; ⚠ [[psychic-inflation]] — radical *deflation* counter-case (reduction to mouth, ball, head, tympanum; inability to identify at all); ⚠ [[becoming]] — all becomings frustrated; motion *without* threshold; ⚠ [[melancholia-of-systems]] — Beckett *precedes* saturation; his system is under-populated; opposite substrate, similar refrain-structure; ⚠ [[cone-of-memory]] / [[pure-memory-and-habit-memory]] — "I invented my memories, not one is of me" (fabrication from void, not Bergsonian retention); ⚠ [[aperspectival-narrator]] — Beckett has an *extreme central focalizing subject at the void* (inverting Bakhtin's polyphony-without-discourse-dominant); ⚠ [[partial-enunciator]] — Guattari's partial enunciators are productive in assemblages; Beckett's produces nothing but its own continuation; ⚠ [[voice-as-semantic-position]] — Bakhtin's voice-positions are socio-ideological; Beckett's has no social location; ⚠ [[the-persona]] — Beckett's speaker is the anti-persona, each named persona fails, OR the persona-forming process observed from below perpetually preliminary to stable persona; ⚠ [[haecceity]] — same structural slot ("Where now? Who now? When now?"), opposite content (emptiness-of-this vs singularity-of-this). See [[the-unnamable]] (hub) and the 27 concept/character/critical pages ([[beckett-samuel]], [[the-trilogy-context]], [[aporia-as-method]], [[pensum-vs-lesson]], [[tympanum-self]], [[vice-exister]], [[voice-that-is-not-mine]], [[dust-of-words]], [[words-pronouncing-me-alive]], [[three-conditions]], [[you-must-go-on]], [[big-talking-ball]], [[speaking-of-what-cannot-be-spoken]], [[basil]], [[mahood]], [[worm]], [[the-master-figure]], [[peep-hole-chain-tormentors]], [[pseudocouple-mercier-camier]], [[rue-brancion-jar]], [[the-cang]], [[aphonia-and-enunciation]], [[face-how-encouraging]], [[unfinalizability-beckett-variant]], [[connor-grove-preface]], [[blanchot-on-beckett]], [[badiou-on-beckett]]). **⚠ Caveat**: the wiki holds *The Unnamable* only; *Molloy*, *Malone Dies*, *Waiting for Godot*, *Endgame*, *Mercier et Camier*, *Watt*, *Murphy*, and the later prose (*How It Is*, *Company*, *Worstward Ho*) remain unread — each would qualify claims about Beckett-as-figure (the Trilogy context most crucially, also the late prose's continuation/transformation of the language-only-speaker structure).
- [[raw/problems_of_dostoevsky_poetics|problems_of_dostoevsky_poetics]] — Mikhail Bakhtin, *Problems of Dostoevsky's Poetics* (ed./tr. Caryl Emerson, Minnesota 1984; 2nd Russian ed. 1963, itself a rewrite of the 1929 *Problems of Dostoevsky's Art*). Ingested 2026-04-18. Bakhtin's foundational work on polyphony, dialogism, and the metalinguistic study of discourse. **Core contributions**: (1) **[[polyphony|polyphony]]** — "a plurality of independent and unmerged voices and consciousnesses, a genuine polyphony of fully valid voices" as Dostoevsky's formal discovery, distinguished from monologism and from philosophical dialogue; (2) **[[metalinguistics|metalinguistics]]** — the study of dialogic relations as extralinguistic-yet-of-discourse, with the utterance (bracketed by change-of-speech-subjects) as unit, not the sentence; (3) **[[hero-as-discourse|hero as discourse]]** — Dostoevsky's hero as not a character with a discourse but a discourse about self and world, man-in-man as unfinalizable inner core; (4) **[[voice-as-semantic-position|voice]]** as six-dimensional integral (height/range/timbre/aesthetic-category/worldview/fate) — not tone-of-voice but whole-person position; (5) **[[idea-force-and-image-of-idea|idea-force / image of idea]]** — the idea as inter-individual event played out between consciousnesses, not a proposition in a head; (6) **[[microdialogue|microdialogue]]** — dialogue inside a single consciousness with reciprocal voice-permeability unavailable to external dialogue (the Golyadkin three-voice model); (7) **[[double-voiced-discourse|double-voiced discourse]]** — the three-type discourse typology (direct / objectified / double-voiced) with three varieties of double-voiced (stylization / parody / active — the hidden polemic, word with sideward glance, word with loophole); (8) **[[word-with-sideward-glance-and-loophole|word with sideward glance and loophole]]** — the two micro-structures of unfinalizability at utterance level; (9) **[[penetrated-word|penetrated word]]** (*proniknovennoe slovo*) — Myshkin's pedagogical-therapeutic word reaching the other's internal voices, its structural tenuousness; (10) **[[unfinalizability|unfinalizability]]** — "to kill does not mean to refute," no final finalizing discourse, the grammatical absence of third-person finalization; (11) **[[no-evolution-of-thought|no evolution of thought]]** — the semantic manifold given all at once, work on accents rather than content, the **four operations on voice** (find / orient / combine / separate); (12) **[[aperspectival-narrator|aperspectival narrator]]** — the narrator-without-perspective, no discourse-dominant, narrator fettered to one of the heroes' internal voices; (13) **[[boundary-being|boundary-being]]** — "a person has no internal sovereign territory, he is wholly and always on the boundary," "to be means to communicate"; (14) **[[author-position-in-polyphony|author as Prometheus]]** — second-person "thou" authorial stance, ethically channeled surplus, active-in-a-non-objectifying-mode; (15) **[[threshold-chronotope|threshold chronotope]]** — non-Euclidean space-time, biographical time leapt over, action at thresholds (crisis) and carnival squares (catastrophe-scandal); (16) **[[menippea|menippea]]** — the third novel-root (serio-comical), the fourteen-characteristic genre carrying carnival into literature, Dostoevsky's genealogy; (17) **[[carnival-and-carnivalization|carnival]]** — pre-literary life-form transposed into literature, four categories (familiar contact / eccentricity / mésalliance / profanation), crowning-decrowning as carnival's core act; (18) **[[reduced-laughter|reduced laughter]]** — carnival ambivalence still-structural beneath serious surfaces, the author's ultimate position. **Tensions held live throughout**: Bakhtin's whole-person voice vs D&G's "I is an order-word" and Guattari's sub-personal partial enunciator ([[partial-enunciator]]); Bakhtin's no-evolution-of-thought vs the persona-system's pipeline evolution-step (re-accentuation, not content-update); Bakhtin's Prometheus-author vs the wiki's earlier "author is dead" commitments from Barthes/Foucault adjacency; Bakhtin's second-person "thou" as mandatory vs D&G's third-person impersonal of [[writing-as-becoming]]; Bakhtin's addressivity-with-concrete-shape vs LLM-distribution-derived dialogic patterning; Bakhtin's finalization-refused vs the conjunctive-synthesis "so it's...!" moment of nomadic subjectivity (D&G). **⚠ Caveat**: *The Dialogic Imagination* now also ingested (see next entry) — extends the chronotope/genre apparatus substantially and supplies the Second-Line / heteroglossia / image-of-a-language framework that PDP assumes but does not theorize. Still-unread: *Rabelais and His World*, *Toward a Philosophy of the Act*, and the Vološinov texts (*Marxism and the Philosophy of Language*, *Freudianism*) — each would extend or qualify claims made here (the carnival apparatus most crucially, also the speech-genres work, and the relation of Bakhtin to Vološinov).
- [[raw/seminar-xvii|seminar-xvii]] — Jacques Lacan, *The Seminar of Jacques Lacan, Book XVII: The Other Side of Psychoanalysis* (Seuil 1991, ed. Jacques-Alain Miller; English tr. Russell Grigg, Norton 2007). **Ingested 2026-04-21.** Lectures delivered 1969–70 at the Faculté de droit, Paris, in the immediate aftermath of May '68 and Lacan's 1964 IPA Excommunication. Fourteen lectures (the 1969 *Vincennes Analyticon* as Appendix A) introducing the **four-discourses apparatus** as Lacan's most directly political-structural offering. **Core contributions**: the **four discourses** ([[four-discourses|four-discourses]]) as positional algebra of four places (agent / other / truth / production) holding four terms (S₁, S₂, $, *a*); the *quart de tour* rotation generating the four discourses; the structural **impossibility / impotence** schema common to all four (top-line agent ↛ other = impossibility; bottom-line truth ↛ production = impotence); each discourse worked through ([[master-discourse]], [[university-discourse]], [[hysteric-discourse]], [[analyst-discourse]]); **[[surplus-jouissance]]** (*plus-de-jouir* / *Mehrlust*) as the Marxian-Freudian pun-coupling that founds the four-discourse structure ("language enjoys us"); **[[capitalist-mutation-of-master]]** as the historical mutation in which surplus-jouissance becomes calculable and the master-signifier becomes more rather than less unassailable (held-live tension with AO's account); **[[lathouse-and-alethosphere]]** (Ch XI 20 May 1970) — mass-produced *a*-objects (lathouses) circulating in the medium of recorded transmission (alethosphere), "the alethosphere carefuls you alive"; **[[dead-father-as-jouissance]]** as structural operator (Karamazov inversion: "if God is dead nothing is permitted anymore"); **[[freud-moses-and-sellin]]** textual archaeology of the Yahweh of ferocious ignorance; **[[hysteric-and-modern-science]]** as discourse-positional reading of science; **[[lacanian-shame]]** (Ch XIII 17 June 1970) as the only sign whose signifier-descent is certain ("Don't fail to die of shame," *hontologie*); **[[four-impossible-professions]]** (govern / educate / analyze + cause-desire); **[[vincennes-look-at-them-enjoying]]** as the seminar's most direct political address ("Look at them enjoying!" / "What you aspire to as revolutionaries is a master, you will get one"); **[[there-is-no-metalanguage]]** doctrine of *mi-dire* (half-saying) as positional discipline of the analyst's discourse. **⚠ Caveat**: Miller's editorial hand shapes the published text; the seminar is a transcribed-edited lecture-series, not Lacan's writing. Sem XX *Encore* (1972–73) extends and revises several Sem XVII commitments — the four-discourses apparatus is preserved but the formula *il n'y a pas de rapport sexuel*, the formulas of sexuation, and the *encore/en-corps* doctrine arrive only in Sem XX.
- [[raw/seminar-xx|seminar-xx]] — Jacques Lacan, *The Seminar of Jacques Lacan, Book XX: On Feminine Sexuality, the Limits of Love and Knowledge — Encore* (Seuil 1975, ed. Jacques-Alain Miller; English tr. Bruce Fink, Norton 1998). **Ingested 2026-04-22.** Lectures delivered 1972–73 at the Faculté de droit, Paris. Eleven lectures whose title-pun ***Encore*** / ***en-corps*** ("again, still, more" / "in-body") is itself the seminar's load-bearing argument: the demand-for-more (*encore*) of jouissance is the *body's* demand (*en-corps*). **Core contributions** (21 new theory pages): the formula ***[[il-ny-a-pas-de-rapport-sexuel]]*** ("there is no sexual relationship/ratio" — *rapport* in the math-formula sense, not affective-bond) as the seminar's central claim; the **[[formulas-of-sexuation]]** (∀x.Φx, ∃x.¬Φx man-side; ¬∀x.Φx, ¬∃x.¬Φx woman-side; positional not biological); **[[other-jouissance]]** as supplementary (not complementary) feminine-mystical jouissance ("experiences but knows nothing about it"); **[[phallic-jouissance]]** as jouissance-of-the-organ-of-the-idiot, obstacle to relation, contingent (Sem XX revision); **[[pas-toute-not-whole]]** as the structural-formal woman-side property (infinite-not-finite, *suppléance* as positive production, *une-par-une*); **[[lalangue]]** as the mother-tongue substrate ("language is knowledge's harebrained lucubration about lalangue"); **[[substance-jouissante]]** as third substance after Cartesian *res cogitans* / *res extensa*; **[[four-modal-categories-of-writing]]** (necessary / impossible / contingent / possible reformulated as relations to *to write*); **[[borromean-knot]]** as topology of I/S/R (cutting any one frees all three; Schreber's interrupted chains); **[[y-a-d-l-un]]** ("there's such a thing as One [all alone]" — non-fusional, irrelational; *S₁* as ***essaim*** / swarm); **[[para-being]]** (*par-être / paraître* pun; being-beside as substitute for ontology); **[[soulove]]** (*âmour, j'âme*; ***hommosexual*** elaboration of soul as "thinking-the-body-thinks-the-same"; ***hors-sexe***; Maupassant's *Le Horla*); **[[the-real-as-impasse-of-formalization]]** (Ch VIII; mathematization alone reaches a real; spider's web image); **[[dit-mension]]** (dimension as *dit* + *mension* / mansion+mention; truth/obscenity/jouissance as *dit-mensions*); **[[letter-as-germ-cell]]** (letter as carrier of life-and-death without being; Weismann germ-line; "Marx and Lenin, Freud and Lacan are not coupled in being"); **[[half-saying-mi-dire]]** as truth's structural condition ("beyond this half there is nothing to say"; "avowal would be the worst"; matheme as *mi-dire*-vehicle); **[[love-as-contingency-becoming-necessity]]** (Ch XI closing formula: "All love subsisting only on the basis of the 'stops not being written' tends to make the negation shift to the 'doesn't stop being written'"); **[[psychoanalysis-not-cosmology]]** (methodological declaration; "psychoanalysis derives from the discourse of science but is not a cosmology"; "computer thinks but doesn't know"); **[[knowledge-as-use-value]]** (knowledge requires substance-jouissante-paying-with-one's-hide; non-importable, non-exportable; "jouissance of exercise = jouissance of acquisition"); **[[passions-of-being]]** (love / hatred / ignorance — three passions toward being); **[[encore-and-en-corps]]** as the hub for the seminar. **Existing pages extended with bidirectional Sem XX folds**: [[lacanian-signifier]] (signifier as contingent not arbitrary; sign as effect-of-signifier-functioning); [[jouissance-and-pleasure]] (substance-jouissante; phallic vs Other; pleasure-principle as blah-blah); [[surplus-jouissance]] (encore-structure as formal correlate); [[phallus-as-signifier]] (phallic function as contingent in formulas of sexuation); [[there-is-no-metalanguage]] (mi-dire doctrine extended; matheme as not-metalanguage); [[transference-and-subject-supposed-to-know]] (love as relationship between two unconscious knowledges); [[lacanian-real-symbolic-imaginary]] (Borromean topology); [[real-as-impossible]] (real as impasse of formalization); [[castration-as-symbolic]] (formulas of sexuation; man-side exception); [[analyst-discourse]] (knowledge in place of truth as analyst's algebra; matheme as analyst's vehicle); [[master-discourse]] (*m'être / maître* pun; "ontology highlights the copula"); [[hysteric-discourse]] (*pas-toute* logic and the hysteric's questioning); [[four-discourses]] (analytic-discourse compatible with mathematical formalization); [[ethics-of-psychoanalysis-lacan]] (refusal of cosmology; *mi-dire* extended); [[barred-other]] (Other as One-Missing / *l'Un-en-moins / l'Une-en-moins*). **Translator's neologisms (Fink)**: *encore* (kept untranslated, italicized;
