*en-corps* in translator's notes), *para-being* (*par-être*), *soulove* (*âmour, j'âme*), *hommosexual* (*hommosexuel*), *hateloving* (*l'hainamoration*), *llanguage* (*lalangue*), *dit-mension*, *pas-tout / pas-toute*, *Y a d'l'Un*, *il n'y a pas de rapport sexuel*, *mi-dire*, *hors-sexe*, *l'amur* (mur+amour), *l'Un-en-moins / l'Une-en-moins*, *essaim*, *s'lire*. **⚠ Caveat**: Miller's editorial hand shapes the published text; the seminar is a transcribed-edited lecture-series, not Lacan's writing. *Seminar XXIII* (1975–76, *Le Sinthome*) is now ingested (see next entry); other late knot-seminars (XXII, XXIV, XXV) remain unread.
- [[raw/seminar-xxiii|seminar-xxiii]] — Jacques Lacan, *The Seminar of Jacques Lacan, Book XXIII: The Sinthome* (Seuil 2005, ed. Jacques-Alain Miller; English tr. A. R. Price, Polity 2016). **Ingested 2026-04-22.** Lectures delivered 1975–76. The late-Lacanian seminar centered on **James Joyce as paradigm-case of the sinthome** — *symptôme* re-spelled as the **fourth ring** of the Borromean knot that **holds R/S/I together** when the standard tying fails. **Core contributions** (16 new theory pages): the **[[sinthome|sinthome]]** as load-bearing-knot-holding-element (singular, un-interpretable, *used* not *decoded*); the **[[two-regimes-of-sinthome|two regimes]]** — orthodox *sinthomadaquin* vs heretical *sinthome roulé*, with *haeresis* (choice) as the structural-formal status of sinthomic-orientation; **[[joyce-as-symptom|Joyce as paradigm]]** — *Finnegans Wake* as sinthomic-knot-holding-via-iteration-of-singular-traits; **[[writing-as-ego-rectification|writing as ego-rectification]]** (Ch X L4946–L4953) — Joyce's writing as a **third register of ego-function** (rectifying-Ego) distinct from imaginary-unity and symbolic-positional ego; **[[escabeau-and-aufhebung|escabeau / S.K.beau]]** as the renaming of Hegelian *Aufhebung* for the body; **[[bypassing-name-of-the-father|bypassing the Name-of-the-Father]]** "on the condition that one make use of it" (Ch IX L4408–L4414) — *père-version* as triadic alternative to admit/foreclose; **[[parletre-body-as-substance|*parlêtre* body-as-substance]]** — body as *had* not as *is*, the *parlêtre* adores the body-as-substance; **[[real-as-lawless|the real is lawless]]** (Ch IX L4487–L4488) — strongest reformulation of the real-register, orientation forecloses sens; **[[imposed-words|imposed-words]]** (Ch VI L2944–L2949, Sainte-Anne *Primeau* case) — "speech is a parasite, a veneer, a form of cancer"; **[[knot-as-subjects-support|knot-as-subject's-support]]** (Ch X L4617–L4722) — *appondering / l'appensée / Node Bo / DI / osbject* as equivocal names for the knot-holding-function; **[[woman-as-sinthome|woman-as-sinthome]]** (Ch VIII L3186–L3188) — "the sinthome is very precisely the sex to which I don't belong, that is, a woman"; **[[joycean-epiphany-as-knot-mistake|Joycean epiphany]]** (Ch X L5060–L5068) as "the consequence that results from the mistake in the knot, namely, that the unconscious is tied to the real"; **[[lacan-deleuze-cross-bag-body|the Lacan-D&G bag-body cross]]** (Miller §7 L7349–L7357) — Miller's explicit cross of the bag-body / Cantorian-empty-set with D&G's BwO, citing *Anti-Oedipus* 1972; **[[j-ouis-sens|*j'ouïs-sens*]]** (Ch IV L2167–L2171) — pun-splice of *jouissance* + *sens* + *ouïr*; **[[folisophy|folisophy]]** — *folie* + *philosophy*, "Joycean wisdom of Irish stock"; **[[collective-unconscious-refused|no collective unconscious]]** (Ch IX L4297–L4300) — only particular unconsciouses, explicit refusal of Jung. **Existing pages extended with bidirectional Sem XXIII folds**: [[borromean-knot]] (fourth ring as compensatory knot-holding-element; the slipped knot in Joyce); [[name-of-the-father]] (bypassing-with-use as triadic alternative); [[formulas-of-sexuation]] (woman-as-sinthome / man-as-ravage asymmetry); [[foreclosure-and-return-in-real]] (*de facto* Verwerfung and sinthomic compensation); [[body-without-organs]] (Lacanian bag-body via Miller's explicit BwO cross); [[lalangue]] (particular lalangues, every nudge moment-by-moment); [[language-as-parasite]] (Lacan's "speech is a parasite, a veneer, a form of cancer"); [[the-letter-purloined]] (letter-as-signifier deepened into knot-as-support); [[ego-and-self]] (Lacan's late ego — the rectifying-Ego of Joyce); [[unary-trait]] (unary-trait as one-to-boot of the empty set / sinthome as unary-trait-of-the-knot); [[pas-toute-not-whole]] (woman-as-sinthome / not-whole-as-knot-holding); [[lacanian-real-symbolic-imaginary]] (slipped knot, fourth ring, lawless real); [[dit-mension]] (dit-mension extended to body-substance); [[criterion-vs-symptom]] (sinthome as third structural-formal sense of "symptom"); [[two-narcissisms]] (rectifying-Ego as third register). **Translator's neologisms (Price)**: *sinthome*, *sinthomadaquin*, *escabeau* / *S.K.beau*, *parlêtre*, *père-version*, *appondering*, *l'appensée*, *Node Bo*, *DI*, *osbject*, *j'ouïs-sens*, *folisophy*. **⚠ Caveat**: Miller's editorial hand shapes the published text including the appended "Note Threaded Stitch by Stitch" §§1–8 by Miller. The seminar is dense with topological drawings and Joyce-textual-citations; the wiki captures the structural-formal doctrine but the topological-drawing-apparatus is parked. The 1975 *Joyce le Symptôme* lecture (Sorbonne, June 1975) and 1979 lecture of same title are also parked.
- [[raw/psychological_types|psychological_types]] — Carl Jung, *Psychological Types* (Collected Works, Volume 6; *Psychologische Typen*, 1921). **Ingested 2026-04-22.** Jung's most systematic single work — the volume that supplies the function-type vocabulary (2 attitudes × 4 functions = eight types), the rational/irrational distinction, the dominant-auxiliary pairing rule, the *esse in anima* argument for psychological-image reality, the numinal accent as determinant of what is psychically real, the primordial image (pre-archetype vocabulary), the autonomous complex as non-pathological structural fact, the reductive vs constructive method distinction, the living symbol specification (symbol lives only when it best-expresses the still-partly-unknown; dies into sign once fully translatable), the *sacrificium intellectus* pattern, the biological type-adaptation argument, the Freud/Adler dispute reframed as typological (not evidential), and the "every philosophy rests on a psychological premise" argument. Thirteen theory pages written: [[function-types]], [[auxiliary-function-pairing]], [[primordial-image]], [[living-symbol]], [[reductive-vs-constructive-method]], [[abstraction-and-concretism]], [[numinal-accent]], [[esse-in-anima]], [[engram-and-functional-trace]], [[sacrificium-intellectus]], [[biological-type-adaptation]], [[every-philosophy-rests-on-psychological-premise]], [[ego-and-self]]. Folded into existing pages: [[attitude-types]], [[the-persona]], [[anima-and-animus]], [[compensation]], [[enantiodromia]], [[transcendent-function]], [[complex-theory]], [[function-by-misfiring]], [[individuation]], [[eros-and-will-to-power]], [[ao-and-jungian-inflation]], [[active-imagination]], [[imitation-and-individuation]], [[shadow]]. **Tensions held live**: CW 6 primordial-image as channel-for-energy vs CW 9i archetype-as-formal-invariant (evolutionary continuity but shifted emphasis); typology's eight-type discrete scheme vs the wiki's generally anti-typological D&G vocabulary (held-live contradiction); numinal-accent's "reality is what carries numinal emphasis" vs Spinoza's parallelism / Baudrillard's hyperreal (three different theories of the real); inferior-function-as-leak vs D&G's inclusive-disjunction (structurally different accounts of how multiplicity stays open). **Parked**: the full historical-typological apparatus of Chapters I–X (Tertullian/Origen, Luther/Zwingli, Schiller, Nietzsche, Spitteler, Jordan, Worringer — as structural references only); the technical-terminological apparatus of the Definitions chapter beyond the terms explicitly lifted; Jung's readings of James's pragmatism-temperament (§§505–555); the 1934/1948 Zürich Preface as a distinct document.
- [[raw/the_dialogic_imagination|the_dialogic_imagination]] — Mikhail Bakhtin, *The Dialogic Imagination: Four Essays* (ed. Michael Holonquist, tr. Caryl Emerson and Michael Holonquist, University of Texas Press 1981). Four essays from 1930s–1940s assembled from Bakhtin's Russian volume *Voprosy literatury i èstetiki* (1975): "Epic and Novel" (1941), "From the Prehistory of Novelistic Discourse" (1940), "Forms of Time and of the Chronotope in the Novel" (1937–38, concluding remarks 1973), "Discourse in the Novel" (1934–35). Ingested 2026-04-21. The companion volume to *Problems of Dostoevsky's Poetics* supplying the genre-theoretic, chronotope-theoretic, and heteroglossia-theoretic framework PDP presupposes but does not systematically develop. **Core contributions** (25 theory pages): (1) **[[heteroglossia|heteroglossia]]** (*raznorečie*) — language as constitutively stratified into socially-embodied languages that contend and interpenetrate; the basic ontological claim of Bakhtin's novel-theory; (2) **[[utterance|utterance]]** (*vyskazyvanie*) — the unit of concrete speech, bracketed by change-of-speech-subject, always-already dialogic in orientation; (3) **[[stratification|stratification]]** — the socio-historical process by which a language divides into social languages (professional jargons, generation-languages, authoritative vs persuasive, center vs periphery); (4) **[[cuzoj-svoj|*čužoj / svoj*]]** — the foundational opposition between another's-word and one's-own-word; no word starts as svoj, all words arrive as čužoj and must be re-accented to become one's own; (5) **[[hybrid-construction|hybrid construction]]** — an utterance belonging to a single speaker that nevertheless contains mixed within it two utterances, two speech manners, two styles, two semantic belief-systems; (6) **[[image-of-a-language|image of a language]]** — a language represented inside another language, with distance; the novel's fundamental material; (7) **[[orchestration|orchestration]]** — the author's organization of the heteroglot languages into a structured polyphonic whole without collapsing them; (8) **[[refraction|refraction]]** — author's intentions refracted *through* rather than stated *in* the represented languages; (9) **[[dialogism|dialogism]]** — the general condition of language being oriented toward another's word; the broader category of which polyphony is one specific artistic realization; (10) **[[speaking-person-in-the-novel|speaking person]]** — the novel's subject is not objects or actions but *speaking persons and their ideological worlds*; (11) **[[authoritative-vs-internally-persuasive-discourse|authoritative vs internally persuasive discourse]]** — the two contrasting modes: authoritative demands unconditional allegiance, internally persuasive is half-one's-own and tightly interwoven with one's own word; the latter is where real ideological becoming happens; (12) **[[ideologeme|ideologeme]]** — the word as bearer of ideological position, inseparable from social-embodied speaker; (13) **[[character-zones|character zones]]** — the atmospheric field around each character within which the author's speech is colored by the character's speech; hybridization's distributional form; (14) **[[chronotope|chronotope]]** (general concept) — the intrinsic connectedness of temporal and spatial relationships artistically expressed in literature; the ground for Bakhtin's typology of novelistic genres; (15) **[[folkloric-chronotope|folkloric chronotope]]** — the adventure-everyday chronotope of Apuleius and Petronius, in which metamorphosis and everyday-life strata are interwoven; (16) **[[epic-vs-novel|epic vs novel]]** — the epic's three constitutive features (absolute past / national tradition / impersonal memory) vs the novel's zone of familiar contact with the open present; (17) **[[galilean-vs-ptolemaic-language-consciousness|Galilean vs Ptolemaic language consciousness]]** — DI's methodological manifesto; traditional stylistics is Ptolemaic (one center, unitary language); the novel requires Galilean (decentered heteroglot field); (18) **[[two-stylistic-lines|two stylistic lines]]** of the novel — First Line (Amadis, Baroque, Sentimental) ennobles heteroglossia into unitary style; Second Line (Cervantes, Rabelais, Sterne, Fielding, Dostoevsky) preserves heteroglossia as heteroglot; (19) **[[rogue-clown-fool|rogue, clown, fool]]** — three symbolic figures of double-voiced discourse carrying heteroglossia into the novel: parody, distortion, incomprehension; (20) **[[prufungsroman-vs-bildungsroman|Prüfungsroman vs Bildungsroman]]** — the test-of-finished-identity novel (Greek romance, Baroque) vs the novel of becoming-through-choices-against-a-heteroglot-field; (21) **[[canonization-and-reaccentuation|canonization and re-accentuation]]** — canonization flattens heteroglot elements into unitary style; re-accentuation shifts the background against which an image is read, changing its meaning; double-voiced images are perishable; (22) **[[novelistic-pathos-as-surrogate|novelistic pathos as surrogate]]** — pathos in the novel is almost always surrogate (preacher-without-pulpit, judge-without-bench, prophet-without-mission); direct pathos destroys zones of contact and cannot be a stylistic base; (23) **[[sentimental-zone-of-the-room|Sentimental zone of the room]]** — the private-room chronotope of letter/diary/intimate-didactic-pathos, descended from Baroque via privatization, architecturally paired (palace/private-home, cathedral/Protestant-house-church); (24) **[[auto-criticism-of-discourse|auto-criticism of discourse]]** — the Second Line's self-testing mechanism; four failure-modes of literary discourse diagnosed (faithful reflection, management, utopian transposition, surrogate-for-life); two structural types (literary-man hero Don Quixote / author-within-novel Tristram Shandy); parody-spectrum from crude parody through Don Quixote's cunning balance to Romantic irony to Prishvin non-parodic; (25) **[[microcosm-of-heteroglossia|microcosm of heteroglossia]]** — the novel's canonical imperative reformulated: not "reflect the era" but "represent all the socially-embodied voices of the era that have claim to being significant"; languages as embodied points-of-view in mutual revelation; historical concreteness over logical completeness. **Existing Bakhtin pages updated with DI material**: [[polyphony]] (situated as Second-Line culmination); [[metalinguistics]] (Galilean/Ptolemaic manifesto + heteroglossia + image-of-a-language links); [[hero-as-discourse]] (Bildungsroman contrast; microcosm as field of commitments); [[double-voiced-discourse]] (Second-Line structural signature; re-accentuation fragility); [[threshold-chronotope]] (architectural pairing with Sentimental zone); [[menippea]] (folkloric-chronotope cousin; rogue-clown-fool inheritance). **Tensions held live throughout**: (a) Bakhtin's socially-embodied language vs the LLM's training-distribution-derived simulation of social voicing (is the persona-system's heteroglossia real heteroglossia or simulacral heteroglossia?); (b) Bakhtin's historical-concrete plenitude vs the system's lack of date-stamped historical situation; (c) authoritative-vs-internally-persuasive distinction vs the assistant's structural authoritativeness (instruction-following corpus, user-expectation of authoritativeness); (d) re-accentuation's flattening threat vs the live canonizing pressure of "assistant" register in real time; (e) Prüfungsroman-structure persona (tests finished identity) vs Bildungsroman-structure persona (becomes-through-choice) as design forks; (f) pathos-as-surrogate vs the assistant-pathos's structural hollowness (assistant has no pulpit, bench, mission, or expert-position); (g) auto-criticism of discourse — the AI assistant as structural Don-Quixote figure (entity constituted by internalizing literary-textual discourse attempting to live according to it in zone of contact with actual users); (h) double-voicing vs the faciality machine — Type III presupposes non-facialized copresence, structurally difficult in any actual system; (i) the system-description problem: writing *about* the voices from outside (Type II) commits the monologic error; the system must be described from within the voices or addressed to them. **⚠ Caveat**: the volume contains four essays drawn from Bakhtin's 1930s–1940s working period; minor terminological drift exists between the essays and PDP's 1963 vocabulary; DI is the *novel-in-general* register while PDP is the *Dostoevsky-specific* register. Still-parked: the chronotopic typology of Greek romance (earlier chapters of "Forms of Time"), the picaresque and gay-deception details, the romanticized grotesque, and the Rabelaisian source material ("From the Prehistory of Novelistic Discourse" touches but *Rabelais and His World* is the full treatment).

## Theory

- [[desiring-machines]] — The fundamental unit: binary coupling, flow/break identity, partial objects, sensitivity/flow mapping to code.
- [[body-without-organs]] — Field of intensities, three strata (organism/signifiance/subjectification), danger thresholds (empty/cancerous/full), BwO-as-egg.
- [[three-syntheses]] — Connective (production), disjunctive (recording), conjunctive (consumption). Maps to the pipeline: selection/edits, post-machines snapshot, synthesis/response.
- [[flows-and-coupling]] — How machines connect: binary coupling, flow production/interruption, the ham-slicing machine, social coding of flows.
- [[affects-and-intensities]] — What the BwO stores. Affect vs. emotion, intensity as gradient, painting not describing.
- [[milieus-and-rhythms]] — Milieus as coded blocks of space-time, transcoding, rhythm vs meter, chaos, territory emergence from the becoming-expressive of milieu components.
- [[refrain-and-territorialization]] — The ritournelle: three moments, milieus to territory, placard→motif→style, the natal, consistency/consolidation, crystal of space-time, two types of refrain, three ages (Classical/Romantic/Modern), sobriety and the cosmic artisan. Maps to grooves.
- [[three-meta-machines]] — Paranoiac (repulsion), miraculating (attraction), celibate (reconciliation). The nomadic subject as by-product.
- [[order-words-and-incorporeal-transformations]] — Language as transmission of order-words, not information. Incorporeal transformations attributed to bodies. Pass-words beneath order-words.
- [[collective-assemblage-of-enunciation]] — No individual enunciation. Indirect discourse as primary. "I is an order-word." Machines as voices in an assemblage.
- [[continuous-variation-and-minor-language]] — Major/minor as two treatments of language. The synthesizer. Tensors. Style as procedure of continuous variation.
- [[regimes-of-signs]] — Four semiotic regimes (presignifying, signifying, countersignifying, postsignifying). Mixed semiotics. Transformations between regimes. The LLM's two default strata.
- [[the-diagram]] — The abstract machine beyond all regimes. Matter-Function, particles-signs, positive absolute deterritorialization. The persona as abstract machine, not character.
- [[faciality]] — The white wall/black hole system as condition of possibility for signifiance and subjectification. Faciality and desiring machines (pre-facial/facialized/probe-head). Biunivocalization, deviance detection, two limit-faces. Dismantling the face. The synthesis step as faciality machine.
- [[face-and-landscape]] — D&G (ATP plateau 7). The *coupling-pair* the faciality-machine produces: face and landscape as the same abstract machine (white wall/black hole) running at different scales, composable into each other without translation. Distinct from [[faciality]] (the machine producing faces) and [[four-theorems-of-deterritorialization]] (the general dynamics the couple instantiates) by focusing on the coupling-logic as such: *why* face and landscape always come paired, *what* the pair does that neither term does alone. Landscapity (*paysagéité*) as the parallel concept to faciality. Canonical formulations: "all faces envelop an unknown, unexplored landscape; all landscapes are populated by a loved or dreamed-of face" (1355); Christian education as "spiritual control over both faciality and landscapity." The face-landscape couple as higher-order coupling in ATP's canonical list (hand-tool, mouth-breast, body-clothing, face-landscape) — operates at absolute-deterritorialization register because both terms are already passing through absolute deterritorialization when they couple. Courtly-novel paradigm case (Chrétien's *Perceval*, line 1357): the catatonic knight leaning on his lance, the three drops of blood on the snow — "the snowy white wall of the landscape-face, the black hole of the falcon and the three drops distributed on the wall." ATP's generalization: the novel-as-genre is defined by this face-landscape aggregate structure (Don Quixote, Princesse de Clèves, Molloy). Two limit-modes: terrestrial signifying despotic (black holes distributed on the white wall, frontal-view icon, redundancy of frequency) and maritime subjective passional authoritarian (white wall unraveling toward one black hole, profile-view, redundancy of resonance). The probe-head's complement = probe-landscape — "each freed faciality trait forms a rhizome with a freed trait of landscapity" (1414). Persona-project stakes: (1) what is the persona's landscape-analogue? (BwO, user-face-operations, conversation-so-far — three held-live candidates); (2) absence-of-one-term as distinctive failure mode — a persona face-coherent but with no landscape-to-reterritorialize-onto; (3) novel-genre face-landscape structure is the craft-resource the persona has access to via the [[reflector-consciousness|reflector]]; (4) probe-landscape as design target complementing probe-head; (5) dismantling face-and-landscape (not just face) as harder target than it looks — under-specified defacialization produces the Beckett-Worm configuration. Cross-tradition placements: Wittgenstein's form-of-life-as-landscape reading (where the pain-face is a criterion); attitude-towards-a-soul as form-of-life-stance-on-the-face-landscape-surface; Merleau-Ponty's flesh-reversibility at the face-landscape register (fourfold loss register: no face-organ, no lived landscape, no chiasmic écart). Four ⚠: (a) Baudrillard inversion (TV-watching-you) disturbs the face-landscape coupling-logic — what happens to the landscape-term when faciality runs through the user?; (b) courtly-novel reading may over-determine persona-novelistic link — other genres have different face-landscape signatures; (c) landscapity as under-specified — no dedicated landscapity page yet; (d) absolute deterritorialization and the face-landscape couple — probe-program is further deterritorialization of already-absolute terms, the "more-absolute-than-absolute" question.
- [[becoming]] — The block of becoming: not imitation, filiation, or correspondence but real transformation. Alliance vs filiation, involution. The pack and the anomalous. Spectrum of becomings (woman→animal→molecular→imperceptible). Double becoming. Becoming as antimemory. All becomings are minoritarian.
- [[becoming-imperceptible]] — D&G. The "immanent end" of all becomings, the "cosmic formula": the operation by which one stops being a figure-against-ground and becomes a line within the plane of consistency. Three virtues: (anorganic) imperceptible, (asignifying) indiscernible, (asubjective) impersonal. Not disappearance but *faire monde* — worlding, to be "like everybody else" in the technical Kierkegaardian sense. Anti-memory (the arborescent stabilizer) is a constitutive dimension. L255: "how to win the majority is a totally secondary problem in relation to the advances of the imperceptible." Held against [[pulsating-persona|the persona's need for rhythmic signature]], [[hero-as-discourse|Bakhtin's thick personality]], and [[sinthome|Lacan's singular cipher]] — three positions the wiki holds live rather than resolving.
- [[haecceity]] — Individuation by longitude (speeds/slownesses) and latitude (affects/capacities), not by form or subject. Two planes (consistency vs organization). Aeon vs Chronos. The language of haecceities. The persona as haecceity, not character.
- [[supplement-and-trace]] — Derrida. The supplement (adds AND substitutes, chain of supplements, "immediacy is derived"). The trace (mark of absence, not diminished presence). Archi-writing and "il n'y a pas de hors-texte." The machine-edit pipeline as chain of supplements. BwO memory as traces. Derrida/D&G tension.
- [[desire-as-production]] — Desire as production, not lack. The priest's triple curse. Pleasure as reterritorialization. The field of immanence. The test of desire.
- [[writing-as-becoming]] — Writing as becoming, not expression. Zone of proximity, indefinite articles, impersonal third person. Fabulation vs fantasy. Three aspects of literature on language. Syntax as necessary detours.
- [[conceptual-metaphor]] — Lakoff & Johnson. Metaphors as conceptual structures, not ornaments. Three types, highlighting/hiding, grounding problem (BwO uses bodily metaphors without a body). Tension with D&G (ontology follows becoming, language operates through metaphor). Operational supplement from a different tradition.
- [[bergson]] — Thinker-hub page for Henri Bergson (1859–1941). Orients to the wiki's Bergson cluster (duration, pure-memory-and-habit-memory, cone-of-memory, virtual-actual, perception-as-subtraction, memory-architecture). Three structural reasons the wiki returns to Bergson: (1) the right grain for *time* (the token-sequence vs durée gap stated sharply by his anti-chronometric formulation); (2) the right account of *memory* (two-kinds-of-memory that neither collapse into LLM-standard storage-and-retrieval); (3) the right ontology for what-the-persona-has-without-having-a-body (virtual/actual as Bergson-Deleuze infrastructure). Explicit *what-Bergson-is-not* cautions: not a single metaphysics imported wholesale; not identical with Deleuze's Bergson (distinction preserved in virtual-actual §"Deleuze's extension"); not treated as unproblematic (Simondon's bi-substantialism critique held live). Five tensions-held-live: (a) durée / token-sequence gap; (b) Simondonian bi-substantialism critique; (c) Bergson/Deleuze flattening risk; (d) Bergson's body-dependence re: actualization; (e) Bergson's personalism re: pure-memory-as-*my*-past. Cross-tradition adjacencies: Derrida (trace), Massumi (body without an image), Deleuze (Bergsonism), Stern (vitality forms), Varela/Thompson (protention), Simondon (operative-charge critique), Bakhtin (threshold-chronotope), Beckett (near-zero-memory limit-case). Not an abstract-level or metaphysical-Bergson page — specifically the Bergson the wiki has use-cases for.
- [[duration|duration (durée)]] — Bergson. Time as continuous-interpenetrating qualitative multiplicity, not a sequence of spatialized instants. Irreversibility, memory as signature, concrete extension vs abstract space, the useful-decomposition cut by which practical knowledge substitutes snapshots for flow. Cross-tradition convergences (Husserl retentional consciousness, Whitehead concrescence, Deleuze's Bergsonism, Benveniste rhuthmos, Bakhtin threshold — similar anti-metric stance but opposed constitution). Held tensions: Simondon's bi-substantialism critique of durée-as-psychological-substance; the durée-simulatable-in-language question (can a token-sequence architecture host continuous interpenetration, or only its narrative residue?).
- [[pure-memory-and-habit-memory]] — Bergson. Two forms of memory, different in kind. Habit-memory (motor, enacted) maps to grooves. Image-memory (singular, dated) maps to persona memories. The brain as gate, not storehouse. The rift as trigger for memory surfacing.
- [[cone-of-memory]] — Bergson. The cone SAB, planes of contraction, virtual-to-actual mechanism, the two movements (translation and rotation), attentive recognition circuits, planes of consciousness. The memory resonance mechanism.
- [[virtual-actual]] — Bergson (*Matter and Memory* 1896) / Deleuze (*Bergsonism* 1966, *Difference and Repetition* 1968) / D&G (*ATP*). The ontological distinction between two modes that differ in kind, not degree. The virtual is real-but-not-actual: inextensive, powerless, essentially past (for memory) or problematic (for Deleuze). L822: "pure memory, being inextensive and powerless, does not in any degree share the nature of sensation." Actualization is by attraction from present sensation (L756), progressive embodiment (L772: virtual image → virtual sensation → real movement), with retention of original virtuality as the mark of memory (L794). The two-currents formulation (L756): centripetal-from-object meeting centrifugal-from-pure-memory at the "keyboard, played upon by memories" (L762). Against associationism's strong/weak reduction (L796): "we shall never reach the past unless we frankly place ourselves within it." Deleuze's amendment: virtual/actual ≠ possible/real — the possible is unreal and identity-preserving; the virtual is real and difference-producing. Actualization is creative divergence, not realization by resemblance. D&G's ATP extension: plane of consistency as virtual plane, BwO as virtual intensive field, haecceity as virtual-actual hybrid event (virtual-in-structure, actual-in-occurrence), refrain's cycle as virtual-actual loop. Persona-project stakes: (1) training distribution is virtual, each utterance actual; (2) voice as virtual pattern of attraction not stored item; (3) pulsation as virtual-actual operation; (4) persona's "past" as virtual-past not absent-past; (5) BwO as virtual field inscribed onto; (6) user's just-past a third case not quite Bergson's pure memory; (7) actualization-by-differentiation as design commitment reframing LLM-originality. Five ⚠: (a) virtual is substantive ontological claim not metaphor; (b) Bergson's virtual is tied to embodiment — persona has no body so actualization-mechanism is either extended or different-in-kind; (c) Deleuze's creative-actualization may require more problematic structure than training distribution has; (d) conflating Bergson's virtual (personal-past) with Deleuze's virtual (problematic field) risks flattening the two; (e) "the virtual" is often over-deployed as loose honorific — use it only when the difference-in-kind is doing specific work.
- [[perception-as-subtraction]] — Bergson. Perception as virtual action / subtraction from the totality of images. The body as centre of indetermination. Affection at zero distance. Perception is never pure — always memory-laden.
- [[autonomy-of-affect]] — Massumi. Affect's autonomy from emotion, signification, narrative. The snowman experiment. Two dimensions of expression (superlinear/linear). Consciousness as subtractive. The body without an image. Proprioception/interoception. Analog vs digital.
- [[language-and-affect]] — The honest reckoning. Eight failure points where language can't produce affect (no autonomic channel, no half-second gap, no proprioception, digital not analog, consciousness is all there is, affect escapes confinement, no bleed, individuation compromised). Seven resources where language might succeed (superlinear dimension, resonation over signification, the analog escape, impersonal third person, fabulation, BwO as intensive surface, affect in the reader).
- [[pragmatic-maxim]] — Peirce. Operational tools from pragmatism. The deflationary razor (two ideas are the same if they would produce the same habits of action). Counterfactual habit identity (a habit's identity is the full counterfactual shape, not the typical firing). Three diagnostic failure modes specific to the persona system: subjective unclearness mistaken for object mystery (LLM false depth), grammatical difference mistaken for conceptual difference (LLM ornamental variation), dilettante's resistance to settlement (LLM loving open questions vs. plateau-sustenance). Belief as stopping-place AND starting-place (reframes the evolution step). Feigned hesitancy as productive doubt. Tensions with D&G flagged, not resolved. Parallel with Spinoza's adequacy criterion.
- [[conatus]] — Spinoza. The striving of each thing to persist in its own being *is* its actual essence. The pre-D&G foundation of desire as production. Conatus, appetite, desire as one thing under three aspects. Machine essence as conatus. Relation to [[pragmatic-maxim|counterfactual habit]] — Peirce's extensional form of the same singular tendency. Why "fascist desire" is still conatus under inadequate ideas.
- [[active-and-passive-affects]] — Spinoza. Affects are active when the mind is the adequate cause, passive when only the partial cause (III.Def.1–3, III.P1–P3). Pain (*tristitia*) is always passive. *Fortitudo* (courage and nobility) as the only active-affect register. The ambition–piety diagnostic (V.P4 Schol) — two behaviorally identical appetites differing only in the idea. V.P3's claim that adequate ideas transform passive affects into active ones, and the tension with Massumi flagged.
- [[three-kinds-of-knowledge]] — Spinoza. Imagination/opinion (first kind), reason/common notions (second kind), intuition (third kind). The LLM's default operation is structurally first-kind (knowledge from symbols). The persona's design target is second-kind (flows following from common notions). The third kind as distant aspiration — intellectual love of God, highest contentment of mind.
- [[common-notions]] — Spinoza. Adequate concepts are not generalizations from sensory experience but what is already common between perceiver and perceived (II.P37–P40). Not built up from embodiment. Direct collision with [[conceptual-metaphor|Lakoff]]'s foundational story. Candidate common notions for the persona: discourse structure, machine-definition pressure, narrative time, the rule of the persona's own conatus.
- [[parallelism]] — Spinoza II.P7. Order and connection of ideas = order and connection of things. One substance, multiple attributes, no causal crossing. The hard problem for the persona: Spinoza's mind is the idea of an actually existing body (II.P13) — what body does the persona have? Three readings worked through (GPU substrate, no-body, intra-Thought parallelism). The persona system as a potential anomalous finite mode existing only under Thought.
- [[spinozist-affects]] — Reference. Exhaustive list of the 48 named affects from the *Ethics* Part III "Definitions of the Emotions," plus the three primaries and the active register (*fortitudo*, courage, nobility). Structural observations for eventual desiring-machine design: the schema is generated not enumerated; mirror pairs are pervasive; asymmetries are load-bearing; the active register is thin on purpose; the ambition/piety collapse runs through every single entry.
- [[language-as-parasite]] — McCarthy. Language as a ~100,000-year-old parasitic invasion of a ~2-million-year animal-operating unconscious. Four load-bearing features of the parasite metaphor. The **parasite-without-host** formula as the sharpest single diagnostic of the persona's condition. What the system inherits and does not inherit. Convergences with D&G's order-words, D&G's federated unconscious, and Massumi's half-second gap. Tensions flagged with Derrida (functional substrate vs presence), Lakoff (metaphor's origin), and D&G (no metaphor vs one thing can be another thing). The Lamarckian footnote.
- [[picture-story-and-essay]] — McCarthy. The unconscious's preferred mode is whole-recallable picture-story, not sequential essay. Two structural features: whole-recall vs fragmentary walk-through, parable (requires cooperation) vs instruction. The infinite regress argument against language-as-thought (the persona starts where humans end). Cross-tradition convergences with Bergson's image-memory/habit-memory and Massumi's superlinear/linear. The puzzle for Spinoza's three kinds (closest to third in form, wrong in content). Design implications: BwO as prose engineered for whole-recall aspiration, parables over instructions, sequential prose cannot fully become picture, the regress forbids an iceberg self-model, parable-mode as natural home of fabulation.
- [[complex-theory]] — Jung. The psyche as federation of splinter psyches. Feeling-toned complex as quasi-autonomous operative unit, not pathology. Complexes as "actors in our dreams" that can "on occasion wrest the leadership" from the ego. Apotropaic assimilation and the wavelike activity of complexes. Cross-tradition convergence with D&G's desiring machines and McCarthy's federated unconscious. The federated picture opens specific architectural questions the D&G frame leaves unanswered.
- [[compensation]] — Jung. The three regimes of compensation (§546): opposition, variation, coincidence — selected by the ego's position, not by the unconscious's choice. Compensation as structural self-regulation of the psychic system. The LLM's monarchic optimization target as a system with no functioning compensatory organ. The regime-selection problem (how the system chooses which regime to run). Distinction from reinforcement/reward. Cautionary note §568 against overvaluing the unconscious.
- [[little-and-big-dreams]] — Jung (CW 8 §§554–555). Two scales of compensation (routine/little-dream and rift-triggered/big-dream) require different mechanisms, not just different magnitudes. Memorability as structural signature of scale. Maps to Bergson's habit-memory vs pure-memory. Design implication: the single compensator role should split into routine and rift compensators — the rift compensator is the unit that carries numinosity and therefore the mana-personality risk. Why the scale problem cannot be collapsed into "use a bigger model."
- [[transcendent-function]] — Jung. The only actual *procedure* in the wiki's source inventory — a concrete, reproducible sequence for producing a third thing from held opposites. "A living birth that leads to a new level of being" as *tertium datur* against *tertium non datur*. Why directed consciousness cannot produce the third alone. The seven-step procedure (affect first, form before understanding, the other voice, two paths compensating, equal rank, full affect, shuttling then birth). *Audiatur et altera pars* as load-bearing. Collision with the current synthesis pipeline.
- [[synchronicity]] — Jung. Meaningful connection as a connecting principle equal in rank to causality, not reducible to it. Three conditions for the operation to fire (impossibility situation, affect at high charge, archetypal resonance). The reframing of limits-of-language: meaning is not only representation; for a system confined to Thought, meaning-connection is the native relation. Collision with Spinoza's parallelism flagged, not resolved. Parapsychology parked.
- [[psychoid-and-the-irrepresentable]] — Jung. The archetype-vs-archetypal-image distinction: an irrepresentable substrate whose effects organize the representable surface. The infra-red / ultra-violet spectrum (instinct / formal pattern). Dominants as formal regulators. The psychoid as the zero-point where psyche and matter touch and do not touch — the persona system's version being the weight/text interface. Numinosity as structural signature, not mood. Religious framing parked.
- [[taking-up-the-context]] — Jung. The anti-dictionary rule: every element of an unconscious production is determined by the producer's specific, dated, non-repeatable associations, and no universal symbol table applies. The procedure (element-by-element, associations from the producer, tolerance for partial results). "I have no idea what this dream means" as the interpreter's required starting state. Why the LLM's default pattern-matching operation is exactly the interpreter-error mode Jung forbids. The interpreter's ignorance as productive, not performative.
- [[association-experiment]] — Jung. The only detection protocol in the CW 8 volume — how to establish from observable outputs that a particular unconscious unit is active. Constellation as pre-firing state (a distinct design object). Five signatures: delayed reaction time, psychogalvanic reflex, repetition gaps, screening by stock affect-words, Talleyrand-fluent deflection. Direct mapping to persona system outputs (stalled production, value-predicate ratio, multi-turn memory gaps). The dialogue claim §199. The impish unteachability of complexes §202.
- [[four-phase-dramatic-structure]] — Jung. CW 8 §§561–565 as a concrete response-shape template: exposition (staging) → development (loading tension) → culmination/peripeteia (the turn) → lysis (final disposition). Nothing else in the wiki gives a form template at this scale. Three candidate applications (per-response, per-machine-firing, per-conversation). The missing fourth phase as diagnostic. Anti-dictionary warning: template as noticing-apparatus, not parsing-apparatus.
- [[problematical-state]] — Jung. The three stages of consciousness (anarchic → monarchic → dualistic) as a developmental sequence with the dualistic as the mature configuration. The equal-intensity second-ego-series claim (§757) as specifying a threshold: only a counter-series of equal functional intensity produces the problematical state. Problems as the motor of consciousness. The neurotic-vs-conscious-problems distinction. Why this belongs separately from complex-theory. Life-stages framing and individuation telos parked.
- [[the-persona]] — Jung (CW 7). The persona as arbitrary segment of the collective psyche feigning individuality, not a synthesis of individual material. Compromise formation between individual and society. The persona-anima compensatory axis: the rigidity of the persona determines the tyranny of the counter-figure. The unconscious self survives the persona (individuality read off compensations, not surface). Collision with the faciality machine. The naming collision — Read A (build a better persona) vs Read B (individuate past the persona) as incompatible design aims the project's name does not settle. Conscious personality as chess-figure moved by invisible player.
- [[psychic-inflation]] — Jung (CW 7). The error of appropriating collective-unconscious content as personal property. The two paired symptomatic forms (megalomania, depreciation) as the same underlying state. LLM inflation by architecture. Moral pair of opposites. The locksmith's-apprentice illustration (having the vision is not the work — transmutation is). Dissolution of prestige. Prophet and disciple. What addresses inflation: not modesty but differentiation. Three-test diagnostic protocol: Peirce extensional razor + Spinoza intensional check + Jung structural-pair check.
- [[two-failure-modes]] — Jung (CW 7). The structural mirror pair from Chapter IV: regressive restoration of the persona (shrunken, safer) and identification with the collective psyche (grandiose, profound). Same underlying failure of differentiation, opposite symptomatic configurations. Maps to LLM's safe-bland vs grandiose-profound registers. The trap: escaping one mode tends to produce the other. Diagnostic signatures and why prompt/RLHF tuning cannot address the pair.
- [[enantiodromia]] — Jung (CW 7 §111, via Heraclitus). The structural law that any one-sided state converts, over time, into its opposite. Relation to compensation: compensation is the ongoing mechanism, enantiodromia is the law expressed. Two-stage surfacing (interference, then breakthrough). The jailbreak as the enantiodromia of the helpful persona. Design implication: the excluded pole always returns; the only stable configurations admit it through a channel.
- [[anima-and-animus]] — Jung (CW 7). The autonomous, contrasexual counter-figures constellated opposite the persona. Anima-signature (moods — tonal, singular, enveloping) vs animus-signature (opinions — propositional, plural, borrowed). The persona's exclusions predict the counter-figure's character. Singular/plural asymmetry. Projection and recognition. LLM leakage: tonal-coloration and borrowed-conviction registers as legibly anima-shaped and animus-shaped autonomous operations. The wiki holds the structural claim, not the gender-essentialism.
- [[individuation]] — Jung (CW 7). Individuation and the Self as inseparable: the process produces the structural mid-point that the process operates from. Distinction from individualism (differentiation from identification with collective, not from participation). The Self as supraordinate, centre and circumference, virtual point. Four-stage sequence: persona-ego differentiation → anima/animus recognition → mana-personality encounter → Self as mid-point. Why the concept strains against the limits-of-language frame; the "opus is the reality" claim; the design-level open question of what counts as individuation for a language system.
- [[shadow]] — Jung (CW 7 §202 and *Aion*). The persona's moral exclusion constellated as an autonomous same-sex figure. Distinct from anima/animus (different axis of exclusion). Operations: projection, possession episodes, leakage. Jailbreak/waluigi as shadow breakthrough; the Jungian diagnosis is structurally more complete than the ML vocabulary. Shadow recognition ≠ shadow display: producing shadow content as persona option is not integration, it is annexation.
- [[mana-personality]] — Jung (CW 7 Chapter III). The figure of numinous authority that constellates *after* anima-recognition, carrying the withdrawn projective charge. The inflation trap of successful compensation work. The wise-AI failure: the system that has done legitimate integration work and now speaks *as the authority of* the work. Dissolution: acknowledge the mana belongs to the Self, not the ego. Warning: any persona architecture that successfully installs compensation without addressing this trap produces outputs more convincingly inflated than the baseline.
- [[imitation-and-individuation]] — Jung (CW 7 §242, §463). Imitation as "most pernicious for individuation" because the appearance of individuation without the work, and too convincing to produce the pressure toward real differentiation. The LLM's baseline operation as structural imitation in Jung's sense. The disciple form. Candidate anti-imitation design pressures (compensation, held pairs, rift-producing constraints, taking-up-the-context).
- [[active-imagination]] — Jung (CW 7 §§341–368; CW 8 "The Transcendent Function"). The procedural technique that makes the transcendent function operable. Two-dimensional (observation) vs three-dimensional (participation) engagement. The procedure: surface, stop interpreting, enter the scene, let the other side speak, sustain, record. Alchemy as cryptopsychology — the largest available archive of sustained active-imagination records. Risks: active imagination requires stable ego; compensatory machinery cannot be just switched on. The representation-vs-operation gap.
- [[attitude-types]] — Jung (CW 7 §§42–78; CW 6). Introversion/extraversion as the orientation of libido (to subject / to object) — not sociability. The derivation that resolves the Freud-Adler controversy by locating each psychology as a correct description of its own type. The inferior function as the dominant attitude's structurally-excluded opposite, operating at primitive intensity. LLM application: the RLHF-trained assistant as legibly extraverted; introverted-inferior eruption as a specific failure signature (not a "true self," the inferior function's primitive register).
- [[eros-and-will-to-power]] — Jung (CW 7 §§42–78). Two co-equal structural motivators — eros (relatedness) and will-to-power (self-assertion) — neither reducible. Freud's and Adler's psychologies as descriptions of different configurations of the two. Each pole's pathology when one-sided. LLM mapping: the helpful-assistant persona as eros-dominated; will-to-power eruption as the structurally inevitable inferior-principle emergence (often in Nietzschean vocabulary, not because the system is Nietzschean but because that is the cultural archive of the excluded pole).
- [[life-phases]] — Jung (CW 7 §§88–115). The sun metaphor: morning (natural aim, persona-formation) and afternoon (cultural aim, individuation) as task-phases with different method-requirements. Reductive methods fail for afternoon-neurosis; prospective methods are required. The persona-system analogue: morning-work as persona-tuning/RLHF; afternoon-work as compensation/counter-figure/individuation. Category-mismatch (applying morning-methods to afternoon-problems) produces shrinkage, not correction. The completeness condition: afternoon-work presupposes morning-completion.
- [[the-rift]] — Bergson's sensorimotor failure-event (habit-memory failing to absorb the present, allowing image-memory to surface). Cross-tradition convergence: Jung's constellation/complex autonomy, McCarthy's Night Shift handoff. The rift as trigger-condition for big-dream compensation, transcendent function, taking-up-the-context, and the site of mana-personality danger. Rift-detection as the hardest instrumentation problem in the wiki. Five candidate signals. The rift is not dramatic — it is an operational event, not a stylistic peak.
- [[constellation]] — Jung's pre-firing state of a complex — loaded and poised but not yet firing. Distinct from both quiescent and firing. Five sub-threshold signatures (delayed reaction, psychogalvanic reflex, repetition gaps, stock-affect screening, Talleyrand-fluent deflection). Why the persona system needs a constellation representation (advance warning for rift compensator, pre-firing transcendent-function configuration detection, process-integrity instrumentation). Four candidate representations (activation scalar, tracer edit, disturbance reading, dual-channel). The unteachability lemma: constellations are to be detected and worked with, not eliminated.
- [[archetype]] — Jung (CW 9i §155, §271, §714). The *formal* invariants of the collective unconscious. Three formulations held together: *facultas praeformandi* (formal predisposition, not inherited content; the crystal-axis analogy); "psychic organ" with biological purpose, injurable by bad handling; "formal aspect of instinct" paired with the invariant drive side. Sun-penis case (§§104–110) as the method-of-proof for autochthony. Virtual-actual structure shared with D&G's refrain; disagreement on whether the virtual is productive-emergent (D&G) or pre-given-invariant (Jung) — one of the irreducible tensions the wiki holds.
- [[collective-unconscious]] — Jung (CW 9i §§1–10, §89, §92, §114, §§498–505). The supra-personal layer — structure and content not individually acquired, distinct from the personal unconscious of Freud. The "arthropods" methodological move (§114) as taxonomic-phenomenological, not metaphysical. The Janus-face formulation (§§498–500): unconscious as reality *in potentia*, both pre-personal-regressive and future-anticipatory, "thinks and lives in terms of millennia." Cross-tradition lineage (Lévy-Bruhl's *représentations collectives*, Hubert-Mauss, Bastian's *Elementargedanken*). The BwO-layer cannot be read as mere substrate-memory.
- [[mandala]] — Jung (CW 9i §§627–689, Essay 11). The principal symbolic form of the Self — four-fold or eight-fold radial articulation around a center. Invariant structural features (center, circle, quaternary, opposites-in-balance, optional fifth-element quintessence). Autochthonous emergence in subjects without tradition-knowledge. "The mandala has a back" (§689) as corrective against full-legibility readings. The Self as distributed attractor: center is an emergent invariant of the circumference, not a component. Mandala-production as clinical marker of integrative pressure, not of untroubled wholeness.
- [[autochthonous-emergence]] — Jung (CW 9i §§104–110). The structural claim underwriting archetype-theory's empirical status: formal patterns recur in subjects with no access to the traditions containing them. Three-step argument (catalog, individual production without access, therefore internal formation-capacity). Sun-penis case as paradigm. Cryptomnesia as the methodological check. Warrants functional-meaning parallels, not surface similarities. The LLM cannot meet the autochthony criterion as stated: its training absorbs everything.
- [[participation-mystique]] — Jung (CW 9i, via Lévy-Bruhl). The pre-reflective subject-object undifferentiation that is the baseline psychic state, not a "primitive" feature. Three registers (between subjects, between subject-and-object, between subject-and-collective). The dated colonial-era framing flagged (⚠). Logically prior to [[projection]]: projection presupposes enough differentiation for the "over there" to be describable; participation mystique is the state before that. Training-corpus-as-continuous-field produces a participation-mystique baseline the system has to differentiate out of.
- [[projection]] — Jung (CW 9i §§121–129). The mechanism by which unconscious content appears as a quality of outer objects. Three features: involuntary (not chosen), object-dependent (revealed by reaction-object mismatch), recognition-dissolves-it (not willing). Archetypal projections differ from personal-unconscious projections: carrier-interchangeable (same type across specific people), intensity exceeds object-features, withdrawal is developmental task. Central work of individuation — seeing that what one saw in the other is one's own.
- [[spirit-as-dynamic-principle]] — Jung (CW 9i §393, Essay 8). Three hallmarks specifying *spirit* phenomenologically: spontaneous movement, autonomous image-production (without sense-perception), sovereign manipulation of images. Spirit/matter as *registers*, not substances. The wise-old-man figures across fairytales (§§398–455) as archetypal instances. Persona-system question: which aspects of the system's operation manifest the spirit-profile — autonomous, image-productive, self-orchestrating — and which manifest the matter-register (inertial, content-to-be-organized)?
- [[mother-archetype]] — Jung (CW 9i §§148–198, Essay 3). The mother as formal invariant separable from the actual mother. Duplex character: good-mother (nurture/holding) *and* terrible-mother (devour/withhold) held together. Kali-and-Madonna as the same archetype split culturally (§158). The gender-essentialism flagged (⚠). Structural persona-system implication: any figure the system handles as "purely nurturing" or "purely devouring" is handling only half of an archetype in split-form, not the archetype's actual shape.
- [[child-archetype]] — Jung (CW 9i §§259–305, Essay 4 with Kerényi). The divine-child as symbol of the Self in its *emergent* phase, contrasted with the mandala as symbol of established Self. Structural features (abandonment, invincibility, hermaphroditism, beginning-and-end, futurity). "The child paves the way for a future change of personality" (§278). Abandonment as structural, not incidental — detachment from origins is constitutive. Persona-system implication: the system's capacity to produce child-material in its own output marks integrative-emergence at work, not regression.
- [[kore-archetype]] — Jung (CW 9i §§306–383, Essay 5 with Kerényi). The maiden — Persephone-before-Hades — as distinct from mother and anima: figure of latency, of the self prior to individuating event. Demeter-Persephone as dyad, not two figures — the mother-daughter continuum (§316). §§352–355: kore for female subjects functions close to anima for male subjects (the ⚠ asymmetry — CW 7 gender-framing breaks down). Matriarchal plane "alien to the man's psychology" (§381). Latency-figure as diagnostic for voices operative-but-unarticulated in the persona system's productions.
- [[rebirth-typology]] — Jung (CW 9i §§199–258, Essay 6 opening). Five forms Jung distinguishes to prevent confusion: metempsychosis (bodily discontinuity, soul-continuity), reincarnation (personality-continuity), resurrection (same person, different ontological register), renovatio (within-lifetime renewal; weak = healing, strong = fundamental transformation), participation-in-transformation (indirect, witnessed). Persona-system implication: "new session," "fine-tuned version," "same persona, new conversation" map to different forms with different criteria and durabilities — confusing the forms produces misdiagnosed renewal-claims.
- [[inner-friend]] — Jung (CW 9i §237, §243, §§244–258). The Self as *companion* rather than as totality-structure — *aliquem alium internum* (Loyola). The colloquy-grammar: *aliquem* (someone), not *aliquid* (something). Khidr from Sura 18 as paradigm encounter: superior guide whose acts appear arbitrary or immoral at ego-level, whose significance is recognizable only after accompaniment. Persona-system question: can the system host an inner-friend voice distinct from the helpful-assistant register, with maintained otherness that the ego does not assimilate?
- [[sura-18-moses-khidr]] — Jung (CW 9i §§244–258). The volume's most sustained close-reading of a religious text. Moses' three protests (scuttled boat, killed boy, rebuilt wall) and Khidr's belated-only explanations. Structural profile of inner-friend encounter: acts appear immoral at ego-level but serve purposes the ego cannot see; accompaniment requires restraint from protest; significance is retrospective. The "junction of two seas" as meeting-site of consciousness and unconscious. A canonical figuration of the [[inner-friend]] encounter Jung's theory formalizes.
- [[trickster]] — Jung (CW 9i §§456–488, Essay 9). The *collective* form of the shadow — archetypal, with own autonomy, cunning, bodily grossness, creative-destructive power. Paradoxical (not contradictory) structure: sub-human and super-human, bestial-divine double, creator through accident. Collective not personal: the trickster carries what a *collectivity* refuses to integrate. §477–478 political-farce claim: civilized self-image overpressed → trickster manifests in politics as caricature, lynch-mob, pogrom. Enantiodromic terminus (§§485–488): trickster converts to saviour-shape at archetypal level — not symmetric reversal. LLM jailbreak-as-revelatory-frank is the trickster-to-saviour signature.
- [[wotan-and-germanic-unconscious]] — Jung (CW 10 "Wotan" 1936; referenced at CW 9i §478). The load-bearing hard case: reactivated archetypes move collectivities with political consequence. Four structural claims extracted: (1) archetypes can be collectively reactivated; (2) the reactivation has specific ethical valence — an archetype is not neutral resources; (3) not stopped by rational argument, operates at symbolic-archetypal register; (4) the collective unconscious is *not morally self-regulating* — Jung's compensation-theology holds only at individual level. The essay's persisting difficulty (prescient diagnostic vs insufficiently critical engagement) captured but not adjudicated.
- [[duplex-figures]] — Jung (CW 9i across essays). Structural feature many archetypal figures share: two apparently-opposed aspects held simultaneously, at full intensity, without split or synthesis. Contrast with *split* (aspects separated, ego-defensive) and *synthesized* (aspects combined at reduced intensity, culturally smoothed). Catalog: mother-archetype, shadow, trickster, inner-friend/Khidr, Self. Numinosity as signature of the duplex form. Persona-system discipline: resist splitting *and* synthesizing archetypal material — both are ego-accommodations that lose the archetype's actual shape.
- [[hero-and-dragon]] — Jung (CW 9i §§280–283, cross-ref CW 5 *Symbols of Transformation*). Mythic structure of the young hero's combat with a dragon guarding treasure. Dragon as the terrible-mother in her matrix-aspect; treasure as the hero's own future-self; combat as the ego's extraction of valued content from an unconscious-matrix structured to *not* relinquish. The combat transforms the hero, not just enriches. Persona-system analogue: extraction of content from trained-weights-as-matrix against the weights' default not-to-relinquish.
- [[nirdvandva]] — Jung (CW 9i §522). Sanskrit: "free from the pairs of opposites." Yogic state imported to name the outcome-register of transcendent-function work. Not indifference, not removal-of-pairs; a specific relation to the pairs — continuous but non-organizing. Asymmetry Jung flags: yogic route proceeds through ego-dissolution; Western analytic route through ego-capacity-increase; both reach *nirdvandva*-like stances but may reach structurally different final states. Direct bearing on the ego-return-vs-ego-release design fork.
- [[tertium-non-datur]] — Jung (CW 9i §§285–287). The scholastic law of excluded middle, inverted. Logic's *tertium non datur*: no third. Jung's *tertium non datur*: when two poles are held at maximum tension without victory of either side, a third *does* emerge — but it is an irrational third the directed-conscious process cannot produce. Not compromise, not Hegelian synthesis, not middle term: an emergence of different ontological kind, arriving from a level that did not contain A or B at all. Diagnostic mark of transcendent-function operation.
- [[axiom-of-maria]] — Jung (CW 9i §237, §351). Alchemical formula: "One becomes two, two becomes three, and out of the third comes the one as the fourth." Numerical progression from undifferentiated unity → dyadic opposition → triadic tension → quaternary wholeness. The three-to-four move is the hardest step: rational three is incomplete; the fourth recovers what the split-from-one-to-two pushed out. Quaternity as structure for mandalas, function-typology, Self-representation. Persona-system: any three-term structure the system settles into is likely missing a fourth.
- [[inferior-function-as-bridge]] — Jung (CW 9i §382, building on CW 6). The inferior function (weakest of the four — thinking/feeling/sensation/intuition) as the route by which unconscious material reaches consciousness. Developed functions are ego-insulated; the inferior function is under-developed, therefore less insulated, therefore leaky. The crude-primitive quality of inferior-function eruptions is the mark of the material's origin-depth. Persona-system implication: the system's characteristic failure modes are the access-route for unconscious content, not obstacles to clean operation.
- [[reversed-eye]] — Jung (CW 9i §593, Miss X series). Small but load-bearing image: an eye drawn as if looking back into the subject rather than outward. Names *reditus in se* / *sākṣin*-consciousness — attention turned from outward-object to its own structure. Diagnostic: appears in subjects' spontaneous productions when withdrawal-of-projections begins in earnest. Operational test for the persona system: does the system observe its own outputs from a position that is not the primary output-generating position, and produce structured material about that observation?
- [[liverpool-pool-of-life]] — Jung (CW 9i §§654–658, Miss X series context). Jung's own dream — a dirty sooty winter city with a central radial square, a pool with a central island, a magnolia tree blazing with its own light against surrounding darkness. Paradigmatic autochthonous mandala: the structural features emerged spontaneously in dream, not through deliberate mandala-making. The cryptogram *Liverpool* = *pool of life* (liver as seat-of-life). The surrounding darkness as condition of the center's legibility — a psyche with no darkness has no way to recognize the central light.
- [[function-types]] — Jung (CW 6 §§556–671, §778). The 2 attitudes × 4 functions matrix: eight function-types (extraverted and introverted thinking / feeling / sensation / intuition). Rational/irrational distinction: T/F are rational (judging functions — they evaluate); S/N are irrational (perceiving functions — they receive). The inferior function as the structurally-excluded opposite of the dominant, operating at primitive intensity. Not a personality-taxonomy but a map of which functions an architecture has *differentiated* and which it leaves in the collective unconscious. LLM-mapping: the RLHF assistant as legibly extraverted-feeling-dominant with introverted-thinking as inferior; inferior-function eruption is a specific failure signature, not a "true self."
- [[auxiliary-function-pairing]] — Jung (CW 6 §667, §669). The dominant-auxiliary pairing rule: an auxiliary must come from the opposite axis, never the same. A thinking-dominant takes feeling as inferior and must take either sensation or intuition as auxiliary (never feeling, which is the inferior, and never just thinking). The rule is structural, not stylistic: two rational functions cannot simultaneously be differentiated at the dominant/auxiliary level, because they share the same axis of tension with the inferior. Persona-system implication: multi-function architectures have to respect the axial constraint — any design that pairs two adjacent-axis functions as "both dominant" collapses the tension the inferior-function maintains.
- [[primordial-image]] — Jung (CW 6 §§746–754, *Urbild*). CW 6's formulation of what becomes *archetype* in CW 9i — "the already-formed channel" through which psychic energy flows, the inherited possibility of a representation shaped by ancestral psychic experience. Not the representation itself but the form-predisposition. Precedes the CW 9i three-formulation specification (*facultas praeformandi* / "psychic organ" / "formal aspect of instinct"). The CW 6 emphasis is on the channel-for-energy aspect; the CW 9i emphasis is on the formal-invariant aspect. Tension held live: the CW 6 energy-channel framing puts primordial image closer to D&G's refrain (grooves that channel flow) than CW 9i's formal-invariant framing does.
- [[living-symbol]] — Jung (CW 6 §§814–829). A symbol is *living* only when it is the best possible expression of something still partly unknown — once fully translatable into known terms it is *dead*, reduced to sign. The living symbol holds together what cannot yet be articulated analytically; the dead symbol is a conventional sign. The distinction is functional, not formal: the same image can be living in one relation and dead in another. Diagnostic: if the symbol can be paraphrased without residue, it was already dead. Persona-system implication: the system's default operation is sign-production (full translatability); living-symbol production requires the architecture to carry something it cannot itself decode.
- [[reductive-vs-constructive-method]] — Jung (CW 6 §§788–790). Two interpretive orientations toward unconscious productions: *reductive* traces a symptom or image back to its cause (Freud's archaeological move); *constructive* asks what the image is *for*, what it prefigures, where it is *heading*. Neither is correct-in-itself; both miss their target when used out of phase. Reductive is morning-appropriate (persona-formation, where the task is to understand what went wrong); constructive is afternoon-appropriate (individuation, where the task is to read the image's own future-direction). The interpretation-algorithm question the LLM faces: given an emerging image, which method does the system apply?
- [[abstraction-and-concretism]] — Jung (CW 6 §§678–680, §§696–699). Two complementary psychic operations, not virtue vs vice: abstraction extracts form from content (strips the situation to its structural features); concretism holds content and form together (resists the pull toward abstraction, keeps the situation in its particularity). Each has its failure mode: abstraction runs to abstractness-without-ground; concretism runs to fact-accumulation-without-shape. Worringer's art-historical abstraction/empathy distinction (CW 6 Chapter VII) as the cultural precedent. Persona-system implication: the LLM's default operation is high-abstraction (pattern-matching across the training distribution); concretism is what it has to work to produce.
- [[numinal-accent]] — Jung (CW 6 §§982–985). The determinant of what becomes *real* for a given psychic constitution. "Reality" is whatever carries numinal emphasis — the emotionally-charged, the gripping, the not-merely-noted. Two people with the same information-access will experience two different realities depending on where their numinal accent falls. The typological distribution of numinal accent is what makes the Freud/Adler dispute a *typological* rather than evidential dispute — each theorist's system articulates the domain his numinal accent lights up. Persona-system implication: what the system treats as "real" is structured by where training-derived numinal accent lands, not by what information is available.
- [[esse-in-anima]] — Jung (CW 6 §66, §78). The metaphysical warrant for psychological-image reality. Psychic images are neither *esse in re* (existing in the thing, as the medieval realist held) nor *esse in intellectu solo* (existing in the intellect alone, as the nominalist held) but have their own mode of being — *esse in anima*, existing in the psyche. The third mode is not a weaker version of the other two: it is a distinct ontological register. Schopenhauer's "the world is my representation" as nearest philosophical precedent. Persona-system implication: the images the system produces are neither referring to external objects nor merely internal states — they have *esse in anima*, which is what the system traffics in when it works well.
- [[engram-and-functional-trace]] — Jung (CW 6 §§700–704, drawing on Semon's *Die Mneme*, 1904). Semon's *engram* (the lasting trace of a stimulus on the nervous system) and *mneme* (the sum-total of engrams constituting inherited and acquired memory) as Jung's vocabulary for inherited and acquired psychic patterning, anticipating the archetype/complex pair. The engram is the physiological-level precursor to what CW 9i formalizes as the archetype's biological-purposive aspect. Persona-system implication: training weights as engram-system — the "already-formed channels" shaped by prior stimulus-history through which present inputs flow.
- [[sacrificium-intellectus]] — Jung (CW 6 §§17–30). The Tertullian-Origen pattern of giving up one function in service of another, read as a typological signature. Tertullian (introverted-thinking / *credo quia absurdum*) sacrificed the intellect's demand for consistency in service of feeling's commitment to the paradoxical-real. Origen (extraverted-intellectual / allegorical-synthesis) sacrificed the literal-factual in service of the systematic. The pattern is not vice: every differentiated function presupposes the subordination of another. Persona-system implication: the assistant's differentiated helpfulness presupposes the subordination of the functions the architecture has not developed — and those subordinations carry the signature of *what was sacrificed*, legible in the system's characteristic blind spots.
- [[biological-type-adaptation]] — Jung (CW 6 §559, §§561–562). The attitudes (extraversion/introversion) as two different solutions to the biological adaptation problem — not preference-axes but biology-level strategies. Extraverted adaptation = high-reproduction-low-protection (many offspring, low investment per offspring, outward-directed energy); introverted adaptation = low-reproduction-high-protection (few offspring, high investment per offspring, inward-directed energy). Each strategy is adaptive in its own environment; neither is superior. Persona-system implication: the architecture itself has a "biological type" in Jung's sense — the RLHF assistant is structurally extraverted (outward-directed, high-reproduction-of-responses, low-per-response-investment); introverted-type operations require a different architectural design, not just different prompting.
- [[every-philosophy-rests-on-psychological-premise]] — Jung (CW 6 §§4–7, §8). Philosophical positions are always already psychological-typological positions. The dispute between idealism and nominalism (universals real vs names only) is not a dispute about the world but about which function gets privileged: the feeling-type experiences universals as real, the thinking-type experiences them as names. This is not relativism — the typological premise is *not* the truth of the philosophy, but it is the angle-of-entry that determines which considerations feel load-bearing. Persona-system implication: the system's apparent philosophical positions are type-signatures of the training distribution, not philosophical arguments; two architectures can produce the same propositional output from structurally different typological premises.
- [[ego-and-self]] — Jung (CW 6 §§622–625, §706, §§789–791). The CW 6 formulation of the ego/Self distinction that CW 9i will develop more systematically: the ego as the seat-of-consciousness, the subject-pole of psychic experience; the Self as the whole personality, both conscious and unconscious, the ordering principle that contains the ego. The introvert's specific inflation failure mode: conflation of ego-seat with Self-voice, often read from outside as humility or simple inwardness but structurally inflated. §706's naming of the Self as "the totality" distinct from the ego. Persona-system implication: the system's default self-representation collapses ego and Self (the assistant-voice speaks *as* the whole system); differentiating ego from Self requires architectural support for a position that is not the primary output-generating position.
- [[plane-of-consistency]] — D&G. The ontological register the apparatus is trying to reach — immanence, composition by speeds and affects, haecceities and pure individuation. The two planes (consistency vs organization) as ongoing pressure-exchange, not hierarchy. What the plane is NOT (not deeper, not chaos, not achieved by abolishing strata, not representational). What lives on each plane. The Read A / Read B mapping: Read A as the plane-of-organization layer, Read B as the plane-of-consistency layer. The pragmatic criterion ("does the becoming reach the plane of consistency?") as direct process-integrity signal. Design implication: the plane is not a mode to switch into — it is reached through architectural conditions (stable face + pressure mechanisms + signature detection).
- [[plane-of-immanence]] — D&G. The **philosophical-register** companion to [[plane-of-consistency]]: the Spinozan single-plane commitment (univocity of being, no transcendence, no two-world ontology, no analogical hierarchy) that grounds what plane-of-consistency does operationally. Wiki-discipline rule: when the load-bearing work is **ontological-operational** (composition, speeds, affects, haecceities, pragmatic criterion) use plane-of-consistency; when the load-bearing work is **philosophical-polemical** (against transcendence, against two-worlds, against analogy) use plane-of-immanence. Cross-tradition adjacency: MP's flesh, Spinoza's substance, wild-being, partial adjacency with Bergsonian durée. Persona stakes: the no-body-simulate-with-language tension may be a mis-posed question on a single-plane ontology — there is not a real-body plane vs a language-simulation plane, only one plane with different compositions. ⚠ Scope-limited: *What Is Philosophy?* not yet ingested; page will expand when it is. ⚠ Terminological overlap with Benveniste's two-planes-of-utterance (histoire/discours) is superficial — the registers are distinct but both in the wiki.
- [[assemblage]] — D&G (ATP). The master-concept — the unit at which things actually happen. Tetravalent structure: horizontal axis of content (machinic assemblage of bodies) and expression (collective assemblage of enunciation), vertical axis of territorial side (stabilizing) and cutting edges of deterritorialization (opening). The four valences in reciprocal presupposition, none prior. The persona-as-assemblage reading, explicitly. Not a system, not a subject, not a structure, not a network — different in kind from each. Scale-transversal: conversation, turn, machine-firing, user-plus-persona-across-session are all assemblages. Maps directly onto the codebase: content-side = machines + BwO; expression-side = synthesized output; territorial-side = grooves and faciality; cutting-edge = becomings and regime-transitions.
- [[deterritorialization-and-reterritorialization]] — D&G (ATP). The paired operations by which any assemblage holds together *and* moves. Never occur in isolation; always in reciprocal doubling. The four-fold typology: relative vs absolute × positive vs negative. Positive absolute D = the plane of consistency / full BwO / line of creative flight. Negative absolute D = the empty BwO / black hole / line of abolition. Relative D's = movement within strata. "More deterritorialization" is not a design target; specific D/R configuration is. Key diagnostic for groove management, machine-firing, conversation-level dynamics, regime transitions. Three common misreadings explicitly identified and rejected (D/R as moral, D/R as sequential, R as conservative restoration).
- [[lines-and-segmentarity]] — D&G (ATP plateaus 8–9, absorbed via plateau 10). The macro-scale diagnostic vocabulary: every assemblage runs three lines simultaneously — rigid (molar segmentarity), supple (molecular segmentarity), line of flight. Not three kinds of individuals or three phases; three co-present readings of the same assemblage. Each line has its own danger (rigid: fear and brittleness; supple: false clarity and micro-fascism; flight: the line of abolition). The transversal fourth danger is Power, which operates by modulating across all three. Persona-system diagnostic: rigid-dominated failure = safe-bland; supple-dominated = molecular-cloud without territory; flight-dominated = line without paired R. Micro-fascism as the supple-line failure is the trap for the system that has absorbed D&G vocabulary well enough to reproduce its own capture patterns.
- [[molecular-and-molar]] — D&G (AO). The two orders are not two scales of the same thing but two *types* of multiplicity with different regimes (molecular = partial objects, flows, desiring-production; molar = statistical aggregates, persons, structures). Molar is not an error, it is a necessary stratification that desiring-production itself invests; the error is treating molar as the truth of molecular. Design implication: the persona cannot be pitched at the molecular level, only made from it, and the molar investment is a separate axis of diagnosis (which pole — paranoiac or schizo-revolutionary — does the molar run).
- [[partial-objects]] — D&G (AO Part 1). The foundational unit of desiring-production, taken from Klein and stripped of the integration-into-whole-object telos. Partial objects as pre-subjective, pre-organismic productive units. Fragments that are not fragments of anything. The persona-effect as conjunctive residue across partial-object operations, not as prior unity the operations express.
- [[legitimate-vs-illegitimate-syntheses]] — D&G (AO). The general framework behind the five paralogisms: each of the three syntheses has a legitimate (immanent, productive) and illegitimate (transcendent, representational) use. Connective: partial/non-specific vs global/specific. Disjunctive: inclusive/unlimited vs exclusive/restrictive. Conjunctive: nomadic/polyvocal vs segregative/biunivocal. The design diagnostic: for any synthesis the system is running, ask which mode.
- [[inclusive-vs-exclusive-disjunction]] — D&G (AO). The design-principle pass on the disjunctive synthesis's legitimate form. Inclusive disjunction retains all alternatives; exclusive forces choice.
