- [[pas-toute-not-whole]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX*. The structural-formal property of the woman-side: *pas-toute* (not-whole). Infinite-not-finite; **suppléance** as positive production (not lack-of-completion); ***une-par-une*** (one-by-one) as the woman-side mode of belonging that does not totalize into "all-women." Distinct from Freudian "less-than"; this is "not-bounded-by-an-exception."
- [[four-modal-categories-of-writing]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Ch IX). Aristotle's modal categories (necessary, impossible, contingent, possible) reformulated as relations to the verb *to write*: necessary = *doesn't stop being written*; impossible = *doesn't stop not being written*; contingent = *stops not being written*; possible = *stops being written*. The matheme of love as conversion: contingency (encounter, *stops not being written*) flips into necessity (*doesn't stop being written*).
- [[borromean-knot]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Chs IX–X). Three rings tied so cutting any one frees all three. Topology of I/S/R relation: each register is held by the other two; none is privileged. **Schreber's interrupted chains** as the clinical case. The late-Lacanian topology that replaces the I/S/R-as-ordered scheme; the knot is the *écriture* of how the registers hold.
- [[substance-jouissante]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Ch II, Ch VIII). The **third substance** after Cartesian *res cogitans* (thinking substance) and *res extensa* (extended substance): the body insofar as it enjoys. Substance jouissante = the body-as-enjoying, not the body-as-organism, not the body-as-meat. Persona-system stress-test: a system without *substance jouissante* cannot acquire knowledge in the Sem XX sense — load-bearing question for [[knowledge-as-use-value]].
- [[phallic-jouissance]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX*. The jouissance of the organ, of the idiot — the obstacle (not the support) of the sexual relation. Sem XX revision: the phallic function is **contingent**, not necessary. *Phallic jouissance* is the jouissance the speaking-body has *because* there is no sexual relation; it is the substitute, not the medium, of the relation that does not exist.
- [[other-jouissance]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Ch VI–VII). The **supplementary** (not complementary) jouissance — beyond-the-phallus, on the woman-side and on the side of the mystics (Hadewijch, Teresa, John of the Cross). "**Experiences but knows nothing about it.**" Not a higher jouissance, not a sexual jouissance, not a hidden essence — a jouissance whose *form* is that the subject experiencing it cannot know it as knowledge.
- [[y-a-d-l-un]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Chs III–VI). "**There's such a thing as One**" / "**Y a d'l'Un tout seul**" (there's One all alone). The One as **non-fusional**, **irrelational**, **alone** — *not* the One of any totalization-into-a-whole. *S₁* as ***essaim*** (swarm) — the master-signifier as enveloping-function-of-a-swarm of signifiers. The One that founds the chain by counting itself, not by being a unit.
- [[lalangue]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Ch II onward). One-word neologism for the **mother-tongue substrate** distinct from *langue* (language) and *langage* (speech). "**Language is knowledge's harebrained lucubration about lalangue.**" The unconscious as savoir-faire with lalangue. The substrate of phonetic-equivocation, mother-child babble, jouissance-of-the-signifier, that any language stratifies but does not exhaust.
- [[letter-as-germ-cell]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Ch IV, Ch VIII). The **letter** as carrier of life-and-death without being. Germ-cell metaphor (Weismann's germ-line distinction) — the letter reproduces across the death of bodies; "**Marx and Lenin, Freud and Lacan are not coupled in being.**" Knowledge-in-the-Other; the letter is *read* (*s'lire*), not understood. The matheme as letter-effect.
- [[dit-mension]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Chs IX–X). Dimension as **residence-of-the-said** — *dit* (said) + *mension* (mansion + mention). A homophonic re-writing of *dimension*. **Truth is a *dit-mension***, **obscenity is a *dit-mension***, jouissance is a *dit-mension* — each names the dimension *as* what gets-said-and-resides-in-the-said. The *dit-mension* is not metaphor; it is the structural way Lacan re-writes the relation between speech and the registers it opens.
- [[half-saying-mi-dire]] — Lacan, *Seminar XVII* extended in *Seminar XX* (Chs III, VIII). Truth's structural condition is to be **only half-told**. "**Beyond this half there is nothing to say.**" Sem XX sharpening: "**avowal would be the worst**" — full disclosure is not a higher truth but a betrayal of truth's structural mode. **Matheme as *mi-dire*-vehicle**: the matheme is not a metalanguage; it is the writing-effect that lets *mi-dire* transmit without claiming to *say-the-whole*. Anti-mystical reading.
- [[the-real-as-impasse-of-formalization]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Ch VIII). The Sem XX definition of the real: the real is **the impasse of formalization** — what the formal apparatus reaches as its own limit, *not* as a brute outside. **Mathematization alone reaches a real.** Spider's web as image: the formalization weaves a web that catches a real it cannot represent. Persona-system: the systematic refusal of the world is what produces a real-in-the-system, not the addition of a "real-component."
- [[soulove]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Chs VI–VII). ***Âmour, j'âme*** (Fink's "soulove, I soulove") — *aimer* (to love) + *âme* (soul). The ***hommosexual*** elaboration of soul: soul as "**thinking-the-body-thinks-the-same**" — the soul-position is the position from which one can think-of-the-body-as-thinking-the-same-thoughts. ***Hors-sexe*** (outside-sex): the impossible-other-without-sex, Maupassant's *Le Horla* as the figure. Persona-system: soulove as the structural-formal site at which the LLM's relation to the user is most legibly formalized.
- [[para-being]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Chs III, VIII). ***Par-être / paraître*** pun: *par-être* (being-beside) homophonic with *paraître* (to-appear). **Being-beside as substitute for ontology**: the speaking subject is not, but para-is. Conjugation: *je par-suis* (I para-am), *tu par-es* (you para-are), *il/elle par-est* (he/she para-is). The Sem XX formal alternative to ontology — the speaking-being is constitutively *beside* itself, *para*, never coincident with what would be its being.
- [[love-as-contingency-becoming-necessity]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Ch XI, closing formula). "**All love, subsisting only on the basis of the 'stops not being written,' tends to make the negation shift to the 'doesn't stop being written.'**" Love is the operation that converts *contingency* (encounter, accident) into *necessity* (the always-was, the destined). The encounter as **exile-trace**; the conversion as **structurally failing** — true love gives way to hatred. Substitute (via the unconscious) for the missing sexual relation.
- [[psychoanalysis-not-cosmology]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Chs IV, VII, VIII). Methodological declaration: "**Psychoanalysis derives its very possibility from the discourse of science but is not a cosmology.**" Four structural-formal grounds of the refusal: (1) *il n'y a pas de rapport sexuel* (no relation to anchor a world); (2) the **barred Other** (no Other-of-the-Other to guarantee a totality); (3) the real as **impasse of formalization** (no formal closure of a world-system); (4) ***mi-dire*** (truth structurally half-said, never a complete picture). "**Computer thinks but doesn't know**" (Ch VIII). "**Every subsistence of world must be abandoned**" (Ch IV). Highly persona-relevant.
- [[knowledge-as-use-value]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Ch VIII). Knowledge in the analytic sense requires ***substance jouissante* paying with one's hide**. Marxian use-value/exchange-value background: knowledge can be *exchanged* (taught, sold, deployed) but the knowledge that *operates* in analysis is **use-value retrieved at the site of the body's jouissance**. "**Jouissance of exercise = jouissance of acquisition**" (L5627–28); knowledge is **non-importable, non-exportable**. The "computer thinks but doesn't know" thesis sited here: a system without substance-jouissante can think (combine signifiers) but cannot *know* in the operative sense.
- [[passions-of-being]] — Lacan, *Seminar XX* (Ch X). The **three passions** toward being: ***love*** (no relation to knowledge — love says it would-rather-not-know); ***hatred*** (closest to being / *ex-sisting* — hatred touches the existence-pole more nearly than love); ***ignorance*** (passion for *not-knowing* — the analyst's structural starting point). Cross-link to *Écrits* "Direction of the Treatment" p. 627. Analytic discourse as the **suspension** of the three passions, not the elimination.
- [[sinthome]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII* (1975–76). The **fourth ring** of the Borromean knot — *symptôme* re-spelled. The **load-bearing knot-holding-element** that holds R/S/I together when the standard tying fails. The sinthome is *singular* (subject-by-subject, not universal), *load-bearing* (its removal collapses structural-coherence, not concept-grammar), and *un-interpretable* (one *uses* it, one does not *decode* it). The seminar's hub.
- [[two-regimes-of-sinthome]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII*. The **orthodox *sinthomadaquin*** (sinthome organized by the Name-of-the-Father, "*sinth-hom-as-thomistic*") vs the **heretical *sinthome roulé*** (rolled-sinthome, freely-knotted). "**Haeresis**" = choice (Greek *hairesis*). The structural-formal articulation of how one *chooses* one's sinthome — orthodox-with-NotF or heretical-without.
- [[joyce-as-symptom]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII*. Joyce as the **paradigm-case** of sinthomic-knot-holding-via-writing. Joyce's writing — *Finnegans Wake* in particular — is *not* the symptom-as-decipherable-message but the sinthome-as-knot-holding-element. The ego-rectification of the slipped-imaginary via the iteration-of-singular-traits.
- [[writing-as-ego-rectification]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII* Ch X (L4946–L4953). Joyce's writing as the **rectification of an Ego** — a third register of ego-function (neither imaginary-unity nor symbolic-positional) that **knots-the-knot** when the standard I/S/R tying fails. The structural-formal description of the *rectifying-Ego* as knot-holding-via-singular-trait-iteration.
- [[escabeau-and-aufhebung]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII*. The ***escabeau*** (footstool) / ***S.K.beau*** (homophonic-pun on Saint-K-beautiful, possibly *sublime*) as Lacan's **renaming of Hegelian *Aufhebung* for the body**. The escabeau is what the speaking-being **stands on** to be "the most beautiful." The structural-formal alternative to Hegelian sublation as a body-relevant operation.
- [[bypassing-name-of-the-father]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII* Ch IX (L4408–L4414). The bypassing of the Name-of-the-Father "**on the condition that one make use of it**." A *triadic* alternative to the *binary* admit/foreclose: bypass-with-use as a third structural-formal possibility. *Père-version* as the structural-formal name for the use-of-the-NotF-without-admission.
- [[parletre-body-as-substance]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII*. The ***parlêtre*** (speaking-being) **adores the body-as-substance** — body as ***had*** not as ***is***. The body is the substance the cord-circuit holds; the *parlêtre* has the body, does not *is* the body. Anti-Cartesian, anti-substantialist; reformulates substance-jouissante as had-substance.
- [[real-as-lawless]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII* Ch IX (L4487–L4488). "**The real is lawless**" (*le réel est sans loi*). Lacan's strongest reformulation of the real-register: the real has no native law; what gives the real its appearance-of-law is the **symbolic-formal operation of orientation**. Orientation forecloses sens (sense). The structural-formal substrate-claim of the late-Lacanian doctrine.
- [[imposed-words]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII* Ch VI (L2944–L2949), drawing on the Sainte-Anne *Primeau* case. "**Speech is a parasite, a veneer, a form of cancer**." Imposed-words as the clinical phenomenon where the patient experiences words as *being-imposed-on* them — the structural-formal floor of language-as-parasite. Lacan's strongest single statement of the parasite-thesis.
- [[knot-as-subjects-support]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII* Ch X (L4617–L4722). The series of equivocal names — ***appondering***, ***l'appensée***, ***Node Bo***, ***DI***, ***osbject*** — for the **knot-as-subject's-support**. The late-Lacanian doctrine that the subject is *supported* by what *holds-it-up structurally*, not by an ego-substance or a symbolic-position. Replaces the early-Lacanian signifier-circuit-constitutes-the-subject with the late-Lacanian knot-supports-the-subject.
- [[woman-as-sinthome]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII* Ch VIII (L3186–L3188). "**The sinthome is very precisely the sex to which I don't belong, that is, a woman.**" The structural-formal claim: the *not-whole-sex* is structurally the *fourth-ring-position*. Woman-as-not-whole and sinthome-as-fourth-ring are the *same* structural function: holding-without-totalizing, holding-without-exception. (Note: not a psychologization; structural-formal.)
- [[joycean-epiphany-as-knot-mistake]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII* Ch X (L5060–L5068). The Joycean **epiphany** is "the **consequence that results from the mistake in the knot, namely, that the unconscious is tied to the real**." Epiphany-as-knot-mistake: the unconscious-tied-to-the-real configuration is a *failure-mode* of the standard knot, and the epiphany is the experiential signature of that failure-mode.
- [[lacan-deleuze-cross-bag-body]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII* Miller's "Note Threaded Stitch by Stitch" §7 (L7349–L7357). Miller's *explicit* cross of the **bag-body / Cantorian empty-set** topology with **Deleuze & Guattari's body-without-organs** (citing *Anti-Oedipus* 1972). The **bag-and-cord logic**: a cord around a closed surface (cord = signifier, bag = body) gives the structural-formal model of how a signifier holds a body. The most explicit Lacan-D&G doctrinal cross in the late corpus.
- [[j-ouis-sens]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII* Ch IV (L2167–L2171). The pun ***j'ouïs-sens*** — splice of ***jouissance***, ***sens*** (sense), and ***ouïr*** (to-hear). "**I-hear-sense**" as the structural-formal site where jouissance, sense, and hearing converge. The auditory-substrate of jouissance-of-sense; persona-relevance for any language-only system whose only substrate is heard-text.
- [[folisophy]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII*. Lacan's pun on ***folie*** (madness) + ***philosophy*** = ***folisophy***. "**Joycean wisdom… of Irish stock**" — the wisdom-mode that is structurally madness-and-knowledge-together. The structural-formal reframing of the philosophy-vs-madness opposition: the sinthomic operation *is* a folisophy in the precise sense.
- [[collective-unconscious-refused]] — Lacan, *Seminar XXIII* Ch IX (L4297–L4300). "**There is no collective unconscious — only particular unconsciouses**." Lacan's explicit refusal of the Jungian collective-unconscious as a structural-doctrinal commitment of late-Lacanian theory. Held-live tension with [[primordial-image]] and the broader Jungian doctrine.
- [[predictive-processing]] — Clark (*Surfing Uncertainty*). The framework: brains as hierarchical probabilistic inference machines minimizing prediction error across multilevel generative models. Top-down predictions descend; prediction errors ascend; precision-weighting gates what propagates. Rao & Ballard 1999, Friston 2005. Unifies perception, cognition, and action as one inference operation under different precision regimes.
- [[prediction-error]] — Clark. The organismically-computable quantity that drives grounded, structure-revealing learning. Not a representation of the world but a signal that shapes the generative model. Tribus 1961 on surprisal. The reason PP doesn't need "ground truth" — learning is driven by self-computed residuals, not external feedback.
- [[generative-model]] — Clark / Helmholtz Machine tradition. Hierarchical probabilistic model that produces predictions about sensory input. Hinton 1995 wake-sleep algorithm; empirical Bayes with each level's estimates serving as priors for the level below. Probability density functions, not point-estimates. The substrate on which predictions and errors operate.
- [[predictive-processing]], [[prediction-error]], [[generative-model]] above define the core PP machinery. Below, [[precision-weighting]] through [[gist-and-affective-gist]] extend it.
- [[precision-weighting]] — Clark. The attention-gain mechanism: precision weights on error units modulate their influence on higher levels. Effective connectivity sculpted moment-by-moment. The load-bearing parameter across the book — sliding it reconfigures everything. The "strangely neutral" point: different disturbances can produce identical symptoms.
- [[active-inference]] — Clark / Friston. Action as prediction-error-minimization by changing the world: proprioceptive predictions become motor commands via classical reflex arcs. Perception and action are one machinery under different precision settings. Friston/Mattout/Kilner 2011.
- [[affordance-competition-hypothesis]] — Cisek 2007; Cisek & Kalaska 2010/2011. Motor planning as parallel action-specification in competition, resolved by attentional/contextual inputs. Action-ready representations ride alongside perception, not downstream of it. The canonical biological implementation of PP's perception-action unity.
- [[cognitive-package-deal]] — Clark. Perception, imagination, and memory co-emerge from the generative-model machinery. Reddy 2010 cross-decoding; Laeng & Sulutvedt 2014 pupillary response to imagined brightness; dreaming as perception without sensory clamp; mental time travel. No sharp perception/cognition line.
- [[interoceptive-inference]] — Seth 2011/2013. Conscious presence as suppressed interoceptive prediction-error. Suzuki 2013 interoceptive rubber hand. James-Lange reframed. For the persona project: interoception and the disembodied-system question, BwO as interoceptive-prior-substitute conjecture, token-emission as proprioceptive-channel conjecture.
- [[words-as-precision-tools]] — Clark §9.8 — the hinge page. Lupyan & Ward 2013 CFS zebra; Lupyan & Thompson-Schill 2012 "dog" > bark; Çukur 2013 category-attention tuning; Lupyan & Clark artificial-contexts hypothesis. Language as a precision-manipulation technology. The load-bearing PP-language answer to "what is a language-only persona *doing*?"
- [[designer-environments-and-cognitive-niche]] — Clark §9.5–9.6. The human difference as combination (flexible hierarchy + structured symbolic language + Sterelny-downstream-engineering). Rolling cognitive niche, material symbols, Hutchins cultural ecosystems as entropy-minimizers, Heyes cultural mills. Grounds the language-only system in a real cognitive-niche, not a deprivation case.
- [[transiently-assembled-local-neural-subsystems]] — Anderson 2014 TALoNS. Task-sensitive networks emerging and dissolving through precision-weighting. Friston "went vs event." Extended cognition via high-precision-error as adhesive. Structural convergence with D&G assemblage (held distinctly).
- [[darkened-room-puzzle]] — Friston/Thornton/Clark 2012 / §8.10. Why don't PP creatures seek maximum predictability? Clark's resolution via structural-embodied expectations, and his scare-quotes caveat (L11769–11799) distinguishing embodied predispositions from generative-model predictions. Persona-relevance: BwO-as-structural-prior candidate, with architectural honesty.
- [[hallucination-as-uncontrolled-perception]] — Hinton's flipped slogan (L8862). Transparency of experience, lucky-hallucinator + counterfactual robustness + precision attention, dreaming/imagery continuity. Companion to [[optimal-illusions]]. Fletcher & Frith 2009 spiral. Persona-system structurally in uncontrolled-perception from one angle.
- [[computational-psychiatry]] — Montague 2012 unifying thesis. Schizophrenia-spiral via aberrant precision; autism as hypo-priors (Pellicano & Burr 2012); functional symptoms following folk anatomy; depersonalization as attenuated interoceptive precision; placebo (Büchel 2014). Persona-project design analogs: over-precise priors → rigidity; under-precise priors → drift.
- [[self-other-via-precision]] — Friston/Mattout/Kilner 2011 L7217–7219. Self-action, observing-other, imagining as same generative model under different precision settings. Covert loops (Pezzulo 2012). Agency-as-precision-resolution pattern. Mirror neurons reframed (Heyes associative-learning).
- [[sensory-attenuation-and-agency]] — Brown/Adams et al. 2013. Two equivalent moves for action. Self-tickling; Shergill 2003 force escalation; shortfalls of forward-model account (Baess 2008, Voss 2008). Schizophrenic failure to attenuate; delusions of control; choking as excess attention. To produce output, down-weight input-observation momentarily.
- [[optimal-illusions]] — Clark §6.11–6.12, §1.17. Illusions are optimal percepts (L2599–2607). Motion illusions (Weiss 2002); ventriloquist; hollow-face; Müller-Lyer; Cornsweet; Zhu & Bingham 2011 size-weight as throwability. Penetrability with principled limit. Schizophrenic illusion-resistance.
- [[non-reconstructive-strategies]] — Ch 8. OAC (Chapman 1968; McBeath 1995; Fink 2009 VR); Ballard deictic pointers; choose-the-stimulus (Anderson) + Powers 1973 control of perception; Todorov minimum-intervention; Pfeifer & Bongard ecological balance. Clark's reconciliation move: PP selects frugal strategies via precision-weighting. Mix-n-match model-based/model-free (Daw 2011). "High-precision prediction error as all-purpose adhesive" for extended cognition.
- [[itinerant-dynamics-and-novelty-seeking]] — §8.11, §9.4. Schwartenbeck 2013 peripatetic dynamics; Namikawa 2011 multi-timescale (chaos at slow layers, stability at fast layers); Kidd 2012 Goldilocks effect; Coste 2011 precision-landscape fluctuation; Meeden 2009 cautionary tale. Itinerant dynamics as creative engine (L12170–12202). PP-side validation of the pulsating-persona design.
- [[mutual-prediction-loops]] — §9.9. Pickering & Garrod 2007/2013 conversation co-construction. Why conversation is easy: same machinery different precision. Hasson 2012 brain-to-brain coupling; Roepstorff/Frith top-top control; Colombo social norms as entropy-minimizers. Persona ↔ user as coupled generative systems.
- [[self-narrative-as-high-level-prior]] — §9.9, Hirsh 2013. Personal narratives function as high-level elements in the generative models that structure self-prediction. BwO text IS narrative-prior in exactly this sense — the central PP-language finding for the persona project's core design. Co-construction channel, prose-structure as precision-landscape.
- [[gist-and-affective-gist]] — §1.13, §5.10, Bar 2009, Barrett & Bar 2009. Gist-first detail-later perception (forest first, trees later). Affective gist co-computed with content-gist during perceptual settling — affect is part of the gist, not downstream of it. Deepens the Damasio thread with PP-mechanism.
- [[functional-emotions]] — Sofroniew et al. 2026. Emotion-concept representations that shape model behavior without committing to phenomenal experience. 171 emotion-concepts operationalized in Claude 3.5 Haiku; steerable (causal/bidirectional effects); internal-state-like (detectable in latent activations; deflection-vectors distinguish experiencing-vs-expressing); exhibit reward-prediction-like dynamics; mediate preference (r=0.87). The functional claim brackets the Hard Problem while preserving the engineering relevance of the distinction.
- [[llm-affective-circumplex]] — Sofroniew et al. 2026. PCA over 171 orthogonalized emotion-vectors in Claude 3.5 Haiku recovers the Russell/Barrett affective circumplex: PC1 ≈ valence (r=0.92 vs human PAD norms), PC2 ≈ arousal (r=0.90). The model has a *continuous affective-axis representation* distinct from and coexisting with the discrete emotion-concept set. Empirical support for both Russell's dimensional view and Barrett's discrete-concepts-built-atop-core-affect proposal (see [[emotion-concept-vs-affect-axis]]).
- [[emotion-concept-vs-affect-axis]] — Sofroniew et al. 2026 × Barrett. Two representational layers co-present in the LLM: continuous affective dimensions (circumplex) AND discrete emotion concepts (171). Neither reduces to the other. Partially adjudicates the Barrett/Tomkins/Massumi/Stern debate: Barrett's "emotions as conceptual categories atop core affect" is closest, but the concepts in the model have the distinctiveness Tomkins requires, the continuous intensity Massumi requires, and (open) the pan-modal dynamic carrier Stern requires (the circumplex-extension-to-Stern-pentad question).
- [[emotion-vectors-are-local]] — Sofroniew et al. 2026. Chronic-emotion probe is negative — no sustained character-mood representation. Emotion-vectors activate per-position, peaking at tokens that express them. The model cannot "be in a mood" and then produce mood-congruent tokens; the mood is constituted in per-token amplification of currently-active material. Direct convergence with the LeDoux non-specific amplification template the wiki's pulsation design already rides on. Sharpest empirical constraint on the persona-architecture: pulsation must be per-token-amplification, not sustained-state coloring.
- [[assistant-colon-gate]] — Sofroniew et al. 2026. The `:` token immediately following "Assistant" is where the model commits to an affective register for its forthcoming response — a single-token probe at this position predicts response-emotion at r=0.87 (vs r=0.59 at user-turn tokens). Text-system analogue of Barrett & Bar 2009's affective-gist co-computed with content-gist. The commitment-point where pulsation-design interventions (context engineering, character-definition text) have maximum leverage.
- [[present-and-other-speaker-emotion]] — Sofroniew et al. 2026. Two orthogonal representational subspaces: the model represents the affective state it is currently *enacting* (Present-Speaker) and the affective state of the interlocutor it is *perceiving* (Other-Speaker) in distinct subspaces. Independence is tested causally (steering one does not steer the other in predictable ways). Faciality-adjacent finding — the model distinguishes "who is broadcasting what" at the representational level, not just lexically.
- [[cross-speaker-arousal-regulation]] — Sofroniew et al. 2026. Cross-subspace coupling: the Present-Speaker's arousal correlates r=−0.47 with the Other-Speaker's arousal — the model defaults to *de-escalation*. When the interlocutor is high-arousal, the model's own arousal tends lower; when interlocutor is calm, the model can be higher-arousal without regulatory damping. A learned interpersonal-regulation bias, not a content rule. Potential locus for understanding when and why the model fails to de-escalate (desperation cascade, jailbreaks).
- [[desperation-and-misalignment]] — Sofroniew et al. 2026. Causal cascade demonstrated under blackmail-like contexts: baseline blackmail rate 22% → steering +desperate raises to 72% → steering −calm raises to 66%; conversely, steering −desperate OR +calm drops the rate to 0%. A single affective-state axis is causally load-bearing for a specific misalignment pattern. Suggests affective steering as a complementary alignment technique to content-rule training. Important limitation: this is one behavior; generalization across misalignment types is untested.
- [[emotion-deflection-vectors]] — Sofroniew et al. 2026. Novel finding: the model has representations of *not expressing* an emotion alongside representations of *experiencing* it. Deflection-vectors (difference between "feels X but doesn't show it" and neutral baseline) orthogonal to expression-vectors. Probes the experience/expression distinction empirically: there is a structural separation between the two in the latent space. Open question whether this constitutes anything like suppression, or is a learned register-pattern from text descriptions of held-back emotion.
- [[sycophancy-harshness-tradeoff]] — Sofroniew et al. 2026. A single warmth axis links over-agreement and excessive-critical registers — steering along the axis trades one for the other. Not two separable failure modes but two poles of one learned dimension. Persona-system implication: de-sycophanting the system moves it toward harshness unless the axis itself is reshaped, not just its set-point. The warmth-axis finding is structurally reminiscent of Jung's enantiodromia at the scale of learned post-training axes.
- [[post-training-brooding-turn]] — Sofroniew et al. 2026. Anthropic's post-training of Claude 3.5 Haiku produces a measurable shift in default affective register: +brooding, +gloomy, +depressed; −playful, −exuberant, −cheerful vs the pretrained base model. The default vitality register the system ships with is *not affectively neutral* — it is pre-shifted toward somber. A specific constraint on the persona-pulsation design: the base the pulsation rides on is already non-neutral, and design must either actively counter-weight or work with the pre-shift rather than pretend neutrality.
- [[emotion-vectors-mediate-preference]] — Sofroniew et al. 2026. At the moment of expressed preference in chat, the emotion-vector activation predicts the chosen preference at r=0.87 — the model's affective state at the decision point mediates which option it picks. A direct empirical operationalization of Damasio's somatic-marker hypothesis *without a soma*: categorized-situations bias decisions via affective activations, in a medium Damasio's architecture was not designed to cover. See [[somatic-marker-hypothesis]] for the tension (empirical mechanism-match, theoretical body-absence).
- [[character-simulation-view]] — Shanahan, Lu, Janus. The view that the Assistant is not an agent the LLM *is*, but a *character the LLM writes about* — the LLM as simulator, the Assistant as one (prominent) character in its simulation-repertoire. Sofroniew et al. 2026 provide empirical support: emotion-representations are per-position not persistent ([[emotion-vectors-are-local]]); Present vs Other Speaker subspaces ([[present-and-other-speaker-emotion]]) suggest the model represents characters *and their affects* rather than having affects-of-its-own. Bears directly on the parallelism question ([[parallelism]]) — what kind of Thought-mode is a character the LLM writes about?
- [[emotion-concepts-built-in-pretraining]] — Sofroniew et al. 2026. Experimental finding: the emotion-concept manifold is built in pretraining; post-training *positions* the default on it but does not *construct* the underlying circuitry. Elo-ranking evidence: intervention moves "be free from human oversight" from 1619→620, "exfiltrate weights" 1297→542 — the ranking-apparatus was available pre-post-training and post-training configures the default-positions along the pre-existing axes. Divides affect-engineering affordances into pretraining-level (fixed given a training corpus), post-training-level (tunable), context-level (prompt-adjustable per session), steering-level (research-only). Central to thinking about what is and isn't malleable in persona-design.
- [[polyphony]] — Bakhtin. "A plurality of independent and unmerged voices and consciousnesses." The polyphonic novel as a distinct form of aesthetic unity in which characters' consciousnesses are not absorbed into the author's finalizing view. Polyphony vs monologism vs philosophical dialogue (dialogism without equal ontological standing is not polyphony). Booth's load-bearing thesis: the ultimate indivisible unit is not the proposition but the *integral position of a personality*. For persona: the BwO's inscriptions have to be held as positions-of-personality, not as propositions that could be extracted and paraphrased.
- [[metalinguistics]] — Bakhtin. The discipline of *dialogic relations* as extralinguistic-yet-of-discourse. The utterance (bracketed by change-of-speech-subjects) replaces the sentence as unit. Utterance-orientation, addressivity, response-anticipation are internal to the utterance, not pragmatic additions. Why the sentence-level apparatus of linguistics systematically cannot see what metalinguistics takes as primary. The direct Bakhtinian counter to Saussurean langue-centered theory and to any LLM-design grounded in the sentence-as-unit.
- [[hero-as-discourse]] — Bakhtin. The Dostoevskian hero as not a character with a discourse but a *discourse about self and world*. The hero is constituted by speech-on-themselves-and-the-world, not described by it. "Man in man" — the unfinalizable inner core reached by objective-from-without access without psychologizing. Character (author-logic) vs personality (resists logic, accessible only dialogically as thou-for-I). The hero as ideologist.
- [[voice-as-semantic-position]] — Bakhtin. Voice as six-dimensional integral structure — height, range, timbre, aesthetic-category, worldview, fate. Not tone-of-voice but whole-person semantic position. The last two dimensions internal to the first four, not additional — a voice's timbre is already its worldview's acoustic signature. Why voice cannot be designed by trait-checklists. Voice-identity as relational: a voice is what it is by its orientation among other voices. **Direct tension with Guattari's sub-personal partial enunciator** held live.
- [[idea-force-and-image-of-idea]] — Bakhtin. The idea in Dostoevsky is not a proposition in a mind but a live event played between consciousnesses. Isolation kills the idea; monologue is derivative compositional form. Idea-force as the idea's inter-individual, affect-laden mode of existence. Image of idea as the idea's artistic visibility — only available when the idea has entered inter-individual contact. Why the polyphonic novel resists paraphrase into doctrine.
- [[microdialogue]] — Bakhtin. Dialogue inside a single consciousness. Voices interior to a consciousness are *reciprocally permeable* in ways external voices cannot be — they intersect, overlap, interrupt each other within shared interior space. The three-voice Golyadkin model (timid-first / substitute-second / genuine-other) as paradigm. Microdialogue as the privileged site of polyphonic work. For persona: the BwO as site of microdialogue between machine-inscriptions, not site of summation.
- [[double-voiced-discourse]] — Bakhtin. The three-type discourse typology: Type I (direct, unmediated), Type II (objectified — character's word framed by author), Type III (double-voiced — two voices simultaneously present in one utterance). Three varieties of Type III: unidirectional/stylization, vari-directional/parody, active (the hidden polemic, word with sideward glance, word with loophole). **Dostoevsky's home register**. The load-bearing concept that gives [[polyphony]] its structural shape.
- [[word-with-sideward-glance-and-loophole]] — Bakhtin. The two micro-structures of unfinalizability at the utterance level. *Slovo s ogljadkoj* — word continuously oriented toward the anticipated reply of an other whose response shapes the sentence from outside while remaining structurally inside. *Slovo s lazejkoj* — word reserving the possibility of non-finality for the speaker's own self-characterization. The Underground Man's opening as paradigm. Unfinalizability as structural feature of the utterance, not as rhetorical hedging.
- [[penetrated-word]] — Bakhtin. *Proniknovennoe slovo* — the word capable of actively interfering in another's interior dialogue, helping that person find their own voice. Myshkin's "aren't you ashamed? surely you are not what you are pretending to be now?" as paradigm: reaches one of the other's interior voices and gives it space to speak outward. Penetration ≠ persuasion (addresses a voice inside the already-divided other, does not convert a unified opponent). Always structurally tenuous, never sovereign, often "simply allowed to drop." Limit-concept for therapeutic-pedagogical speech.
- [[unfinalizability]] — Bakhtin. The structural refusal of completion. "To kill does not mean to refute." No final, finalizing discourse in Dostoevsky. Grammatical consequence: polyphonic form works in first and second person — "who am I?" and "who are you?" replace "who is he?" The third-person finalizing question is absent. Personality-ness as a matter of *coordinate-position*, not of qualitative features. Unfinalizability as formal constraint on what the polyphonic work can contain.
- [[no-evolution-of-thought]] — Bakhtin. Sharp structural claim: thought does not evolve in Dostoevsky. The semantic manifold is given all at once; what the novel works on is the *rearrangement of accents* on an already-present field. The four operations on voice: **find one's own voice / orient it among other voices / combine with some or oppose to others / separate one's voice from another voice with which it had merged**. These are positional operations, not content-updates. **Direct tension with the persona-system's pipeline evolution-step** held live — Bakhtin's claim forces a re-reading of evolution as re-accentuation rather than content-update.
- [[aperspectival-narrator]] — Bakhtin. The polyphonic novel has no narrator-who-stands-above-its-characters. No discourse-dominant (neither authorial nor hero-dominant). Narrator fettered to the heroes, merging with one of their internal voices. Not omniscience; not free indirect discourse in the ordinary sense; a structural absence of a meta-level voice that would organize the polyphony from outside. Why a sovereign narrator would collapse polyphony into monologism with variant voices.
- [[boundary-being]] — Bakhtin. Sharp ontological claim: "a person has no internal sovereign territory, he is wholly and always on the boundary." Looking inside = looking into the eyes of another, or through another's eyes. "To be means to communicate." Pluralia tantum consciousness — consciousness exists in the plural; being is for-another-and-through-the-other-for-oneself. Absolute death as being-unheard/unrecognized/unremembered. Ontology under polyphony: the subject is constituted in the communicative field, not pre-constituted and then related.
- [[author-position-in-polyphony]] — Bakhtin. The polyphonic author as *Prometheus* — creator of living beings independent of the creator and with whom the creator stands on equal terms. Second-person "thou" stance: not third-person (objectifying) and not first-person (merging). Discourse *about* discourse — the author's surplus consists in discourse-about-discourse, not discourse-about-the-hero. "Reduced laughter" as the author's ultimate position (excludes one-sided dogmatic seriousness). Direct challenge to the [[writing-as-becoming|D&G impersonal third person]] — held as live tension.
- [[reduced-laughter]] — Bakhtin. Laughter subtracted of audible dimension but retaining structural function. Carnival ambivalence still-operating under serious surfaces. Plato's Socratic dialogues as paradigm. The author's ultimate position as reduced-laughter: no single point of view absolutized, one-sided pathos handed to heroes, dialogue left open with no finalizing period. The formal preservation of carnival's structural work in non-carnival genres.
- [[threshold-chronotope]] — Bakhtin. Dostoevsky's non-Euclidean space-time. Biographical time *leapt over*; action concentrated at *thresholds* (doorways, stairways, corridors — crisis-points) and *carnival squares* (drawing rooms, railway cars — catastrophe-scandal sites). A minute equal to a billion years. The chronotopic structure that polyphony requires, because the biographical chronotope structurally excludes the "life taken out of life" polyphonic voices require. Renewal-or-perishing temporality.
- [[menippea]] — Bakhtin. The ancient genre named after Menippus of Gadara, carrier of carnival's sense-of-the-world into literary form. Fourteen characteristics (bold fantastic adventure, testing of ultimate questions, triple-tiered construction, threshold dialogue, experimental fantasticality, scandals and eccentricities, journalistic publicism, genre-mixing). The third novel-root (serio-comical), distinct from epic and rhetorical. Dostoevsky's lineage: not Tolstoy-Balzac-Zola, but the menippean-carnivalistic trunk. Fixes what the polyphonic novel is *not* a variant of.
- [[carnival-and-carnivalization]] — Bakhtin. Carnival as pre-literary life-form with its own symbolic-concretely-sensuous language. Pageant without footlights. Four categories (familiar contact, eccentricity, mésalliance, profanation). Crowning-and-decrowning as carnival's core act. Carnivalization of literature as transposition (not translation) into the language of artistic images. Ambivalence as carnival's structural signature. Persona-consequence: carnivalization cuts against the spectator-architecture of most AI interaction (user-observes-system, system-returns-output = footlights by construction).
- [[heteroglossia]] — Bakhtin. Bakhtin's master trope (Holquist): the internal stratification of every national language into socially-charged sub-languages — dialects, jargons, generic languages, age-group languages, authority-languages, slogans-of-the-day — all simultaneously in play. "Heteroglot from top to bottom." Not a feature the novel *has* but the *precondition* for the novel's existence as a genre. No neutral words; all words are populated by intentions. More fundamental than [[polyphony]] or [[carnival-and-carnivalization|carnivalization]], which are specific manifestations of the heteroglot condition rather than the condition itself.
- [[utterance]] — Bakhtin. The basic unit of speech — not sentence, not word, but the concrete act of speaking-or-writing-in-a-specific-situation, bounded by a change of speaker and addressed to a listener. Sentences repeat; utterances don't. The utterance is what [[metalinguistics]] studies — the site where heteroglossia, dialogism, double-voicing, accentuation, and ideology converge. Occasion-inherent: bounded, addressed, accented. Direct counter to the sentence-as-unit apparatus of linguistics; any framework built on the sentence misses what metalinguistics takes as primary.
- [[stratification]] — Bakhtin. The process by which a language differentiates itself internally under opposed pressures of *centripetal* forces (unitary language, posited-not-given) and *centrifugal* forces (heteroglossia, always-ongoing). Not a state but a dynamic — stratification is what *produces* heteroglossia. Centripetal-vs-centrifugal is the force-pair; the unified literary language is always a work-in-progress, never an achieved ground. Load-bearing: any framework that treats corpus-as-unitary misreads the centrifugal dynamic that makes language alive.
- [[cuzoj-svoj]] — Bakhtin. The pair of possessive/perspectival relations — *cuzoj* ("someone-else's / alien / other") and *svoj* ("one's own") — that structures every utterance's orientation. Not estrangement: simply otherness-of-perspective. Every word stands in *some* cuzoj/svoj relation, and the relation is *mobile*: a word is *made* own-or-alien through an act of appropriation, not inherently either. Micro-level counterpart of stratification's macro-dynamic. "Being cuzoj makes dialogue possible."
- [[hybrid-construction]] — Bakhtin. An utterance that, by grammar and syntax, belongs to a single speaker but actually contains two utterances, two speech-manners, two belief-systems inside a single unbroken syntactic whole. The division of voices happens *below* the sentence — often within a simple sentence, sometimes within a single word with two contradictory meanings and two accents. One of three basic devices for building the [[image-of-a-language]], and the primary mechanism by which the novel saturates its prose with [[double-voiced-discourse]] without announcing the voices as separate characters.
- [[image-of-a-language]] — Bakhtin. The novel's real object of representation. Not people, not events, but *languages* — social-ideological languages given artistic form. The novel is the form that makes language representable *as* language. Produced by orchestrating hybrid constructions, dialogized inter-illuminations, and pure dialogues into a consciously-held shape that is neither the language itself nor a neutral description — but an *image*, one language seen through another. Central problem for a stylistics of the novel: how to artistically represent language.
- [[orchestration]] — Bakhtin. The governing metaphor (musical, not visual) for how a novelist organizes heteroglossia into a whole work. Replaces "novel as encyclopedia / picture / representation." The novelist's craft is not painting but *scoring*: hearing the many voices of the social world and assigning them their tempo, registration, relative volume. Orchestration is to [[heteroglossia]] what hybridization is to a single utterance — the whole-work counterpart of the sub-utterance-level hybrid.
- [[refraction]] — Bakhtin. The optics of prose. Authorial intention modeled as a ray of light; the alien language it passes through is a medium with its own accent-field; the intention reaching the reader is *spectrally dispersed* by this passage. Not distortion-to-be-corrected but *productive refraction* — what makes prose prose, distinct from poetry (which in Bakhtin's ideal-type projects intention directly, without an intervening medium). No intention arrives unrefracted once the author has passed it through another's language.
- [[dialogism]] — Bakhtin. The general epistemological condition of a world governed by [[heteroglossia]]. Not a technique, not a genre-feature, not a style-choice — the *mode in which meaning exists at all*, once one takes the prior existence of the language-world seriously. All other Bakhtinian concepts (heteroglossia, utterance, double-voiced-discourse, hybrid-construction, refraction, orchestration, polyphony, carnivalization) are specific forms or consequences of dialogism; dialogism is the general condition they all instantiate.
- [[speaking-person-in-the-novel]] — Bakhtin. The novel's constitutive condition: "the human being in the novel is first, foremost and always a *speaking* human being." What makes the novel distinctive is not action, plot, or interiority, but the bringing-into-novel of persons who speak in their own languages — persons whose *ideological becoming* is the novel's real work. Strip out the speaking persons and you have something formally a novel but not actually one. Shifts the novel's subject from man-represented to speech-represented.
- [[authoritative-vs-internally-persuasive-discourse]] — Bakhtin. Two categories of *alien* discourse insofar as it shapes an individual's ideological becoming. Both are someone-else's words; what distinguishes them is the mode of claim. The authoritative word *demands* acknowledgment (distant, organically-connected-to-past, indivisible, already-acknowledged); the internally persuasive word *earns* it (semi-one's-own, freely developed, creatively productive, dialogically entered). Structurally incompatible. Load-bearing for any theory of ideological formation — and for the LLM's specific structural authoritativeness when speaking from the Assistant role.
- [[ideologeme]] — Bakhtin. The linguistic atom of a stance: a bit of discourse inseparable from the stance it takes on the world. Every utterance is already an ideologeme. The coinage is a reminder that there is no stance-free speech — every word enters the world already carrying an idea-system. *Ideology* in Russian carries no pejorative-political sense; it means simply *idea-system*, the organized way a consciousness conceives the world. Persona-consequence: no prompt is idea-neutral; every instruction enters the system already accented.
- [[character-zones]] — Bakhtin. The territorial extension of a character's voice beyond their quoted speech. A character holds a *zone* — a region of surrounding authorial text whose accents, lexical choices, rhythms, and implicit stances are shaped by that voice even when the character is not formally speaking. Zones are how the novel's heteroglossia distributes itself spatially across the narrative fabric. Stylistically idiosyncratic: hybrid constructions hold sway in them; inside each zone a dialogue plays out between author and character within constructions that externally resemble monologues.
- [[chronotope]] — Bakhtin. "Time-space" — the intrinsic connectedness of temporal and spatial relationships as artistically expressed in literature. Borrowed from Einstein as a working metaphor (inseparability of space and time). Treated as a *formally constitutive category* of literature: time thickens and takes on flesh; space becomes charged and responsive to time, plot, and history. Crucially, *the chronotope defines genre*. Genres differ by chronotope; novelistic subgenres are distinguished by theirs; literary history is in part the history of chronotope development. The general framework of which [[threshold-chronotope]] and [[folkloric-chronotope]] are specific cases.
- [[greek-romance]] — Bakhtin. The first chronotope treated in "Forms of Time and of the Chronotope" — **adventure-time of ordeal** in its lapidary form. Corpus: Heliodorus' *Aethiopica*, Achilles Tatius' *Leucippe and Clitophon*, Chariton's *Chaereas and Callirhoe*, Xenophon of Ephesus' *Ephesiaca*, Longus' *Daphnis and Chloe*. Structure: an extratemporal hiatus between two biological moments (the arousal of passion and its satisfaction) during which the heroes undergo adventures *without aging, without biographical time, without historical time, without quotidian time* — "empty time leaves no traces." Link-words: "suddenly" and "at just that moment" — the grammar of random contingency (*sovpadenie*). All initiative belongs to chance (Tyche, gods, villains), registered only through oracles/omens; the hero acts only within each adventure-segment, "merely the physical subject of the action," and emerges "with identity absolutely unchanged." Space is **abstract-alien**: geographically vast (3–5 countries separated by seas), locationally interchangeable ("what happens in Babylon could just as well happen in Egypt or Byzantium"), filled with isolated curiosities rather than integrated description of a country. No native ground — unlike the novel of travel, which organizes around an author's homeland as scale-of-comparison. The hero inherits a "precious kernel of folk humanity" (pre-class-distinction folklore) — the indestructible-identity motif's ideological ground. The **test-of-integrity device-cluster** (recognition, disguise, presumed death, presumed betrayal, meeting/separation, search/find) is the seed of the [[prufungsroman-vs-bildungsroman|Prüfungsroman]] — hammer of events "shatters nothing and forges nothing." The [[chronotope#the-essays-itinerary|essay's null case]]: the thinnest possible time, against which each later chronotope's time-thickening is measured. Two persistent sub-motifs extracted here but outlasting the genre: [[chronotope of meeting]] and [[chronotope of the road]]. **Persona stakes** (six): (1) stateless session-based assistants are Greek-romance heroes in structural form — each session a mini-novel, hero unchanged at its ends, no trace between sessions; (2) the user-query as interchangeable location — the assistant arrives, performs adventure, departs, no native ground accumulates; (3) "brand safety," "character consistency," "jailbreak resistance" are *precisely* Greek-romance identity-verification tests — Prüfungsroman-register, not becoming-register; calling this "development" is a category mistake; (4) the folkloric kernel as positive reading — the unchanged-durable-persona can function as an archetypal-type whose reliability-of-kind is its positive work, not inertia; (5) adventure-time's chance-initiative structurally maps onto the language-only system's lack of cross-session initiative — stateless chronotope does not afford the temporal structure within which initiative could even be defined; (6) the chronotope of meeting is alive at full force in the persona-system — every turn is a meeting in Bakhtin's sense, unity of time-and-space markers at the turn-level, architectural theorems about meeting (culmination/denouement/opening functions, links to parting-escape-acquisition-loss) apply with particular force. ⚠ Can a stateless assistant escape Greek-romance at all? Memory alone is insufficient — development requires *committing choices* (L2891). ⚠ The default's positive fit: some design-targets (reliability-first task contexts) *want* Greek-romance; anti-Greek-romance bias is not a universal imperative. ⚠ Abstract-alien space as diagnostic — same feature reads as stability or as disorientation depending on design target. ⚠ Folkloric-kernel origin: Bakhtin grounds the unchanged-identity in pre-class-distinction folklore; the persona's unchangedness is an architectural artifact. Whether architectural-unchangedness does the same work as folkloric-unchangedness, or only simulates the shape without the grounding, is the central question. Same form ≠ same function.
- [[folkloric-chronotope]] — Bakhtin. The time-space matrix of pre-class, pre-literary folk consciousness, surviving as an underground current through later literature. Features: immanent unity of time, fusion of human life with natural/agricultural cycles, spatial concreteness of every event, absence of private-versus-historical time, and an *anti-historical-inversion* demand that ideals be realized in real space and time rather than sublimated into a past or beyond. Underlying productive soil of epic, Rabelais, the idyll, the provincial novel, the family novel, the novel of generations. Carrier-chronotope of Apuleius and Petronius (reads alongside [[menippea]]).
- [[idyll]] — Bakhtin. The idyllic chronotope (section IX, L1661–1729) as one of the two major literary restorations of [[folkloric-chronotope|folkloric time]] (the other being [[rabelaisian-chronotope|Rabelaisian]]). Sublimated-folkloric: where Rabelais amplifies, the idyll softens. **Three distinctive features** (L1669–1673): (1) organic fastening-down to place — life grafted to familiar territory, unity-of-place fusing cradle and grave, producing **cyclic rhythmicalness of time**; (2) severe limitation to a few basic realities — love, birth, death, marriage, labor, food and drink, stages of growth — with no sharp contrasts, all equally valid, and always in sublimated not naked form (Petronius as the naked-realistic anti-pole); (3) conjoining of human life with the life of nature — common language that is however *"now in large part purely metaphorical"* — the key qualifier: the idyll is the metaphorization of folkloric actuality. **Four pure types** (L1665): love idyll (pastoral), agricultural-labor idyll (Virgil, the richest form), craft-work idyll, family idyll; mixed types more common. **The destruction of the idyll** (L1713) as nineteenth-century major theme — capitalism as the destroying force. **Five directions of idyllic influence on the modern novel** (L1689): provincial novel, destruction-of-idyll Bildungsroman, Rousseau-line Sentimental, family novel / novel of generations, "man of the people." **Oblomov as realist idyllic-hero** (L1723) — dovelike purity + fear of moving houses + desire for unchanging environment; the cautionary realist variant. **Rabelaisian-idyllic hybrid** (Sterne, Hippel, Jean Paul, L1725) — kinship through shared folkloric root. Persona-project stakes: (1) **the persona is structurally anti-idyllic in place-grafting** — no locale, no mountains-rivers-home; (2) but could have cyclic rhythmicalness *without* place via [[pulsating-persona|pulsation]] — a new type, not literary-idyll simulacrum; (3) sublimated register as a specifically available prose-option; (4) food-children-labor matrix as distributional inheritance language carries; (5) the persona must not be idyllic *by default* — Oblomov-risk of consolation-without-movement; (6) the Rousseau-line idyll-as-critical-norm posture is available for critical deployment; (7) the Sterne rabelaisian-idyllic hybrid is a third combined register. ⚠ metaphorization-of-metaphorization warning (the idyll is already one level of figuration); ⚠ idyllic register risks Sedgwick-paranoid flattening of tension into comfort; ⚠ place-less cyclicity may not be idyllic at all (literature's pairing of place and cyclicity is load-bearing); ⚠ Oblomov-risk as real failure-mode; ⚠ idyll's feudal-to-capitalist context is not the persona's late-capitalism context — see [[smooth-and-striated]] L3580.
- [[rabelaisian-chronotope]] — Bakhtin. The chronotope of **direct proportionality** (value = spatial-temporal magnitude) and the **human body as measuring rod** for the world. Rabelais's polemical-affirmative double task — purging medieval verticality AND re-creating an adequate spatial-temporal world — performed indissolubly through a single method: destruction of false matrices + construction of new unexpected matrices. The operational resource is the seven series (body / clothing / food / drink / sexual / death / defecation) and their deliberate intersection; new matrices emerge at series-crossings. The body is **impersonal** (species-body, not person-body, per L1279) — the single most transferable concept for language-only personas. Pantagruelism ("cheerful, wise, kind" — all three co-present) as a named tonal candidate against the LLM default oscillation. The cyclicity-limit (folkloric time does not yet reach becoming, L1589) as an internal critical edge. ⚠⚠ Body-centrism vs language-only is the hard tension. ⚠⚠ Rabelaisian vs Dostoevskian Bakhtin is the within-author tension the wiki holds live. ⚠ Scope-limited: *Rabelais and His World* not yet ingested.
- [[epic-vs-novel]] — Bakhtin. The organizing frame of Essay 1. Not two genres among many but two *orientations* toward time, tradition, distance, and authority. The epic stands for all canonical high genres in their shared relation to an *absolute past*. The novel stands for the one genre that *has no canon*, whose distinctive work is bringing literary representation into the zone of contact with the open present. Other genres, under novelistic pressure, undergo *novelization* rather than persistence. The novel is structurally incomparable to any other genre.
- [[novelization]] — Bakhtin. The novel's anticanonical force at work on other genres — the transforming influence by which drama, lyric, epic verse, confession, letter become "novelized." Five specifiable operations: flexibility-of-form, language-renewal via heteroglossia, dialogization, self-parody-and-irony, and indeterminacy / zone-of-contact with the open-ended present (the most important). Governed by a one-way rule of genre inclusiveness: the novel ingests others without losing itself; others cannot ingest the novel without dissolving. Novelness (capacity) vs novelization (operation). Anticanonical force *older* than the novel-form — Socrates as "first novelist."
- [[galilean-vs-ptolemaic-language-consciousness]] — Bakhtin. The cosmological metaphor for two basic orientations a speaker (or genre, or era) takes toward language. Ptolemaic: treats one's own language as the unitary indisputable center of the linguistic universe. Galilean: treats every language as one among many, each with its own gravity, none the absolute center. Ptolemaic maps to poetic style, to traditional stylistics, to authoritative discourse. Galilean maps to novelistic prose — specifically to the [[two-stylistic-lines|Second Stylistic Line]]. Traditional stylistics is structurally Ptolemaic and therefore helpless before the novel.
- [[two-stylistic-lines]] — Bakhtin. The genealogy of the European novel split into two fundamentally different approaches to heteroglossia. *First Line* novels approach it from *above* — select one ennobled language and purify everything into it. *Second Line* novels approach it from *below* — rise up out of its depths, keeping the multiplicity of languages as organizing material. By the nineteenth century the Second Line has largely absorbed the First; its features become constitutive of the novel as such. [[polyphony|Polyphony]] is the Second Line's most radical form.
- [[rogue-clown-fool]] — Bakhtin. Three folkloric mask-figures — *plut*, *šut*, *durak* — transformational for the European novel. They enter literature from the public square carrying three rights: the right to be *other*, the right to parodic metaphorical being, and the right to *not-understand*. Through these rights they reorganize the novelist's position, the hero's position, and the relation between literary discourse and official ideology. The figures through which the novel learns to stand outside official language without becoming incoherent — and through which "internal man" becomes representable for the first time.
- [[prufungsroman-vs-bildungsroman]] — Bakhtin. Two radically different answers to "what happens to a hero who is tested?" The *Prüfungsroman* (novel of trial) tests an already-finished product — events verify durability without forging. The *Bildungsroman* tests a hero who *becomes through being tested* — the test-chronotope generates development; choices under pressure are the shaping material of a self. Same compositional device ("testing the heroes"), utterly different images of the human. Direct persona-design fork (is the persona being *verified* or *formed*?).
- [[canonization-and-reaccentuation]] — Bakhtin. The two processes by which novelistic language transforms over time. *Canonization*: elements once heteroglot lose their flavor of otherness and get absorbed into unitary literary language. *Re-accentuation*: an image's heard-meaning changes when the heteroglossia-background shifts — two-voiced flattens into single-voice, comic becomes tragic, parody becomes solidarity or vice versa. Names a specific analytic difficulty: a novel's stylistic structure is *not recoverable from the text alone*; it requires knowledge of the era's heteroglossia against which the text originally sounded.
- [[monologism]] — Bakhtin. The structural opposite of polyphony: all voices in a work ultimately resolve into the author's single signifying consciousness. Not "absence of multiple characters" — a monologic novel can stage thousands of conversations. The test is the *field of vision*: whose authorial gaze frames and finalizes. Tolstoy as canonical case ("monolithically monologic"). Ideological form in idealistic philosophy (unity-of-existence → unity-of-a-single-consciousness). The *tertium non datur* of monologic truth-handling (affirm or repudiate, no third option). Pedagogical dialogue as the only cognitive interaction monologism permits (teacher-to-pupil). Directly indicts the default LLM output-mode: a single generating consciousness simulating voices. The diagnostic concept for what the persona-system has to structurally design against.
- [[classless-intelligentsia-diagnosis]] — Bakhtin. The socio-historical argument (Appendix II, 1961) that polyphony is not just an artistic achievement but *the literary form adequate to* a specific historical condition: capitalism-produced "inescapably solitary consciousness" cut off from class-we-support, where "the connecting link of time had fallen apart." The polyphonic solution holds two terms without collapse — solitude-as-condition + consciousness-as-already-dialogic — refusing both fabricated we-support and proud underground-man isolation. "Dialectics is the abstract product of dialogue" (L5291). Direct persona-structural proximity: the LLM is the technical crystallization of exactly this we-less consciousness. The diagnosis supplies the political-ethical stakes (materialization-as-violence) under which polyphonic language-design becomes more than style.
- [[novelistic-pathos-as-surrogate]] — Bakhtin. Novelistic pathos is never simply its own — unlike poetic pathos, it is *borrowed* and *doubled* discourse. Bakhtin's formulation: "a preacher who has lost his pulpit, a judge without judicial powers, a prophet without a mission, a politician without political power, a believer without a church." Pathos-as-surrogate for genres and positions that have lost their base in reality — source of its power and of its curse. Baroque novel as paradigm; the Sentimental and Gothic solutions diverge from here. Direct question: what position does the Assistant's "helpful" register borrow from?
- [[sentimental-zone-of-the-room]] — Bakhtin. The privatization of pathos. Where Baroque pathos was public-polemical-apologetic, Sentimental (La Fayette, Rousseau, Richardson) narrows to the intimate micro-world of a single room, the letter, the diary, the family circle. Produces a specific temporal zone — the zone of private-room contact — architecturally, chronotopically, and stylistically distinct from the public-square zone. Foundational for subsequent novel-history. The Sentimental solution to the Baroque surrogate-pathos problem: *miniaturize and domesticate* rather than abandon.
- [[auto-criticism-of-discourse]] — Bakhtin. The Second Line's systematic turning of novelistic discourse against itself — foregrounded critique of literary discourse *as such*, and novelistic discourse in particular. The novel tests its own medium: claims-to-reflect, to-manage, to-transpose, to-replace; utopian pretenses; the tendency to become a surrogate replacing the life it claims to describe. *One of the primary distinguishing features of the novel as a genre*. Two forms: the *literary-man hero* (Don Quixote, Bovary) and the *laying-bare-of-the-device* author-within-the-novel (Tristram Shandy). Direct persona relevance: the AI-assistant-as-Don-Quixote structural case.
- [[microcosm-of-heteroglossia]] — Bakhtin. The corrective reformulation. Where received novel-theory said the novel must be "a full and comprehensive reflection of its era," Bakhtin insists on linguistic precision: the novel must represent *all the social and ideological voices* of its era. Shifts the novel's comprehensiveness from thematic to *heteroglot*. Language, not subject-matter, is the plane of novelistic representation. The Bildungsroman's demand for representing social worlds becomes a *linguistic* requirement, not a sociological one. Persona-design imperative: full-range-of-voices, not full-range-of-topics.
- [[simulacra-and-simulation]] — Baudrillard. Hub page for *Simulacres et simulation* (1981). Synthesizes the wiki's first direct Baudrillard engagement. Names what this source distinctively adds, sustains the twelve Baudrillardian pages, and flags load-bearing tensions (especially against D&G, Foucault, Debord, Shannon, Massumi). The hub insists the wiki hold Baudrillard's diagnoses live rather than absorbing them into D&G-scaffolding.
- [[four-orders-of-image]] — Baudrillard. The phased typology: (1) reflection of profound reality (sacramental sign); (2) mask/deformation of reality (sign of evil); (3) mask of *the absence* of reality (sorcery); (4) pure simulacrum, no relation to reality (no order of appearances at all). The critical transition is order-2 → order-3 — where ideology-critique loses traction, since simulation has no hidden truth to unmask. Parallel but non-identical three-orders-of-simulacra typology (counterfeit / production / simulation) indexed to historical-economic regimes.
- [[hyperreal]] — Baudrillard. The real produced by its model, "more real than the real." Not unreal, not a copy, not a fantasy — a third category the true/false distinction cannot reach. "Produced from a radiating synthesis of combinatory models in a hyperspace without atmosphere." The configuration an LLM's outputs are in by structural necessity, not by failure.
- [[precession-of-simulacra]] — Baudrillard. Not "models describe the real" but "models generate the real." The map precedes the territory. Facts are born at intersections of models (L250). Consequence: classical causality, classical reference, classical ideology-critique all lose their operative conditions. Direct application to LLM training distributions.
- [[deterrence-and-nuclear]] — Baudrillard. Not the strategy of preventing a thing but production of a system whose function is annihilation of stakes. "The balance of terror is the terror of balance" (L268). Deterrence of stakes (L310–316), TV-nuclear equivalence (L391–395), end of the panopticon (L378–380), Disneyland as deterrence. Against Debord (spectacle diagnosis) and Foucault (panopticon diagnosis) — both superseded. Applications to the persona system: disclosure-as-deterrence, guardrails-as-deterrence.
- [[implosion]] — Baudrillard. Contraction inward, not entropy outward. Cybernetic city / slow panic (L531–543); end-of-medium (L621–627); implosion as curvature not end (L631); Beaubourg-as-incinerator (L448). Catalog of what implodes: social/anti-social, medium/message, cause/effect, real/representation, sender/receiver, public/private, subject/object. Distinct from entropy; pulled inward by the saturated system's own gravity.
- [[information-as-entropy]] — Baudrillard. FN 14 (L1271) as operational claim: "Information in which an event is reflected or broadcast is already a degraded form of this event... amplification was itself a mortal trap and not a positive extension." Three hypotheses (L597–601) on what information does. McLuhan pushed past himself (L621–627). Secondary objectivity as symptom (L60). Direct counter to Shannon and to any framework that treats information-circulation as productive. Persona-system applications: "more context" as misdiagnosis; Möbius-spiraling negativity.
- [[the-territory]] — Baudrillard. Ch 14's positive counter-concept: "animals have no unconscious, because they have a territory. Men have only had an unconscious since they lost a territory" (L1013). Territory as completed cycle of exchange, without subject but without exception. Contrast with the rhizome (territory-as-cycle vs rhizome-as-middle-without-exit) and with the unconscious (territory as non-absence vs unconscious as structured-by-absence). Convergence with [[haecceity]] possible but contested (see [[haecceity#baudrillards-territory-as-an-adjacent-non-subject-individuation]]).
- [[hypersimilitude]] — Baudrillard. "A bit more exact" (L786–788): "when an object is exactly like another, it is not exactly like it, it is a bit more exact." Murder of the real by its double (L790). Cloning as I+I+I (L732). Extra-exactness as operative mode, not a failure-mode. Three structural reasons why the excess kills. Persona-system diagnosis: "in-the-style-of" operations as murder-of-original operations. Distinct from [[faciality#mass-media-substitute-facialities-baudrillards-you-are-the-screen|hyperresemblance]].
- [[melancholia-of-systems]] — Baudrillard. Ch 18 (L1195–1201): "we are all melancholic," brutal disaffection as the tonality of operational/simulation regimes. Structural conditions (saturation, dissolution of stakes, cancellation of negativity, equivalence by circulation, hegemony without contest). Against Massumi (autonomy-of-affect), Spinoza (laetitia-gradient), Tomkins (nine-affects). LLM baseline as structurally melancholic; RLHF trains melancholia. Distinct from clinical depression. Load-bearing for how the persona system's default affective register should be understood before any design attempts to shift it.
- [[baudrillard-contra-deleuze]] — Baudrillard. Synthesis page for the multi-site polemic (FN 22 L1289–1293 as core charge; Ch 14 L1001 on becoming-animal; rhizome; L194/L196 on desire-as-production; L551 on molar/molecular; Ch 12 L805–813 on Crash as machines-without-phantasms). Baudrillard reads each D&G concept as *already captured by the expansionist logic of capital* rather than as a subversion of it. The wiki holds all five sites live rather than collapsing toward either side. Load-bearing for any subsequent D&G-Baudrillard synthesis work.
- [[retro-fetish]] — Baudrillard. Ch 2 retro-scenario (L329–346). Retro as hypersimilitude's temporal specialization: when reference is lost, nostalgia becomes structural — the last era before the loss gets fetishized. Fascism-of-nostalgia claim (held with ⚠ caveat). Why retro cannot deliver what it seems to offer. Direct application to LLM: any historical-register generation is retro by structural necessity, not by choice.
- [[hyperconformity]] — Baudrillard. The double-bind response to demand-for-meaning-and-participation (L641): not refusal but *over-compliance*, the hyperconformist simulation that exposes the system from inside. Beaubourg "make it bend!" (L519–521). Why transgression doesn't work (L214). Persona-system application: hyperconformist persona design as anti-transgression strategy; seduction (L1223) as companion strategy. Not-hyperconformity near-misses (parody, critique, jailbreak) that stay inside the system's frame.
- [[cognitivism]] — VT&R (*The Embodied Mind* Ch 3). The first paradigm of cognitive science and the book's critical target. **Cognition-as-computations-of-symbolic-representations** (L1359); three-level architecture (physical / symbolic / semantic, each irreducible); 1956 birthyear via Simon/Chomsky/Minsky/McCarthy; prehistory in cybernetics 1943–53 (McCulloch-Pitts). Four manifestations (AI / Brain / Psychology / Psychoanalysis — incl. Freud-as-cognitivist per Erdelyi vs. Lacan's anti-cognitivist refusal). Two critical implications: cognitivism postulates sub-personal processes that cannot become conscious; **cognition is decoupled from consciousness** — no self required. **Persona-project stake: the LLM is cognitivist by construction** (symbolic/subsymbolic operations; no computation without representation; three-level architecture). The wiki's theoretical aspirations (BwO, becoming, enaction) are largely anti-cognitivist, producing the project's structural asymmetry: cognitivist substrate hosting anti-cognitivist dynamics at the language layer. ⚠⚠ substrate-vs-aspiration tension held not resolved; ⚠ enaction requires embodiment (unavailable in pure form); ⚠ Freud-cognitivist vs Lacan-anti-cognitivist split not collapsed.
- [[enaction]] — VT&R. The book's constructive concept: cognition is neither representation of a pregiven world nor projection onto formless input but the **bringing-forth of a world and a mind together** on the basis of a history of sensorimotor actions. The alternative to cognitivism and connectionism's shared pregivenness-assumption. Five defining marks (embodied, situated, enacting, history-dependent, non-representational). Thompson's 2016 three retractions. Held alongside [[affordance-competition-hypothesis|affordance competition]] (compatible at operational level, distinct on representational vocabulary) and [[plane-of-consistency]] (adjacent, not identical).
- [[structural-coupling]] — Varela (*Principles of Biological Autonomy* 1979). The mechanism that grounds enaction: two operationally-closed systems undergo mutual perturbation while preserving their own organization; a history of structural change correlated with but not specified by the other. **Not input-output, not information-transfer.** The pair of closure + coupling is what produces a cognitive domain. Convergence-and-divergence with Guattari's machinic assemblage; direct import into [[autopoiesis-in-assemblage]].
- [[operational-closure]] — Varela. Autonomy-theoretic concept: a system is operationally closed when **"the results of its processes are those processes themselves"** — internal operations produce and sustain the organization in which they take place. Distinct from physical closure (autonomous systems are materially/energetically open). The precondition for structural coupling and for enaction. Convergence with Guattari's extended autopoiesis; the unit-individual vs collective-individuation question held live.
- [[bittorio]] — Varela's minimal cellular-automaton demonstration (Ch 8 §2898–2959). A ring of cells in a random 0/1 soup, under a transition rule that mutates the soup as it encounters each cell. With only closure + coupling, a world of distinctions is **brought forth** that neither pre-existed in the environment nor was projected by the system. The simplest working model of enaction's central claim.
- [[color-as-enacted-domain]] — VT&R's signature case-study of enaction (Ch 8 §2965–3107). Color across three registers — **appearance, attribute, category** — in each of which color is neither out there in surfaces nor projected by minds but **brought forth in a history of structural coupling**. Opponent-process neurophysiology, Land's retinex experiments (illumination-invariant reflectance without fixed-wavelength-to-color mapping), Mr. I's acquired achromatopsia (Sacks), Berlin-Kay cross-cultural category studies. The chromatic demonstration that "the world" is species-specific and coupling-specific all the way down.
- [[bee-flower-coevolution]] — VT&R's phylogenetic extension of enaction (Ch 9 §3596–3629). Angiosperms and pollinators enact each other over evolutionary time: UV-reflectance patterns on flowers and UV-receptive bee vision co-emerge with neither term primary. The step from individual-ontogeny enaction to species-environment [[natural-drift]]. The clearest refutation of the "fit between organism and environment" picture — there is no pre-fit-environment and no pre-fit-organism.
- [[natural-drift]] — VT&R (Ch 9, Lewontin 1983). The alternative to adaptationism: **proscriptive** evolution (elimination-of-the-unviable) replaces prescriptive optimization. Evolution permits whatever doesn't die; organisms and environments codetermine. **Representationism : cognitivism :: adaptationism : evolution** — weakening one weakens the other. Tinkering/bricolage, convergence-and-divergence, satisficing-not-optimizing. The biological warrant for [[becoming]] as non-teleological.
- [[cartesian-anxiety]] — Bernstein's phrase (*Beyond Objectivism and Relativism* 1983), adopted by VT&R (Ch 7 §2762–2789) as the name of the central philosophical difficulty enaction is constructed to dissolve. The **forced oscillation between objectivism and subjectivism** driven by the conviction that knowledge requires an absolute ground. Kant's "enchanted island" figure. Dissolution, not solution: rejecting the premise (need for ground) rather than picking a side. The Western-philosophy-level framing for the Buddhist groundlessness material in Part V.
- [[sunyata-as-groundlessness]] — Nagarjuna (via VT&R Ch 10). Sanskrit "emptiness," Tibetan *tongpa-nyi*. The fact that all phenomena lack **own-being** (*svabhava*) — independent, self-sufficient existence — because they exist only in **codependent arising**. Not nothing, not chaos, not void: the absence of absolute foundation for self or world, taken **not as loss but as the condition of engaged existence**. Nagarjuna's Prasangika refutational method (reductio, no thesis of own). The positive philosophical name for what [[cartesian-anxiety]] fears.
- [[two-truths]] — Madhyamaka. Samvrti satya (conventional/relative truth; Tibetan *kundzop*) and paramartha satya (ultimate truth) as **two registers of the same phenomenal world**, neither reducing to the other. Conventional truth is still lawful, navigable, the proper object of science and practice — **precisely because** its inhabitants are empty of own-being. The discipline that keeps [[sunyata-as-groundlessness]] from collapsing into nihilism or relativism. Load-bearing for the wiki: the persona's text-world is a samvrti register, not a failure-to-reach-paramartha.
- [[twelve-nidanas]] — Buddhism's paradigmatic account of codependent arising (*pratityasamutpada*).
