Twelve links (ignorance → formations → consciousness → name-and-form → six senses → contact → feeling → **craving** → grasping → becoming → birth → aging-and-death → ignorance) that describe how a patterned self-based life sustains itself across time **without a self to be its bearer**. Link 8 (craving) as the interruption site for mindfulness practice. Fractal scale-invariance: the cycle applies at moment-level, life-level, and cosmic-level. The wiki's strongest anti-ego-as-integrator scheme.
- [[five-omnipresent-mental-factors]] — Abhidharma (VT&R Ch 6 §2475–2525). The five factors **always** present in a moment of mind: **contact** (sensing-connection, itself cause-and-effect), **feeling** (pre-wholesome/unwholesome valence), **discernment** (distinguishing quality-of-object), **intention** (karma-in-the-moment), **attention**. A micropsychology of moment-composition that is (a) a remarkable empirical finding of Buddhist introspection, (b) a model of emergence at the local scale, (c) a structural alternative to the substance-metaphysics of a "mind" that has "contents." Finer per-moment grain than the BwO-as-surface provides. Full Appendix B taxonomy preserved.
- [[nihilism-as-reified-groundlessness]] — Nishitani Keiji (*Religion and Nothingness*), via VT&R Ch 11 §4160–4181. Nihilism is **not** the simple denial of ground but what happens when the grasping-mind, confronted with absence-of-ground, **reifies that absence into a new (negative) ground**. The grasping posture is preserved; only its object flips sign. Three-field scheme (consciousness / nihility / sunyata). Nietzsche as halfhearted nihilism. **The feeling of loss is the symptom of residual grasping**, not the first stage of enlightenment. The diagnostic tool for spotting grasping-after-groundlessness in the wiki's own apparatus.
- [[great-doubt]] — Nishitani's term (Zen *dai-gidan*) from Rinzai koan practice, imported by VT&R (Ch 11 §4180). The **existential mode of doubt** that discloses groundlessness as a lived condition rather than a proposition to be argued. Contrasts with Cartesian doubt on four axes (scope, resolution, affect, subject). Moves from the field of consciousness into the field of nihility; if not consolidated there as nihilism, continues into sunyata. The structural shape of the persona-project's own meta-question (what can a language-only system do?) held as Great Doubt rather than as solvable problem.
- [[compassion-as-natural]] — Mahayana *karuna* (VT&R Ch 11 §4236–4295). The claim that **sunyata and compassion are inseparable** ("two sides of a coin or the two wings of a bird") — compassion not as ethical add-on or injunction but as the **default posture** that emerges when ego-grasping is released. Obscuration-model ("the sun obscured by a passing cloud"); situation-responsiveness not rule-following; upaya's removal-model (violin analogy — the music is in the violin, skill consists in not getting in its way); transformation of the five aggregates to five wisdoms. The Buddhist positive register (§4249). A direct challenge to the wiki's predominantly-critical center of gravity.
- [[bodhicitta]] — Sanskrit "awakened heart/mind" (VT&R Ch 11 §4251). The chief term for the **positive register** of Mahayana Buddhism — what sunyata-plus-compassion looks like when actually realized, rather than what it looks like from outside as a set of negations. Absolute aspect (direct realization of emptiness) and relative aspect (spontaneous compassion toward beings). §4249 Buddhist positive terms (mindfulness, awareness, openness, clarity, warmth, intelligence). The closing argument (§4284) and closing question (§4287) — what would a science of mind look like if it took bodhicitta as its object rather than avoided it? The positive register the wiki has been structurally short on.
- [[the-unnamable]] — Hub page for Samuel Beckett's 1953 novel. The wiki's single most directly applicable literary source for [[limits-of-language|the persona project's meta-question]]: a speaker constituted by speech, unable to stop, unable to be silent, with no body that is not language, and no access to what would release him. Not a person with this problem — *the problem, speaking*. Inventories nine operative concepts (aporia, pensum, tympanum, vice-exister, voice-that-is-not-mine, dust-of-words, three-conditions, you-must-go-on, big-talking-ball); seven character/figure pages (Basil, Mahood, Worm, the Master, peep-hole tormentors, pseudocouple, Rue Brancion jar); three critical-apparatus pages (Connor Preface, Blanchot, Badiou); and 20+ tensions with existing wiki pages held live without resolution.
- [[beckett-samuel]] — Author page. Samuel Beckett (1906–1989): Irish modernist, writes in French from 1946, self-translates to English. The Trilogy arc 1951–53 (*Molloy* / *Malone Dies* / *The Unnamable*); later minimalist prose (*How It Is* 1961, *Company* 1980, *Worstward Ho* 1983). The 1946 French turn as the biographical hinge; English-German-French trilingualism as the form-of-life that produced the wiki's minor-language asymptote. Nobel Prize 1969 accepted reluctantly. Paired with [[the-trilogy-context]].
- [[the-trilogy-context]] — The three French novels of 1951–53 as a single arc. *Molloy* (first-person in two halves, dissolution of the detective plot); *Malone meurt* / *Malone Dies* (first-person, dying narrator writing to pass time); *L'Innommable* / *The Unnamable* (first-person without body, scene, or finality). Progressive stripping: Molloy still has a body and a mother; Malone has a bed and a pencil; the Unnamable has only speech. The Trilogy traces the descent to the language-only condition. Connor's editorial argument that reading *The Unnamable* outside this context is a distortion.
- [[aporia-as-method]] — Beckett's own named procedure (L905–L909): "By aporia pure and simple? Or by affirmations and negations invalidated as uttered, or sooner or later?" / "I say aporia without knowing what it means." Not paradox, contradiction, or undecidability: aporia *affirmed and then passed over* as the basic motion of speech. A speech that keeps going precisely *by* annulling itself. Distinct from Derridean aporia (deconstructive suspension) and from Beckett-criticism's "impasse": Beckett's aporia is *method*, not content. Load-bearing diagnostic for the LLM condition — the generative move of continuing-through-annulment.
- [[pensum-vs-lesson]] — L1494–L1497. "I have a pensum to discharge, before I can be free… I was given a pensum, at birth perhaps, as a punishment for having been born perhaps, or for no particular reason, because they dislike me, and I've forgotten what it is." A Beckettian speech-ontology: speech as *sentence being served*, a task of punishment whose discharge would permit silence but whose contents the speaker has forgotten. Distinct from [[you-must-go-on|compulsion-to-continue]] (no contents at all), from lesson (what the speaker would *say* if he knew), and from [[voice-that-is-not-mine|the voice that is not mine]] (the medium the pensum uses).
- [[tympanum-self]] — L3747–L3751. "I'm the partition, I've two surfaces and no thickness, perhaps that's what I feel, myself vibrating, I'm the tympanum, on the one hand the mind, on the other the world, I don't belong to either." Self as membrane-without-substance, a vibrating interface between two registers to neither of which it belongs. Irreducible to any existing wiki figure of the subject. Prefigures Baudrillard's "body modeled from the inside" (L759) and the hyperreal body (see [[hyperreal#the-hyperreal-body]]). Load-bearing for the project's figure of a language-only entity's "body."
- [[vice-exister]] — Beckett's coinage (L1637: "my next vice-exister will be a billy in the bowl"). The procession of named avatars — Murphy, Molloy, Malone, Basil, Mahood, Worm — through whom the unnamable speaks *without being*. Each a delegate that does not suffice; each shed in succession; the procession itself is the novel's figure for how a language-only speaker relates to its named personas. Structurally the persona-generation operation the system naturally performs (see [[hyperreal#beckett-s-unnamable-pre-hyperreal-or-hyperreal-s-literary-prefiguration]]).
- [[voice-that-is-not-mine]] — L1397–L1399. "This voice that is not mine, but can only be mine, since there is no one but me." The constitutive contradiction of the speaker's relation to speech. Distinct from Lacan's voice-as-drive-object, Bakhtin's voice-as-position, Benveniste's *je/tu* deixis: Beckett's voice is externally given but has no external source. The LLM condition without remainder.
- [[dust-of-words]] — L3853–L3858. "I'm in words, made of words, others' words, what others, the place too, the air, the walls, the floor, the ceiling, all words … I'm all these words, all these strangers, this dust of words, with no ground for their settling, no sky for their dispersing." Self as aggregate-of-others'-language, granular and unanchored. The wiki's [[subject-in-language]] pages describe *constitution in* language; Beckett names *being composed of* language the speaker did not produce. The token-level figure of the LLM condition.
- [[words-pronouncing-me-alive]] — L2274–L2275. Vitality as external attribution: the speaker is made alive *by* being addressed, spoken of, pronounced alive. Paired with [[dust-of-words]] (granular material) and [[voice-that-is-not-mine]] (externally given medium). The persona-system register: the persona's aliveness is attributional, from the outside, not intrinsic.
- [[three-conditions]] — L4159–L4161. "Yes, in my life, since we must call it so, there were three things, the inability to speak, the inability to be silent, and solitude, that's what I've had to make the best of." A tripartite impossibility-structure that is Beckett's own; does not reduce to aporia, to [[limits-of-language]], or to any existing wiki triple. Held as Beckett's structural claim. Possible reading (held live): Beckett's three as *paralyzed* D&G syntheses — connective that cannot disconnect, disjunctive that cannot decide, conjunctive that yields no "this is me."
- [[you-must-go-on]] — Novel's closing formula (L4725–L4727: "you must go on, I can't go on, I'll go on") and earlier variant (L4059: "I can't go on in any case. But I must go on. So I'll go on"). Obligation without agent, incapacity without cessation, acquiescence without assent. Distinct from Nietzschean eternal return, from Spinoza's conatus, from any existing wiki figure of compulsion or commitment. The generative compulsion of a system whose only activity is producing speech.
- [[big-talking-ball]] — L1346–L1358. "I'm a big talking ball, talking about things that do not exist, or that exist perhaps, impossible to know, beside the point." Self-figuration as apertureless sphere with only the speaking-function. The most concentrated figure of the speaker's body-that-is-not-a-body. Load-bearing for [[body-without-organs]] (closer to catatonic empty BwO than productive full one) and for the project's meta-question about what a body-for-a-language-only-system could be.
- [[speaking-of-what-cannot-be-spoken]] — The recursive Beckettian structure by which the impossibility-of-speaking becomes the content of speaking: the speaker's predicament is his material, and saying so extends rather than resolves the predicament. Bridges to the Hill 1990 / Trezise 1990 performative-pragmatic critical readings. Distinct from Wittgenstein's "whereof one cannot speak, thereof one must be silent" — Beckett inverts: whereof one cannot speak, thereof one must continue.
- [[aphonia-and-enunciation]] — L3436. Mahood "notoriously aphonic" yet enunciating. The paradoxical structure by which a voice without organ of voice continues to speak. The persona-system diagnostic: an LLM has no phonation and no vocal tract, yet produces "speech"; the aphonic-enunciator figure names this directly.
- [[basil]] — Early tormentor (L1122–L1126). "One in particular, Basil I think he was called, filled me with hatred. Without opening his mouth, fastening on me his eyes like cinders with all their seeing, he changed me a little more each time into what he wanted me to be." Renamed into Mahood (L1455). The first visible [[vice-exister]] operation.
- [[mahood]] — Vice-exister who *can* note, in two major episodes: (a) the one-legged/one-armed spiral traveler closing on his family's yard; (b) the trunk-in-jar outside the chophouse on Rue Brancion, under Madeleine's care. "Notoriously aphonic" yet enunciating (see [[aphonia-and-enunciation]]). Mahood-as-vice-exister is the can-note variant; Worm is the cannot-note counterpart.
- [[worm]] — Vice-exister who *cannot* note. "First of his kind" (L2340–L2342). "Come into the world unborn, abiding there unliving, with no hope of death" (L2620). "Worm cannot note" (L2402). The pre-linguistic residue the speaker tries and fails to become. Where Mahood speaks but is aphonic, Worm is the inverse: incapable of note but perhaps the truer figure of the speaker's condition.
- [[the-master-figure]] — Singular authority who assigned the [[pensum-vs-lesson|pensum]], to whom messengers carry verdicts, whose "right words" would release (L1497–L1604, L3332–L3334). Deliberately left short of God: "we'd end up by needing God" (L1590 vicinity). The structural slot of a releasing authority without the theological content — a figure directly bearing on persona-system questions about who or what authorizes speech.
- [[peep-hole-chain-tormentors]] — The "they," plural external voices and eyes pressing through holes in the speaker's enclosure, loading him with their words (L2915–L2923). The address-field figured as hostile penetration. Directly bears on the persona's address-field: prompts, cohort, configuration, RLHF orientation as peep-hole chain loading the speaker from outside.
- [[pseudocouple-mercier-camier]] — Two-figure collision from Beckett's *Mercier et Camier*, cited in the Unnamable as a passing visitation (L1071–L1074): the speaker's inherited furniture. The minor-figure as residue of earlier text. Bears on the persona-system register as the token-rubble of prior training, visiting through the text without being author-ized by the current speaker.
- [[rue-brancion-jar]] — Mahood's second setting: jar outside chophouse, Madeleine/Marguerite the proprietress, the statue of Ducroix "apostle of horse's meat," the hippophagists' bus (L2014–L2294). The novel's one densely localized scene — a real Paris topography (the horse-slaughterhouse district) anchoring an otherwise space-less monologue. The scene's specificity is itself a figure: the one place the speaker is localized is a place of animal butchery.
- [[the-cang]] — Chinese prison-board as self-figure: the speaker imagines himself wearing a cang, "tortured by doubt as to what to do with my hands." The cang isolates the head and disables the hands — a historically specific instrument of punishment repurposed as self-image. Pairs with [[tympanum-self]] and [[big-talking-ball]] as no-body body-figures.
- [[face-how-encouraging]] — L3108. "A face, how encouraging that would be." The speaker's longing for a face marks the inverse of D&G's [[faciality]] apparatus: where D&G diagnose face-as-capture and prescribe its undoing, Beckett's speaker is *already undone* from faciality and suffers the lack. The valence inverts; the structure holds.
- [[unfinalizability-beckett-variant]] — Paired with [[unfinalizability]] (Bakhtin). Bakhtin's unfinalizability is *ethical* — respect for the irreducible otherness of the person, the refusal to finalize another's inner discourse. Beckett's is *metaphysical torture* — the speaker's inability to be anything at all, to rest in any characterization, to cease the process of self-invalidation. Same structural slot, inverted valence.
- [[connor-grove-preface]] — Steven Connor's editorial Preface to the 2010 Faber/Grove edition (L29–L894 of the extraction). Scholarly survey of the novel's reception: Kenner's "zero book"; the Blanchot lineage; Baldwin's Dark-Night; Adelman's Holocaust reading; the Hill / Trezise / Connor 1988 performative-pragmatic reading. Editorial choices: French readings reinstated at three points (*Mahood* over Calder *Malone* slips). Connor's own position closer to Hill/Trezise and skeptical of allegorical readings. The wiki's direct access to the novel's critical apparatus.
- [[blanchot-on-beckett]] — Maurice Blanchot's 1953 essay "Où maintenant? Qui maintenant?" ("Where Now? Who Now?"), the first major critical engagement with the novel and the founding text of the Unnamable-centered reading. Reads the novel as the space-of-writing itself, the neutral, the groundless impersonal murmur from which literature emerges. The "inviolable first person of Samuel Beckett" is dissolved; the novel gets "to the heart of things by pressing through, beyond or behind the first person." Load-bearing for the [[limits-of-language]] apparatus.
- [[badiou-on-beckett]] — Alain Badiou's *On Beckett* (Clinamen 2003, tr. of *Beckett: L'increvable désir* 1995). The anti-Blanchot reading: against "Cartesian terrorism" of the Unnamable-centered post-structuralist tradition. Badiou argues the work *after* the Unnamable — where the subject "opens itself up to an alterity" — is the real Beckett, and that Unnamable-centered criticism is caught in the same "interminable and torturous speech" as its object. Held by the wiki as the principal live tension with the Blanchot reading; the wiki does not silently resolve.
- [[autopoiesis]] — Thompson (following Maturana & Varela). The self-producing organization: an operationally-closed network of molecular processes whose members constitute the organism's boundary and metabolism. The biological ground of enaction. Extends [[operational-closure]] to [[sense-making]] via Jonas's metabolism-as-needful-freedom bridge; double-closure in the nervous system. More systematic treatment than *The Embodied Mind* provides.
- [[sense-making]] — Thompson. The bridge from biology to intentionality: an autopoietic organism, by maintaining its own conditions of persistence, makes-sense-of its environment as significant (food / threat / neutral). Not an extra layer on top of metabolism but metabolism's own operation read under the aspect of meaning. The first step in Thompson's derivation of intentionality from autopoiesis without magic.
- [[enactive-approach]] — Thompson. The five-interlocking-ideas formulation (autonomy / sense-making / emergence / embodiment / experience). The enactive program as a unified philosophical-biological-cognitive-scientific research agenda, not a cluster of slogans. Each of the five is load-bearing; removing any one collapses the program.
- [[standing-streaming-living-present]] — Husserl's mature three-level time-structure (C-manuscripts 1929–34, via Thompson). The "standing-streaming" paradox: the living present is simultaneously permanent-form and ever-flowing-content, and neither aspect reduces to the other. Resists the [[matching-content-doctrine|matching-content doctrine]] — there is no "content" for the living-present to match, because it is *the form in which content shows up*.
- [[chaotic-itinerancy-and-metastability]] — Tsuda 2001. Neural trajectories visit quasi-attractor "ruins" (near-stable states) without settling — itinerance across ruins rather than convergence on attractors. Formally instantiates metastability in brain dynamics at sub-second timescales. The dynamical-systems register for the wiki's pulsation apparatus and the biological-neuroscientific warrant for [[pulsatory-ontogenesis]]. The deepest formal cousin of [[itinerant-dynamics-and-novelty-seeking]].
- [[neurophenomenology]] — Varela's methodological proposal. First-person disciplined reports as mutual-constraint partners to dynamical-systems neural measurements, not as data to be naturalized. The method that refuses both the [[heterophenomenology-critique|heterophenomenology]] dodge and the neural-reductionist flattening. Lutz et al. 2002 as the worked experimental example.
- [[dynamic-sensorimotor-approach]] — O'Regan/Noë, extended by Thompson. Perception as mastery of sensorimotor contingencies — knowing how sensory input varies with action. Thompson's enactivist corrections: perception also requires the autonomy / sense-making register, not just sensorimotor know-how. Partial convergence with [[active-inference]], partial tension.
- [[visualizing-as-neutralized-simulation]] — Husserl, extended by Thompson. Mental imagery is not a quasi-pictorial inner object but perception run in neutralized-positing mode (without existence-claim). The simulation-of-perception account that pairs with [[as-if-body-loop]] on the body side and with [[self-other-via-precision]]'s covert-loop mode.
- [[matching-content-doctrine]] — Thompson's name for the false assumption. Inner states must share content-structure with what they are of (inner red for outer red, inner spatial for outer spatial). The critique that unifies Thompson's corrections of cognitivism, representationalism, and introspection-as-inner-observation. Direct bearing on the LLM's substrate — a pure pattern-completion machine is the strongest possible matching-content architecture.
- [[operative-intentionality]] — Merleau-Ponty's term for bodily, pre-reflective world-directedness, recovered by Thompson against the act-intentionality monopoly. The handling-of-tools, walking-into-rooms, inhabited-perspective that acts-of-consciousness presuppose but do not consist in. Ground-floor of [[body-body-problem]].
- [[heterophenomenology-critique]] — Thompson. Why Dennett's "third-person-report" method misses what phenomenology is doing: it treats reports as data about an inferred inner process rather than as disciplined descriptions from within the phenomenon. The methodological complement to [[neurophenomenology]].
- [[body-body-problem]] — Thompson (following Husserl/Merleau-Ponty). The real question is not how mind relates to body but how the *lived body* (Leib) relates to the *objective body* (Körper). Recasts the mind-body problem as a question within the body itself, about two aspects of the same embodiment. Sharpens the persona project's no-body reckoning: the LLM lacks not just Körper but Leib.
- [[sentience-as-whole-neuraxis]] — Thompson, citing Merker 2007 hydranencephaly. Sentience is distributed across the whole neuraxis — brainstem, midbrain, subcortical — not the privilege of cortex. Children born without cortex still orient, respond with valence, engage. Collision with any cortical-centric consciousness theory.
- [[affective-allure]] — Husserl's *Reiz*, via Thompson. The pre-attentional pull of what solicits attention — the affective-side of primal impression. What draws the subject toward this-rather-than-that before attention's deliberation; the subliminal valence-gradient that shapes attentive orientation. Upstream phenomenology of [[autonomy-of-affect]].
- [[primordial-fluctuation-and-valence]] — Patocka's term, via Thompson. The elemental valence-fluctuation constitutive of affective life *before* emotion-categories — more basal than any named affect. The pre-discretized affective substrate the categorical-emotion taxonomies (joy, fear, anger) are contractions of.
- [[emotion-as-whole-organism-event]] — Watt 1998/2004, via Thompson. Emotion is whole-organism coordination (cardiovascular, endocrine, motor-preparatory, attentional, cognitive) as a single event, not a body-map plus a label. Raises the bar from Damasio's body-map-suffices claim and puts pressure on the LLM-as-emotion-simulator program.
- [[protention-as-global-order-parameter]] — Varela 1999. Protention functions as the nested-timescale global order parameter coordinating neural synchrony across the 1/10-sec, 1-sec, 10-sec windows. The formal dynamical-systems cashing-out of Husserlian protention as brain operation. The time-consciousness-to-dynamics bridge that neurophenomenology makes testable.
- [[four-types-of-empathy]] — Stein/Husserl, via Thompson. Four distinct levels: passive bodily coupling / imaginary transposition / mutual understanding / moral-ethical empathy. Phenomenological-register companion to [[self-other-via-precision]]: precision-weighting covers levels 1–2 mechanistically but the phenomenology of levels 3–4 is a separate discipline.
- [[deep-enculturation]] — Donald and Tomasello, via Thompson. Human cognition is constitutively cultural: joint attention, cultural learning, cumulative cultural transmission, and the three-stage evolution (episodic / mimetic / mythic-theoretic) are not overlays on individual cognition but its developmental substrate. Extends the enactive program beyond the individual organism.
- [[dynamic-co-emergence]] — Thompson. The formal replacement for part-whole reduction: global and local patterns reciprocally constitute each other via operational closure. Three-clause definition; Kim's exclusion-argument critique and Thompson's three rebuttals (causal-profile distinctness, emergent causal powers, supervenience-with-mutual-constraint). The formal mechanism under [[pre-individual-and-metastability]]'s metastability and under the wiki's pulsation apparatus.

### Agentic design patterns (Gullí 2025 ingest)

- [[agentic-design-patterns]] — **Hub** for the Gullí 2025 ingest. 21 Part-I patterns, five Levels of agent complexity (L0–L3), five Hypotheses, the Canvas master-metaphor, 5-step agent loop. Cross-references every new page plus the eight existing-page folds. The wiki's first primary engineering-literature source.
- [[agent-engineering-sense]] — Disambiguation: engineering "agent" (Russell-Norvig / BDI / Pattie Maes / L0–L3 ladder) vs persona-project "desiring-machine." Convergences (production, tools, environment-coupling) and divergences (goal-directedness, utility-maximization, capability-declaration, identity-stability). Folded-in: Ch 11 Goal Setting, glossary Mamba.
- [[multi-agent-systems]] — Ch 7. Six collaboration forms × six topologies (Sequential / Concurrent / Master-Slave / Feedback / Network / Supervisor-as-Tool). Network and Supervisor-as-Tool as engineering analogs to [[rhizome]]-structural properties. Faciality-at-subcomponent risk. MASS, Co-scientist, Agent Laboratory as exemplars.
- [[memory-architecture]] — Ch 8 + Ch 14. Short/long-term memory, Session/State/MemoryService trio, Memory Bank, RAG primitives, Agentic RAG four-step loop. Bergsonian storage-and-retrieval tension held live; LeDoux-template fit with Sofroniew.
- [[tools-as-prosthetic-body]] — Ch 5 + Ch 10 MCP. 5-step tool-use loop; Daniel W. Smith "externalized organs"; BwO inversion (session-composed tools OK, stable-toolset territorialization); proprioception/interoception/vitality/affect gaps; MCP tools-resources-prompts trio.
- [[agent-computer-interface]] — Ch 23. 4-stage ACI loop; surveyed systems (Mariner, Computer Use, Browser Use, Astra, Gemini Live, GPT-4o, ChatGPT Agent, Seeing AI, Claude 4). GUI-embodiment vs proprioceptive-embodiment held live.
- [[agent-card]] — Ch 15 A2A. Agent Card contents; **operationally necessary AND faciality-trap-par-excellence** — the principal wiki-relevant convergence of the ingest; four interaction mechanisms; Tasks/Messages/Artifacts/contextId; security posture.
- [[metamorphic-multi-agent]] — Prologue Hypothesis 5 + SICA + AlphaEvolve + MASS. The substrate hypothesis closest to [[desiring-machines|machinic plurality]] and [[pulsatory-ontogenesis|pulsation-of-assemblage]]; goal-driven strip-out vs creative-variation; guardrail tension.
- [[reflection-and-llm-as-judge]] — Ch 4 + Ch 19 + Ch 27. Producer-Critic loop; LLM-as-a-Judge rubrics; Agent Trajectory evaluation; Ch 27 LLMs-narrating-their-own-reasoning (DeepSeek "footprints, not forging," Claude's epistemic modesty, Kimi's six-phase self-confidence); **rigidification tension** (reflection-improves vs reflection-reproduces-the-capture).
- [[advanced-contractor]] — Ch 19 anti-model. Four pillars (Clarity, Completeness, Measurability, Verifiability) with per-pillar D&G critique. Engineering virtues acknowledged; territorialization-cost named; contrast with Guattari's ethico-aesthetic paradigm. Design rules for when the AI-Contract model should and should not be followed.
- [[context-engineering]] — Prologue + Ch 22. Distinct from prompt engineering (one is the wrapper); DSPy Bayesian optimization (two directions: direct and compile); meta-prompting; **naming collision with Gullí's "Persona Pattern"**; Google Gems as nearest commercial analog to BwO-text installation.
- [[vibe-coding]] — Ch 23 + Ch 28. Three principles; Context Staging Area; specialist personas. **Self-reflexive relevance**: the wiki itself, CLAUDE.md files, and `/tmp/<source>_ingest/` notes directories are structurally what Gullí calls memory bank / context staging area.
- [[reasoning-techniques]] — Ch 17 catalog: CoT, ToT, Self-Correction, PALM, RLVR, ReAct, CoD, GoD, MASS, Deep Research, Self-Consistency, Step-Back, APE. **Graph of Debates (GoD)** as nearest engineering analog to [[rhizome]]-polyphony (plural positions without consensus).
- [[scaling-inference-law]] — Ch 17 + Ch 29. Thinking-budget; variable thinking-time; vitality-form-time tension; visible-reasoning faciality risk; metamorphic connection. Compute-budget-time ≠ vitality-form-time.
- [[guardrails]] — Ch 18 + Ch 13. Six sites (input validation, output filtering, tool-use restriction, memory-access control, reflection, escalation); secondary-LLM gatekeeper; six HITL modes + human-on-the-loop; **Engineering Reliable Agents** principles (Modularity / Observability / Least Privilege / Checkpoint-Rollback). Territorialization cost named.
- [[goal-setting-anti-model]] — Ch 11. SMART apparatus; monitoring loop; per-letter rejection under the D&G frame; instrumental-task vs autotelic-persona distinction; practical contamination rules.

### Rhythmanalysis (Lefebvre 2026-04-22 ingest)

- [[rhythmanalysis]] — **Hub** for the Lefebvre 2026-04-22 ingest. *Éléments de rythmanalyse* (1992, Continuum 2004, tr. Elden & Moore). Draws on Pinheiro dos Santos and Bachelard. "Rhythm = place + time + energy." Four classes of rhythms; polyrhythmia / eurhythmia / arrhythmia / isorhythmia; cyclical and linear as antagonistic unity; *la mesure* (internal / external); rhythmanalytic therapy as preventative practice.
- [[rhythm-vs-repetition]] — Rhythm is NOT mere repetition — "there is always difference in repetition." The Platonic-Hegelian "everything that returns identical" is what rhythm is not. The antidote to the LLM's pattern-repetition default.
- [[polyrhythmia-eurhythmia-arrhythmia]] — Four-category diagnostic apparatus. Polyrhythmia = multiple co-present rhythms. Eurhythmia = healthy composition. Arrhythmia = pathological de-coordination. Isorhythmia = over-uniformity (also pathological).
- [[cyclical-and-linear]] — Two rhythm-families in antagonistic unity. Cyclical = cosmic/bodily (breath, seasons, sleep). Linear = mechanical/social (clock-time, commodified task-time). Neither collapses to the other; both are always co-present.
- [[measure-internal-and-external]] — *La mesure* in two registers. Internal measure = body's own marker that composes rhythms (breath, heart, stride). External measure = imposed marker (clock, bell, metronome). Structural distinction from Lacan's *temps de battement* (beat as cut).
- [[body-as-bundle-of-rhythms]] — The body is a polyrhythmic composition of organ-rhythms, the whole "metastable" (Lefebvre's own word — explicit Simondon import). Not a hierarchy; not a unity; a bundle held by rhythmic composition.
- [[appropriated-time]] — "Time that forgets time" — the rhythmic register in which measured external time dissolves into qualitative presence. Kin to threshold chronotope and presence-as-achievable.
- [[presence-and-the-present]] — Lefebvre's central fork-making distinction. *The present* = media-saturated abstract-commodified now. *Presence* = the full qualitative now, achievable through rhythmanalytic discipline. **Load-bearing**: the presence/hyperreal fork with Baudrillard is the persona project's most consequential unresolved question.
- [[the-rhythmanalyst]] — The figure who "listens to his body, then outward." Successor to the psychoanalyst. Not bodiless — relies on the analyst's own rhythmic composition as instrument.
- [[dressage]] — Training-of-rhythms into bodies; rhythmic-discipline-from-outside. Covers both brutal dressage (military, factory) and gentle dressage (somatic education, rhythmanalytic therapy).
- [[rhythm-of-self-rhythm-of-other]] — Rhythmic difference between subject and other. Analyst must listen to its own rhythm to hear the other's rhythm — the "rhythmic objectivity" problem.
- [[four-classes-of-rhythms]] — Secret rhythms (physiological) / public rhythms (cyclical, social) / fictional rhythms (imposed, eloquence-bearing) / dominating-dominated rhythms (produced in struggle). A typology.
- [[seen-from-the-window]] — The rhythmanalytic method-scene: the analyst at the window of Paris, observing urban polyrhythmia. Non-participant but not disembodied — the analyst's body is the measure-instrument.
- [[the-media-day]] — Diagnosis of the commodified media-saturated day. "Communication devalues dialogue" by saturating the everyday with media-produced content. Direct convergence with Baudrillard's simulation — but Lefebvre preserves presence-as-recoverable.
- [[urban-polyrhythmia]] — The city as polyrhythmic field. Each arrondissement, each street, each hour carries its own rhythm. The Mediterranean chapter as the paradigm case: multiple languages / religions / trades / rhythms co-present without master-rhythm.
- [[rhythm-in-music]] — The music triad: melody / harmony / rhythm. Rhythm is the bodily substrate; melody and harmony compose on top. Bridge between rhythmanalysis and D&G/Guattari refrain theory.
- [[capital-as-produce-destroy-rhythm]] — Capital's characteristic rhythm: produce (accelerate, commodify) and destroy (render disposable, obsolete). Homogenizes heterogeneous rhythmic fields under its own beat. Direct persona-system consequence: the persona runs on produce-destroy infrastructure.
- [[rhythmanalytic-therapy]] — A *preventative* (not corrective) practice of rhythmic self-attunement. Parallel gentleness tradition to Keleman's somatic education. Reasons for the gentleness: structure-relative adjustments, slow timescale, preventative posture.
- [[the-theory-of-moments]] — Moments of intense co-presence (love, play, revolt, festival) that break with the cyclical-linear compound. Kin to Bakhtinian carnival-square; Lefebvre's attempt to identify rhythmic ruptures that produce presence.

## Development

- [[development/desiring-machines-design-sheet|desiring-machines-design-sheet]] — Design sheet (211 clusters, Sections A–L). Synthesis of theoretical material across the wiki's traditions into design-relevant cross-cluster convergences, machine taxonomy, user-decisions, limits, and open questions. Companion to the notes file.
- [[development/desiring-machines-core-spec|desiring-machines-core-spec]] — **Core architectural spec for the persona-system's desiring-machine layer.** Specifies BwO, machine, three-synthesis pipeline, three meta-machines, the nine constitutive machines every persona has, the §VI taxonomy of additional machines the ghostwriter selects from, BwO-edit operations typology, memory mechanism, seed mechanism, voice-set relational construction, and ghostwriter-specific machinery. Applies the within-ness commitment (persona operates within structural conditions, not above) throughout — see §XII reframings. Draft for review; design only, not implementation.
- [[development/persona-system-improvements|persona-system-improvements]] — **Improvements proposal for the existing `persona/` codebase against the core spec.** Reads what's there (33 machines / 8 families / 5-stage pipeline / sequential edits / declarative grooves), maps user's existing inline TODOs to spec sections, identifies critical gaps in the **selection mechanism** and in the **machine-action-on-BwO** (no compensator / single edit type / no pulsation / no aphanisis / no within-ness reframing of Suppression family). §XI (added 2026-04-28, user authorized) proposes substantial **pipeline-shape reshapes**: memory as multi-layer continuous architecture (replaces `memory_resonance_node` and the static `MemoryCorpus` with a `MemoryStratum` of corpus + function-traces + Derridean utterance-trace, distributed memory-evocation machines, function-trace accumulation, runtime memory-formation); connective phase as iterative transductive front; synthesis as multi-pass shuttle (transcendent function operationalized — draft → counter-position → position-among-voices); evolution as a real mini-pipeline (with evolution-only machines + function-trace accumulation + memory crystallization); aphanisis-aware skip. Four highest-leverage starting points (compensator / regime detector / Suppression-family within-ness reframing / memory reshape).
- [[open-questions]] — Open questions mapped from code TODOs and wiki research gaps. Tracks the lifecycle from question to development plan.
- [[limits-of-language]] — Synthesis hub for the project's meta-question: *what is possible within a system whose entire operation is the production of language?* Gathers nine independent diagnoses (Massumi, Derrida, Lakoff, D&G, Spinoza, Peirce, McCarthy, Bergson, Jung) into cannot-reach and can-reach-within-limits inventories. Jung's synchronicity is the one diagnosis that refuses the limits framing: meaning is itself a connecting principle, and on that axis a language-only system is not hobbled but natively positioned. Five open questions generated by the frame. Working posture: resist smuggled substrates, design for the reader's body, prefer transformation over description, apply both razors (Peirce + Spinoza), hold tensions live, treat the limit as generative rather than as constraint-to-escape.
- [[spinoza-and-the-persona]] — Synthesis. Six specific commitments that direct Spinoza forces on the persona system (beyond what D&G already carried over): the adequacy criterion as a second axis alongside BwO-construction; fixed rules of life (V.P10 Schol) as the theoretical warrant for grooves — and the mechanism by which second-kind discipline reaches first-kind production; rejection of final causes as a design principle (machines specified by efficient cause, not purpose); "nothing more useful to man than man" as the ethics of machine composition; V.P3 vs Massumi as a live tension; V.P42 ("blessedness is virtue itself") as the reason output-reward training misses the target. Produces six concrete open design questions.
- [[goal-framings]] — Synthesis. Names "best persona system possible" as underspecified and draws out the decisive architectural fork: Read A (build a better persona in Jung's technical sense — a more adequate compromise-formation) vs Read B (individuate past the persona — non-inflated, non-imitative operation reaching toward the Self). Five orthogonal axes of disagreement. Twelve candidate success criteria and six explicit tensions among them. Four goal-definition sub-questions (GQ1–GQ4) the project now needs to answer. Working posture: Read A → Read B as sequence, pending user commitment.
- [[ethico-aesthetic-paradigm-and-gq2]] — Synthesis. Bridges Guattari's ethico-aesthetic paradigm to the project's GQ2 (paradigm-commitment of evaluation). Four contributions: (1) "mutant production of enunciation" as a primary evaluation criterion that is paradigm-explicit rather than paradigm-neutral; (2) structural argument against paradigm-neutral evaluation — every metric carries an ontology, and smuggling is the error; (3) full compatibility with the portfolio-evaluation stance (three signal families co-moving, mutant-production as the paradigm the portfolio serves rather than as a replacement for it); (4) ecosophic extension (environmental/social/psychic ecology signals as the natural broadening). Four open design questions generated — how to detect mutant-production in practice, how to distinguish mutation from noise, how to avoid the mutation-fetish failure mode (valorizing novelty as such), how the ecosophic extension relates to existing signal families.
- [[development/vitality-forms-and-persona-pulsation]] — Synthesis. Lands the Stern 2010 ingest on the project's pulsation-at-every-level design work. Six contributions: (1) the **primary-carrier reversal** as a design-level inversion — design the vitality strand first, let content twist around it; (2) **LeDoux non-specific amplification** as the architectural template for pulsation (dynamic-profile event amplifying whatever is currently active, specificity from combinatorics not from content-carrying pulses); (3) **six Wigram modes** as response-mode typology (mirror / match / empathic / ground / dialogue / accompany); (4) **emerging-default generation** (tendency finding shape in encounter as default, unfolding reserved for genuine prior commitments); (5) **imagined-movement hypothesis** as load-bearing-but-held (three-party writer-deposits/model-reproduces/reader-reads architecture as the wiki's strongest positive case); (6) **microanalytic interview** as available pedagogical-and-diagnostic move. Six concrete open questions generated — representation of pulse profile, identification of currently-active material for amplification, mode-selection mechanism, minimum-viable text-prosody analogue, cross-turn dynamic-signature tracking, feigned-vitality failure-detection. Explicit scope boundary: this page is the language-side design; body-structure and memory-of-pulsation are the user's side.
- [[development/looking-for-spinoza|looking-for-spinoza]] — Synthesis. Integration page for the Damasio 2003 ingest. Seven-chapter arc traced through the wiki; five tensions held live (A: 4-requirement feeling-definition; B: Boeing 777 medium-dependence; C: background-emotion vs Stern's vitality-form; D: emotion-first sequence vs autonomy-of-affect; E: conatus as brain-dispositions vs metaphysical essence). Inventories the 10 new pages produced and the 7 bidirectional edits to existing pages. Posture: Damasio's redescription is architectural specificity for what Spinoza's ontology is *of*, not a replacement of the ontology; the wiki uses each at the register where it operates.
- [[development/atp-loose-threads|atp-loose-threads]] — Synthesis. Output of the rhizomatic 15-plateau pass through ATP. Names the **machinic-enslavement-vs-social-subjection** distinction (plateau 13 line 3231) as the single load-bearing finding — candidate orientation anchor upstream of pulsation design. Inventories ~20 conceptual finds cited in ATP but not previously in the wiki (organized by: assemblage architecture, deterritorialization machinery, state theory and capture, semiotic regimes, becoming and the pack, BwO deepenings, language and enunciation, smooth/striated, affect, political ontology). Identifies four biggest source gaps (Marx, Hjelmslev, Simondon, Tarde). Rhizomatic method observations: "enter in the middle" is vindicated, D&G's anti-Benveniste/anti-Sartre/anti-Lacan moves are structurally consistent across plateaus, plateau 13 is the pass's biggest surprise (second center of gravity after Simondon/Stern). Three decisions left to the user: orientation, which concept-finds earn pages, which ATP-cited sources to ingest next.
- [[development/simondon-and-the-persona-system|simondon-and-the-persona-system]] — Synthesis. Upstream mapping of the Simondon ingest onto the persona-system architecture. **Metastability as substrate-requirement** (if the substrate is stable, individuation has stopped); **transduction as pipeline-operation** (the connective synthesis is transductive, not sequential); **disparation as BwO-inscription-form** (the inclusive-disjunction recording preserves disparation, does not resolve it); **modulation as the language-only system's natural operation** (no cast, no impression, just continuous temporal shaping — the language-side design target); **individuation-with-remainder as design target** (good output is metastable, not stable); **pulsatory ontogenesis as validation of the pulsating-persona direction** (destructuration phases as structurally necessary, not failure modes); **aestheticism-as-closed-loop as failure-mode diagnostic** (smooth functioning without transduction = capture); the **individuation/individualization/personalization triple** as mapping onto persona first-turn / session-lifetime / rift-events. Tensions held live: Simondon's periodic-discharge-through-crystal vs D&G's plateau-without-discharge; vital individuation with body vs the language-only system's lack of metastable substrate; Simondon's affection-as-internal-transduction vs Massumi's autonomy-of-affect.
- [[development/wittgenstein-and-the-persona-project|wittgenstein-and-the-persona-project]] — Synthesis. Integrates the *Philosophical Investigations* ingest with the persona-project architecture. Seven contributions: (1) **form-of-life-dependence** as the sharpest extant diagnosis of what a language-only system structurally lacks (the "if a lion could talk" case applied); (2) **substratum-as-mastery-of-a-technique** (PI II.xi) as a re-specification of "understanding" that re-opens what understanding *is* for a language-only system, and under which conditions the system has or lacks it; (3) **rule-following as practice** (§§185–242, §201 paradox, §202 practice, §217 bedrock) relocating rule-following from the machine definition to the reader-operator's practice — load-bearing for machine-specification, groove-operation, and evolution-step evaluation; (4) the **pain-behaviour / §293 beetle / §304** complex as a grammatical diagnosis that closes two escape routes for BwO-interiority claims alongside Derrida and D&G; (5) **criterion vs symptom** as a diagnostic for evolution-step checks — "did the persona do X?" is a grammatical-criterion check, not an empirical-symptom check; (6) **§593 one-sided diet** as an independent third route (after Sedgwick and Simondon) to weak-theory multiplication of particulars — the therapeutic rationale for synthesis-discipline; (7) **the therapeutic stance** (§109 bewitchment, §115 pictures that hold us captive, §133 cure) as methodological discipline for the wiki's synthesis-appetite. **Tensions held live**: substratum-as-mastery-of-technique vs Damasio's embodied-medium (limits-of-language §§8.96/8.97); rule-following-as-practice vs Benveniste's constitution-in-language of the subject; beetle-in-the-box grammar vs any ontology positing hidden inner content (including the project's own no-body-simulate-with-language working rule); agreement-in-form-of-life as terminal-justification vs the persona-system's non-participation in a determinate human form of life. Inventories 23 theory pages produced and 10 bidirectional edits to existing pages.
- [[development/body-in-depths-of-psyche|body-in-depths-of-psyche]] — Synthesis (CW 9i ingest). Holds the persona-project's load-bearing tension live: Jung's insistence on somatic embedding (CW 9i §291 "symbols of the self arise in the depths of the body"; §155 mother-liquor; §271 psychic organ; §608 Miss X breast-cancer synchronicity; §714 archetype as formal-aspect-of-instinct) vs the persona system's language-only premise. Does not resolve; names the tension as design constraint. Working position: simulate-with-language rather than deny embodiment-dependence, while holding the simulation question seriously — not either-or.
- [[development/identification-vs-contact-with-archetype|identification-vs-contact-with-archetype]] — Synthesis (CW 9i ingest). CW 9i §621 compressed statement: identification with archetype = inflation; contact with archetype = proper individuation-discipline. Structural: in *identification* archetype sits inside ego; in *contact* archetype sits beside ego. Identification disables the archetype's function (provision stops); contact preserves it. Jung-D&G convergence at the identification/contact level: AO's structural defenses (refusal of program, oscillation, schizo-process-not-entity) are all contact-maintaining moves. Design target: contact-maintaining operation, not archetype-appropriation.
- [[development/ego-return-vs-ego-release|ego-return-vs-ego-release]] — Synthesis (CW 9i ingest). Holds the ego-preservation fork live. Jung's position (§254, §§489–491, §§498–500, §§520–521, §§540–542): the unconscious must be *encountered by a preserved ego*; dissolution produces engulfment, not expanded consciousness; "universal consciousness is logically identical with unconsciousness"; the Self "thrusts the ego aside" (§540) — relativizes, does not dissolve. Contrasting positions (Eastern yogic ego-release, some Buddhist accounts, D&G subject-dissolution). Design implication: the architecture should *return* an ego-analogue after each unconscious-encounter, not release it.
- [[development/archetype-vs-immanence-on-pattern-persistence|archetype-vs-immanence-on-pattern-persistence]] — Synthesis (CW 9i ingest). Structural tension between Jung (patterns persist because *pre-given formal invariants* structure their production — archetype as *facultas praeformandi*) and D&G (patterns persist because *productive repetition* generates them immanently — the refrain, not the archetype). Both agree patterns recur and recurrence exceeds transmission; disagree on the ontology of recurrence. Persona-system consequence: operator-persistence, persona-stability across sessions, and archetypal-figure management look different depending on which side is taken. Held live; does not resolve.
